# P4: Go's hands: travel and tasks Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give Go two hands: ixigo search (real or fake, behind one interface) as quick tools in the composer, and big jobs on the trip's own Vestra Bash through `/v1`, shown live on a working card, with Watch, hand-offs answered from the chat and results back as an options card.

**Architecture:** `travel/` is one `Travel` interface over two backends (an MCP client to ixigo, and a deterministic fake ixigo that is also an MCP server of its own), with a per-query cache, constraints applied in code, Go's image cache and the options cards. `tasks/` drives `/v1` through Go's own client (a key pool, idempotency keys, one live event stream per trip conversation): it sets up the trip's computer, starts a job with `hand_off: always`, follows its to-do list and screen, takes webhooks, and runs a sweep that makes `/v1` the source of truth. `mcp/` is Go's MCP server for the trip's Bash, locked to one trip by a per-task token. Two small Bash PRs keep the trip's computer lean.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22, TypeScript strict (`exactOptionalPropertyTypes`), zod 4.3.6, Fastify 5, `pg` 8, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.29.0 (pinned exactly), `@go/contract` 1.0.0, vitest 4, Vestra `/v1` (staging `https://api-staging.vestra.ai`). In `vestra-ai/bash`: the same stack plus its own `pnpm` workspace and `git wt`.

**Spec:** `docs/spec/2026-10-05-go-design.md` §10, §11 (the sandbox's window into memory), §12, §16 and `docs/plans/2026-10-05-go-the-week.md` (master plan; sections 6 and 7 are binding)

**Lane / gate:** Lane C. Gate **G3 "Go does real work", Wed 23:00**: ixigo flights and stays search in the composer (real or fake behind one interface; trains and buses are the stretch Task 8b, built after G3); a task on the trip's Bash through `/v1` with a ticking working card, a Watch link, a hand-off answered from the chat, the result as an options card; the two Bash PRs merged to staging. Demo beats served (master §1): **0:15** (a flights card from ixigo in about 2 s, filtered by Arjun's "not before 6 pm"), **1:00** (the villa job: "On it" plus a ticking working card and a live Watch screen while Go keeps answering), **2:00** (three villas as an options card, "Put it to a vote").

**Depends on:** P1: `Brain` (with `onStop(fn)`; `built.stop()` runs the stoppers in reverse order), `RegisterLane`, `buildBrain`, `Built`, `Timings` (`app.ts`), `registerRoom` (`room.ts`), `LANES` (`main.ts`); `ServerLink` (`door/server-link.ts`); `TO_SERVER_SCHEMAS` types `ToServerMethod`, `ToServerParams`, `ToServerResult`, `Trip`, `Message`, `CardTapParams`, `CardTapResult` (`door/methods.ts`); `TapRouter` (`door/taps.ts`); `Desk`, `DeskItem`, `DeskKind` (`desk/desk.ts`); `QuickTool`, `TurnCtx` with `cards`, `stableId(prefix)` and `signal`, `Tools` (`composer/tools.ts`); `NowProvider`, `Context` (`composer/context.ts`); `TripPack`, `Packs` (`trips/pack.ts`); `Setup` (`trips/setup.ts`); `Switches`, `SwitchName` (`admin/switches.ts`); `withSchema()` returning `{ pool, schema, drop }` (`db/test-db.ts`); `loadEnv` (`env.ts`); the seam files `travel/travel.ts` and `tasks/tasks.ts` and the env objects `TravelEnv` (`travel/env.ts`) and `TasksEnv` (`tasks/env.ts`) that P1 creates and P4 takes over (adding only, never a second file, never an edit of `env.ts`, which already merges them); `apps/brain/.env.example` (P1 Task 17), whose `env-example.test.ts` checks every key of `Env`; `fakeAnthropic`, `Reply` (`__tests__/fixtures/fake-anthropic.ts`); `SECRET`, `TEST_TIMINGS`, `rightNow`, `newSince`, `afterTools` (`__tests__/e2e/harness.ts`); the Postgres service in CI. P0 (`@go/sim`): `FakeServer` (`{ goUrl, secret, instance, now }`, `start`, `stop`, `createTrip`, `say`, `tap(cardId, actionId, personId)`, `waitFor(check, timeoutMs)`, `messages(tripId)`) and `goaFreshTrip()`. P2: `Memory` (`brain.memory`, read through `Brain['memory']`), optional at runtime. Keshav: the `/v1` org, keys, flags and the two Bash PR approvals (master §4).

## Global Constraints

Everything in master §7 applies. Specific to this plan:

- `/v1` limits per key: 60 requests a minute, 8 in flight, 20 open event streams; 10 computers waking per org (Go keeps to 8); $0.05 left in the wallet for a turn.
- At most 3 jobs run per trip at once; the 4th waits as `queued`. Every job has a deadline of 8 minutes of work and the deployment's `limits.spend_per_task_usd` of 2.
- A working card is replaced at most once every 6 seconds; state changes (needs you, done, didn't work, stopped) go out at once.
- The trip's screen is read at most every 7 seconds while a job runs, and re-hosted only when its hash changes.
- A question or hand-off nobody answers is settled after 10 minutes (a question is cancelled, a hand-off ends the job as "Didn't work").
- A Watch link is never posted in the chat; every tap mints a new 15-minute session. `screen: 'control'` is asked only for the person a hand-off waits on, and falls back to `view`.
- The trip's computer gets reads and `note` from Go's MCP server, nothing else: no card, no booking or paying tool, no budget answers, no contact details.
- Fake ixigo data is sold as `ixigo (simulated)`; images on cards are always Go URLs (`${GO_PUBLIC_URL}/img/...`).
- Prices reach the model only from a stored check, with its age ("checked just now", "checked 4 min ago").
- `/v1` user ids are `gotrip-<32 hex of sha256(trip_id)>` (the `UserRef` pattern allows no underscore).
- Every write a quick tool makes takes its `request_id` from `ctx.stableId('go_<tag>')` and every new card id from `ctx.stableId('c_<kind>')`; a card made in a turn goes into `ctx.cards` and rides with the turn's message. Writes outside a turn (the card updater, taps, the sweep) take ids made from their trigger, such as `go_task_<task_id>_card`. Desk item ids carry the lane's prefix: `task:<task_id>:<event>`, `travel:<what>:<tap_id>`.
- Bash PRs: each in its own worktree from `git wt new <name>` (fresh `origin/staging`), PR to `staging`, merged only after Keshav's OK, with `--squash --admin --match-head-commit <sha>`.
- Every timer the lane keeps is `unref()`ed, and every timer, stream and socket that outlives a call is stopped through `brain.onStop` (the follower's streams and waits, the sweep, the card updater's timers, the ixigo MCP connection). The setup steps keep no timer of their own: P1's setup pass retries them.
- Every variable P4 adds to `TravelEnv` or `TasksEnv` is added to `apps/brain/.env.example` in the same task, commented out and never with a secret value.
- Flights and stays first (master §9 "Stretch"). Trains and buses are Task 8b, built after G3. Until it lands, the types still name all four kinds (master §6.3's `Travel.search`), but no fake catalog, mapping, quick tool or MCP tool offers trains or buses, and `Travel.search('trains' | 'buses')` answers `TravelError('refused', 'Go cannot search trains yet.')` (or buses), which a search tool turns into a plain "can't" for the model. Every list of search tools in this plan works without them.
- Booking flights for the whole group is cut this week (master §9 "Cut now"): flights are searched, shown on an options card with the per-person fare, and put to a vote; the demo books the stay. Nothing in P4 offers to book flights for the group; `Travel.hold` takes any offer only because P5 decides what it books.

## Review Focus

1. **A job outlives its moment (master §10 item 4).** The group moved on, the job finished after a "stop", or a hand-off waits on someone who left: the card always tells the truth, a stopped job never posts results, and a hand-off nobody answers expires into "Didn't work". Tests: Task 16 (completed after stop), Task 18 (hand-off expiry), Task 20 (end to end).
2. **A lost webhook or a dropped stream.** A webhook that never comes, a stream cut mid-job, a resume point that is too old (410): nothing is stranded and nothing is applied twice. Tests: Task 16 (duplicate webhook), Task 17 (drop and resume, 410, poll fallback), Task 18 (sweep reads `/v1`), Task 20.
3. **Constraints the model passes are applied in code.** "Arjun can't leave before 6" must drop the 17:50 Akasa flight even if the model lists it; prices come only from the stored check with its age, a job's picks included (an offer id Go never showed this trip is never trusted, and a pick without an offer cannot be booked). Tests: Task 6 (filters), Task 7 (`search_flights` with `depart_after`), Task 14 (`resolvePicks`), Task 16 (the job's options card).
4. **The trip's computer reaches only its own trip.** A wrong, missing or revoked token gets 401; tokens end when the trip's last job ends; the MCP answers carry no budget, money or invite link. Tests: Task 11 (tokens), Task 13 (MCP server).
5. **Double taps and retries.** Two "Put it to a vote" taps open one vote; a re-run turn posts one card and starts one job; a redelivered webhook is applied once; the same hold confirmed twice books once. Tests: Task 4 (confirm twice, after a restart), Task 7, Task 8, Task 15, Task 16.

**Go (`vestra-ai/go-brain`, `apps/brain`)**

- `package.json` (modify): `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.29.0, `tsx` for scripts, scripts `fake-ixigo`, `setup-v1`, `ixigo-probe`.
- `.env.example` (modify P1's file): the travel variables (Task 8) and the `/v1` variables, with `VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY` and `VESTRA_MCP_SERVER_ID` (Task 11), each with a line on what it is for.
- `src/main.ts` (modify): `LANES` becomes `[registerRoom, registerTravel, registerTasks]`.
- `src/db/migrations/3000_travel.sql`: image cache, result sets, options cards, offers.
- `src/db/migrations/3001_computer.sql`: the trip's `/v1` user and conversation, trip tokens.
- `src/db/migrations/3002_mcp.sql`: the log of every MCP call.
- `src/db/migrations/3003_tasks.sql`: tasks, their interactions, task starts seen.
- `src/db/migrations/3004_task_events.sql`: webhook dedupe, the events cursor, stream resume points.
- `src/travel/env.ts` (modify P1's file): `TravelEnv` keeps its name and fields; `readTravelEnv` is added.
- `src/travel/travel.ts` (modify P1's seam): defines `TravelQuery`, `TravelOption`, `StatusQuery`, `StatusResult`; keeps master's `Travel.offer()` signature and `Hold.refundableUntil`; adds `TravelBackend`, `StoredOffer`, `OfferRef`, `Travel.simulate`.
- `src/travel/ids.ts`: random and stable ids.
- `src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.ts`: the fake catalog (flights and stays; Task 8b adds trains and buses), status, holds and bookings, one tool table.
- `src/travel/fake-ixigo/server.ts`: the fake as an MCP server on Fastify.
- `src/travel/fake-ixigo/main.ts`: the fake as its own process, for the demo.
- `src/travel/mcp-caller.ts`: calling an MCP server over HTTP (ixigo) or in process (the fake).
- `src/travel/ixigo-map.ts`: the mapping table from ixigo's tools to Go's types.
- `src/travel/mcp-travel.ts`: `createFakeTravel`, `createIxigoTravel`, `TravelError`.
- `src/travel/images.ts`: Go's image cache and `GET /img/:file`.
- `src/travel/results.ts`: result sets, options cards and offers in Postgres; `findOffer` (an offer id in the trip's saved searches).
- `src/travel/cards.ts`: the options card, prices, clocks, no dashes.
- `src/travel/filters.ts`: constraints applied in code.
- `src/travel/layer.ts`: `createTravelLayer` (`brain.travel`): switch, cache, routing, `offer()`.
- `src/travel/tools.ts`: `search_flights`, `search_stays`, `show_options`, `put_to_vote` (Task 8b, a stretch, adds `search_trains` and `search_buses`).
- `src/travel/votes.ts`: opening a vote from an options card, once.
- `src/travel/taps.ts`: `options/pick` and `options/vote`, and the tap wake line.
- `src/travel/register.ts`: `registerTravel`.
- `src/travel/ixigo-probe.ts` and `scripts/ixigo-probe.ts`: the day-one look at ixigo's real tools.
- `src/tasks/env.ts` (modify P1's file): `TasksEnv` keeps its name and fields and gains `VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY`, `VESTRA_MCP_SERVER_ID`; `readTasksEnv` is added.
- `src/tasks/testing/goa.ts`, `src/tasks/testing/lane-brain.ts`, `src/tasks/testing/fake-v1.ts`, `src/tasks/testing/harness.ts`, `src/tasks/testing/job-search.ts`: lane C's test kit, a fake `/v1`, the tasks wired for tests, and a job's search through Go's MCP server (also used by P7's demo kit).
- `src/tasks/sse.ts`: server-sent event frames.
- `src/tasks/v1-client.ts`: Go's `/v1` client with the key pool.
- `src/tasks/computer.ts`: the trip's computer and the setup steps `computer.user`, `computer.warm`.
- `src/tasks/instructions.ts`: the `go-trip-task` deployment's instructions, deny list and webhook events.
- `src/tasks/setup-v1.ts` and `scripts/setup-v1.ts`: one-time org setup.
- `src/tasks/store.ts`: task rows, interactions, task starts.
- `src/tasks/returns.ts`: the shapes a job returns (every pick with `offer_id` and `total_paise`), reading them back, and `resolvePicks` (offer ids to the offers Go's search showed; the rest found on the web).
- `src/tasks/working-card.ts`: the working card.
- `src/tasks/card-updater.ts`: `replace_card`, throttled per card.
- `src/tasks/tasks.ts` (modify P1's seam): `Tasks` (start, message, stop, running, plus handoff), `TaskBrief`, the queue, dispatch, deadlines.
- `src/tasks/event-store.ts`: webhook dedupe, the events cursor, stream resume points.
- `src/tasks/events.ts`: one place that applies a `/v1` event, from any source.
- `src/tasks/webhooks.ts`: `POST /v1-webhooks`.
- `src/tasks/screens.ts`: screenshots and the trace fallback.
- `src/tasks/follower.ts`: one live stream per trip conversation.
- `src/tasks/sweep.ts`: the two-minute sweep.
- `src/tasks/tools.ts`: `start_task`, `message_task`, `stop_task`, `settle_handoff`.
- `src/tasks/taps.ts`: `working/watch`, `working/stop`.
- `src/tasks/context.ts`: the `tasks` block of "right now" and the `task` wake lines.
- `src/tasks/register.ts`: `createTasksLane` and `registerTasks`.
- `src/mcp/tokens.ts`: trip tokens, minted and stored hashed.
- `src/mcp/server.ts`: Go's MCP tools (each search kept as the trip's result set).
- `src/mcp/routes.ts`: `POST /mcp`.
- Tests beside each file (`*.test.ts`), plus `src/tasks/e2e.test.ts` (the G3 run on P1's brain and P0's sim).

**Bash (`vestra-ai/bash`)**

- PR 1 `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/dispatch-platform.ts` (modify) and its test: deny `mcp__composio__*` when `apps.allow` is empty.
- PR 2 `packages/shared/src/platform/deployment.ts`, `packages/shared/src/platform/task-context.ts`, `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/task-platform.ts`, `apps/sandbox/src/harness/deny-tools.ts` (new), `apps/sandbox/src/harness/native-tools.ts` (modify), their tests, and the regenerated contract files.

### Task 1: Lane C test kit (the Goa trip and a whole Brain of fakes)

Every later task tests against these. The pool is real (from `withSchema()`); everything else records what Go sent, so travel and tasks tests run without the socket, the composer or a model.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/goa.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/lane-brain.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/lane-brain.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Brain` (`app.ts`, with `anthropic` and `onStop(fn)`); `AnthropicLike` (`composer/anthropic.ts`); `Desk`, `DeskItem`, `DeskKind` (`desk/desk.ts`); `QuickTool`, `TurnCtx`, `Tools` (`composer/tools.ts`); `NowProvider`, `Context` with `addWake` renderers returning `string | null`, `setStory`, `addTurnRules` (`composer/context.ts`); `ServerLink` (`door/server-link.ts`); `ToServerMethod`, `ToServerParams`, `ToServerResult`, `CardTapParams`, `CardTapResult`, `Trip`, `Message` (`door/methods.ts`); `TapRouter` (`door/taps.ts`); `Setup` (`trips/setup.ts`); `TripPack` (`trips/pack.ts`); `Switches`, `SwitchName` (`admin/switches.ts`).
- Produces: `GOA_TRIP: Trip`, `GOA_MESSAGES: Message[]`, `goaPack(): TripPack`; `FakeClock`, `FakeLink`, `FakeDesk`, `FakeTaps` (master's `TapRouter` with `dispatch`, which lane C never calls and which throws), `FakeTools`, `FakeContext` (`wakes: Map<DeskKind, renderer[]>`, `wake(item, pack): string | null`), `FakeSetup`, `FakeSwitches`, `makeLaneBrain(o: { pool: pg.Pool; env?: Record<string, string>; startMs?: number }): LaneBrain` (its `ctx(over?)` is a `TurnCtx` with `cards`, `stableId` and `signal` as P1's `makeTurnCtx` makes them; `brain.onStop(fn)` collects into `stoppers`, and `stop()` runs them in reverse order once, as P1's `built.stop()` does), `eventually(check, timeoutMs?)`, `TEST_START_MS`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/lane-brain.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type pg from 'pg';
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import { FakeLink, eventually, makeLaneBrain } from './lane-brain.js';
import { GOA_MESSAGES, GOA_TRIP, goaPack } from './goa.js';

const notice = { card_id: 'c_1', kind: 'notice', fallback_text: 'Hi', tone: 'info', title: 'Hi', body: 'Hello' } as const;

describe('the Goa fixture', () => {
  it('is a valid trip snapshot with the five people of the guide, and valid messages', () => {
    expect(C.TripSnapshot.safeParse(GOA_TRIP).success).toBe(true);
    expect(GOA_TRIP.people.map((p) => p.person_id)).toEqual(['p_rahul', 'p_priya', 'p_arjun', 'p_meher', 'p_kabir']);
    expect(GOA_MESSAGES.every((m) => C.Message.safeParse(m).success)).toBe(true);
    expect(goaPack().messages.at(-1)?.text).toContain('villa');
  });
});

describe('FakeLink', () => {
  it('answers a repeated request_id with the first answer, and refuses a card id already in the room', async () => {
    const link = new FakeLink();
    const a = await link.request('post_message', { trip_id: 't_goa', request_id: 'go_1', card: notice });
    const b = await link.request('post_message', { trip_id: 't_goa', request_id: 'go_1', card: notice });
    expect(b).toEqual(a);
    await expect(link.request('post_message', { trip_id: 't_goa', request_id: 'go_2', card: notice })).rejects.toThrow(/409/);
  });

  it('refuses replace_card for a card it never saw, and keeps the latest version of each card', async () => {
    const link = new FakeLink();
    await expect(link.request('replace_card', { trip_id: 't_goa', card: notice })).rejects.toThrow(/404/);
    await link.request('post_message', { trip_id: 't_goa', request_id: 'go_1', card: notice });
    await link.request('replace_card', { trip_id: 't_goa', card: { ...notice, title: 'Later' } });
    expect(link.card('c_1')?.title).toBe('Later');
    expect(link.sent('replace_card')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('can fail the next request of a method once', async () => {
    const link = new FakeLink();
    link.failNext = { method: 'open_vote', error: new Error('503 unavailable') };
    const vote = { trip_id: 't_goa', request_id: 'go_v', question: 'Which?', kind: 'choice', anonymous: false, options: [{ label: 'A' }, { label: 'B' }] } as const;
    await expect(link.request('open_vote', vote)).rejects.toThrow(/503/);
    await expect(link.request('open_vote', { ...vote, request_id: 'go_v2' })).resolves.toMatchObject({ decision_id: expect.stringMatching(/^d_/) });
  });
});

describe('makeLaneBrain', () => {
  it('gives a Brain with an idempotent desk, a clock that moves only when told, the Goa pack and default switches', async () => {
    const lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: {} as pg.Pool });
    const item = { id: 'x', tripId: 't_goa', kind: 'task' as const, ref: 'gt_1', dueAt: lb.clock.now(), payload: {} };
    await lb.desk.put(item);
    await lb.desk.put(item);
    expect(lb.desk.items).toHaveLength(1);
    const t0 = lb.clock.now().getTime();
    lb.clock.advance(5_000);
    expect(lb.brain.now().getTime() - t0).toBe(5_000);
    expect((await lb.brain.packs.get('t_goa')).trip.trip_id).toBe('t_goa');
    expect(lb.switches.get('TRAVEL_MODE')).toBe('fake');
    await lb.switches.set('TRAVEL_MODE', 'ixigo');
    expect(lb.brain.switches.get('TRAVEL_MODE')).toBe('ixigo');
    const ctx = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_K' });
    expect(ctx.outcome).toEqual({ spoke: false, silent: false, reactions: 0, posts: [] });
    expect(ctx.cards).toEqual([]);
    expect([ctx.stableId('c_opt'), ctx.stableId('c_opt'), ctx.stableId('go_vote')]).toEqual(['c_opt_turn_K_1', 'c_opt_turn_K_2', 'go_vote_turn_K_1']);
    expect(lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_K' }).stableId('c_opt')).toBe('c_opt_turn_K_1');
  });

  it('collects what lanes register with onStop and runs it in reverse order, once, as P1 does', async () => {
    const lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: {} as pg.Pool });
    const ran: string[] = [];
    lb.brain.onStop(() => {
      ran.push('first');
    });
    lb.brain.onStop(async () => {
      ran.push('second');
    });
    expect(lb.stoppers).toHaveLength(2);
    await lb.stop();
    await lb.stop();
    expect(ran).toEqual(['second', 'first']);
    expect(lb.stoppers).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('eventually waits for a condition, and gives up after its timeout', async () => {
    let ready = false;
    setTimeout(() => { ready = true; }, 30);
    await eventually(() => ready);
    expect(ready).toBe(true);
    await expect(eventually(() => false, 50)).rejects.toThrow(/timed out/);
  });

  it('keeps every wake renderer for a kind, and the first line that is not null wins, as P1 does', () => {
    const lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: {} as pg.Pool });
    const item = { id: 'tap_1', tripId: 't_goa', kind: 'tap' as const, ref: 'c_1', dueAt: lb.clock.now(), payload: { source: 'pay' } };
    lb.context.addWake('tap', (i) => ((i.payload as { source: string }).source === 'options' ? 'options line' : null));
    expect(lb.context.wake(item, goaPack())).toBeNull();
    lb.context.addWake('tap', (i) => ((i.payload as { source: string }).source === 'pay' ? 'pay line' : null));
    expect(lb.context.wake(item, goaPack())).toBe('pay line');
    expect(lb.context.wake({ ...item, payload: { source: 'options' } }, goaPack())).toBe('options line');
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/goa.ts
/**
 * The Goa trip from the guide, trimmed to what lane C's tests read: five people, the dates, the place and the
 * messages of the demo's first two beats. Parsed through the contract, so a fixture that drifts from the wire
 * fails at once.
 */
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { Message, Trip } from '../../door/methods.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../../trips/pack.js';

const person = (person_id: string, name: string, joined_at: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  person_id, name, role: 'member', joined_at, know: [], has_upi: false, go_paused: false, ...extra,
});

export const GOA_TRIP: Trip = C.TripSnapshot.parse({
  trip_id: 't_goa',
  seq: 40,
  title: 'Goa 🌴',
  status: 'planning',
  created_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z',
  organizer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
  invite_url: 'https://go.example/j/8fK2xQ',
  destination: { name: 'North Goa', lat: 15.6, lng: 73.75 },
  dates: { start: '2026-11-06', end: '2026-11-09' },
  vibe: ['beaches', 'food', 'sunsets'],
  stay: 'villa with a pool',
  budget: { lowest: { min_minor: 1000000, max_minor: 1500000, currency: 'INR' }, answered: 4, of: 5 },
  people: [
    person('p_rahul', 'Rahul', '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z', { role: 'organizer', from_city: 'Delhi', earliest_leave: 'Fri after 6 pm', has_upi: true }),
    person('p_priya', 'Priya', '2026-10-05T02:48:00Z', { from_city: 'Delhi', know: ['Vegetarian', 'Window seat'] }),
    person('p_arjun', 'Arjun', '2026-10-05T03:02:00Z', { from_city: 'Bangalore', know: ['No early flights'] }),
    person('p_meher', 'Meher', '2026-10-05T03:15:00Z', { from_city: 'Mumbai', know: ['Birthday on 7 Nov'] }),
    person('p_kabir', 'Kabir', '2026-10-05T03:40:00Z', { from_city: 'Delhi', earliest_leave: 'Fri evening', has_upi: true }),
  ],
  decisions: [],
  bookings: [],
  expenses: [],
  money: { currency: 'INR', spent_minor: 0, by_category: [], transfers: [] },
  itinerary: [],
  record: [],
});

const msg = (seq: number, personId: string, text: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Message =>
  C.Message.parse({
    message_id: `m_${seq}`,
    seq,
    updated_seq: seq,
    author: { kind: 'person', person_id: personId },
    text,
    attachments: [],
    mentions_go: false,
    mentions: [],
    reactions: [],
    withheld: false,
    deleted: false,
    sent_at: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 7, 11, 0, seq)).toISOString(),
    ...extra,
  });

export const GOA_MESSAGES: Message[] = [
  msg(31, 'p_arjun', "I can't leave before 6 on Friday, office till then"),
  msg(32, 'p_priya', "I'm veg btw"),
  msg(33, 'p_rahul', "@Go flights Delhi to Goa Friday evening, Arjun can't leave before 6", { mentions_go: true }),
  msg(34, 'p_meher', 'Go, find a villa with a pool in North Goa under 8k a head, near the beach', { mentions_go: true }),
];

export function goaPack(): TripPack {
  return { trip: GOA_TRIP, messages: GOA_MESSAGES, seq: 40 };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/lane-brain.ts
/**
 * Lane C's test kit: a whole Brain made of small fakes that record what Go sent, so travel and tasks tests run
 * without the socket, the composer or a model. The pool is real (withSchema); everything else is here.
 */
import Fastify, { type FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { Brain } from '../../app.js';
import type { AnthropicLike } from '../../composer/anthropic.js';
import type { Desk, DeskItem, DeskKind } from '../../desk/desk.js';
import type { QuickTool, Tools, TurnCtx } from '../../composer/tools.js';
import type { Context, NowProvider } from '../../composer/context.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../../door/server-link.js';
import type { TapRouter } from '../../door/taps.js';
import type { CardTapParams, CardTapResult, ToServerMethod, ToServerParams, ToServerResult } from '../../door/methods.js';
import type { Setup } from '../../trips/setup.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../../trips/pack.js';
import type { SwitchName, Switches } from '../../admin/switches.js';
import { GOA_TRIP, goaPack } from './goa.js';

/** Wednesday 7 Oct 2026, 12:00 UTC: the day G3 is checked. */
export const TEST_START_MS = Date.parse('2026-10-07T12:00:00Z');

export class FakeClock {
  constructor(public ms: number) {}
  readonly now = (): Date => new Date(this.ms);
  advance(ms: number): void {
    this.ms += ms;
  }
}

export interface SentRequest { method: string; params: Record<string, unknown> }

/** The Server's side of Go's requests. It stores each answer by request_id, as the real one does. */
export class FakeLink implements ServerLink {
  readonly requests: SentRequest[] = [];
  readonly notifications: SentRequest[] = [];
  /** The next request of this method throws this error instead of answering. */
  failNext: { method: string; error: Error } | null = null;
  private readonly answers = new Map<string, unknown>();
  private readonly cardMessages = new Map<string, string>();
  private n = 0;

  async request<M extends ToServerMethod>(method: M, params: ToServerParams<M>): Promise<ToServerResult<M>> {
    const p = structuredClone(params) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    this.requests.push({ method, params: p });
    if (this.failNext && this.failNext.method === method) {
      const { error } = this.failNext;
      this.failNext = null;
      throw error;
    }
    const requestId = typeof p.request_id === 'string' ? p.request_id : null;
    if (requestId !== null && this.answers.has(requestId)) return this.answers.get(requestId) as ToServerResult<M>;
    const answer = this.answer(method, p);
    if (requestId !== null) this.answers.set(requestId, answer);
    return answer as ToServerResult<M>;
  }

  notify(method: 'typing', params: ToServerParams<'typing'>): void {
    this.notifications.push({ method, params: structuredClone(params) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> });
  }

  isOpen(): boolean {
    return true;
  }

  /** Every request of one method, oldest first. */
  sent(method: string): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
    return this.requests.filter((r) => r.method === method).map((r) => r.params);
  }

  /** The latest version of a card, from post_message or replace_card. */
  card(cardId: string): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
    for (let i = this.requests.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
      const card = this.requests[i]!.params.card as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      if (card && card.card_id === cardId) return card;
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  private answer(method: string, p: Record<string, unknown>): unknown {
    this.n += 1;
    switch (method) {
      case 'post_message': {
        const card = p.card as { card_id?: string } | undefined;
        if (card?.card_id && this.cardMessages.has(card.card_id)) throw new Error(`409 conflict: card ${card.card_id} is already in this trip's room`);
        const messageId = `m_go_${this.n}`;
        if (card?.card_id) this.cardMessages.set(card.card_id, messageId);
        return { message_id: messageId, seq: 100 + this.n };
      }
      case 'replace_card': {
        const card = p.card as { card_id: string };
        const messageId = this.cardMessages.get(card.card_id);
        if (!messageId) throw new Error(`404 not_found: card ${card.card_id} is not on one of Go's messages`);
        return { message_id: messageId };
      }
      case 'open_vote':
        return { decision_id: `d_${this.n}`, seq: 100 + this.n };
      case 'read_trip':
        return GOA_TRIP;
      default:
        return { seq: 100 + this.n };
    }
  }
}

export class FakeDesk implements Desk {
  readonly items: DeskItem[] = [];
  readonly cancelled: Array<{ tripId: string; ref: string }> = [];
  async put(item: Omit<DeskItem, 'id'> & { id?: string }): Promise<void> {
    const id = item.id ?? `desk_${this.items.length + 1}`;
    if (this.items.some((i) => i.id === id)) return;
    this.items.push({ ...item, id });
  }
  async cancel(tripId: string, ref: string): Promise<void> {
    this.cancelled.push({ tripId, ref });
  }
  of(kind: DeskKind): DeskItem[] {
    return this.items.filter((i) => i.kind === kind);
  }
}

type TapKind = Parameters<TapRouter['on']>[0];
type TapHandler = (tap: CardTapParams, card: C.Card) => Promise<CardTapResult>;

export class FakeTaps implements TapRouter {
  readonly handlers = new Map<string, TapHandler>();
  on(kind: TapKind, actionId: string, h: TapHandler): void {
    this.handlers.set(`${kind}/${actionId}`, h);
  }
  async tap(kind: TapKind, actionId: string, tap: CardTapParams, card: C.Card): Promise<CardTapResult> {
    const h = this.handlers.get(`${kind}/${actionId}`);
    if (!h) throw new Error(`no tap handler for ${kind}/${actionId}`);
    return h(tap, card);
  }
  /** Master's TapRouter.dispatch. Lane C never dispatches a tap by id (it holds no card store), so tests use tap(). */
  async dispatch(tap: CardTapParams): Promise<CardTapResult> {
    throw new Error(`lane C tests tap with tap(kind, actionId, tap, card); dispatch(${tap.tap_id}) is P1's`);
  }
}

export class FakeTools implements Tools {
  private readonly byName = new Map<string, QuickTool<any>>();
  register(t: QuickTool<any>): void {
    if (this.byName.has(t.name)) throw new Error(`tool ${t.name} registered twice`);
    this.byName.set(t.name, t);
  }
  list(): QuickTool<any>[] {
    return [...this.byName.values()];
  }
  /** Runs a tool the way the composer does: the model's arguments are parsed first. */
  async run(name: string, args: unknown, ctx: TurnCtx): Promise<unknown> {
    const t = this.byName.get(name);
    if (!t) throw new Error(`no tool ${name}`);
    return t.run(t.input.parse(args), ctx);
  }
}

type WakeRender = (item: DeskItem, pack: TripPack) => string | null;

export class FakeContext implements Context {
  readonly nows: NowProvider[] = [];
  /** Every renderer per kind, in registration order, as P1's Context keeps them (P4, P5 and P6 all render 'tap'). */
  readonly wakes = new Map<DeskKind, WakeRender[]>();
  readonly turnRules: Array<Parameters<Context['addTurnRules']>[0]> = [];
  addNow(p: NowProvider): void {
    this.nows.push(p);
  }
  addWake(kind: DeskKind, render: WakeRender): void {
    this.wakes.set(kind, [...(this.wakes.get(kind) ?? []), render]);
  }
  /** How P1 reads one desk item in "why Go woke": the first renderer with a line wins; null means P1's generic line. */
  wake(item: DeskItem, pack: TripPack): string | null {
    for (const render of this.wakes.get(item.kind) ?? []) {
      const line = render(item, pack);
      if (line !== null) return line;
    }
    return null;
  }
  setStory(): void {
    // Lane C never folds the chat.
  }
  addTurnRules(fn: Parameters<Context['addTurnRules']>[0]): void {
    this.turnRules.push(fn);
  }
}

type SetupStep = Parameters<Setup['addStep']>[0];

export class FakeSetup implements Setup {
  readonly steps: SetupStep[] = [];
  addStep(step: SetupStep): void {
    this.steps.push(step);
  }
  async ensureTrip(tripId: string): Promise<void> {
    for (const s of this.steps) await s.run(tripId);
  }
  async run(name: string, tripId: string): Promise<void> {
    const s = this.steps.find((x) => x.name === name);
    if (!s) throw new Error(`no setup step ${name}`);
    await s.run(tripId);
  }
}

const SWITCH_DEFAULTS: Partial<Record<SwitchName, string>> = { TRAVEL_MODE: 'fake', PAY_RAIL: 'fake', CALLS_MODE: 'off', REFLEX_LIVE: '' };

export class FakeSwitches implements Switches {
  private readonly values = new Map<SwitchName, string>();
  get(name: SwitchName): string {
    return this.values.get(name) ?? SWITCH_DEFAULTS[name] ?? '';
  }
  async set(name: SwitchName, value: string): Promise<void> {
    this.values.set(name, value);
  }
}

export interface LaneBrain {
  brain: Brain;
  clock: FakeClock;
  link: FakeLink;
  desk: FakeDesk;
  taps: FakeTaps;
  tools: FakeTools;
  context: FakeContext;
  setup: FakeSetup;
  switches: FakeSwitches;
  http: FastifyInstance;
  /** What lanes registered with brain.onStop, oldest first. */
  stoppers: Array<() => Promise<void> | void>;
  /** Runs the stoppers newest first and empties the list, as P1's built.stop() does. */
  stop(): Promise<void>;
  /** A composer turn's context for the Goa trip. */
  ctx(over?: Partial<TurnCtx>): TurnCtx;
}

export function makeLaneBrain(o: { pool: pg.Pool; env?: Record<string, string>; startMs?: number }): LaneBrain {
  const clock = new FakeClock(o.startMs ?? TEST_START_MS);
  const http = Fastify({ logger: false });
  const link = new FakeLink();
  const desk = new FakeDesk();
  const taps = new FakeTaps();
  const tools = new FakeTools();
  const context = new FakeContext();
  const setup = new FakeSetup();
  const switches = new FakeSwitches();
  const stoppers: Array<() => Promise<void> | void> = [];
  const unsure = { value: null, sure: false as const };
  const noModel = (): never => {
    throw new Error('lane C tests never call the model');
  };
  const brain: Brain = {
    env: { GO_PUBLIC_URL: 'https://go.test', ...o.env } as unknown as Brain['env'],
    pool: o.pool,
    log: http.log,
    now: clock.now,
    http,
    anthropic: { messages: { stream: noModel, create: noModel } } as unknown as AnthropicLike,
    onStop: (fn) => {
      stoppers.push(fn);
    },
    door: { handle: () => undefined, link },
    taps,
    tripLog: { append: async () => 'new', lastSeq: async () => 0, onEvent: () => undefined },
    packs: { get: async () => goaPack(), invalidate: () => undefined },
    setup,
    desk,
    tools,
    context,
    hooks: { onRead: () => undefined },
    switches,
    reflex: {
      read: async () => ({ forGo: unsure, complete: unsure, size: unsure, taskId: unsure, fact: unsure, recordKind: unsure, reaction: unsure, risky: unsure, opening: unsure }),
    },
  };
  const ctx = (over: Partial<TurnCtx> = {}): TurnCtx => {
    const turnId = over.turnId ?? 'turn_test_1';
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    return {
      tripId: 't_goa',
      turnId,
      link,
      pack: goaPack(),
      outcome: { spoke: false, silent: false, reactions: 0, posts: [] },
      wake: [],
      cards: [],
      // The same ids P1's makeTurnCtx gives: a rerun of the same turn repeats them.
      stableId(prefix: string): string {
        const n = (counts.get(prefix) ?? 0) + 1;
        counts.set(prefix, n);
        return `${prefix}_${turnId}_${n}`;
      },
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      ...over,
    };
  };
  const stop = async (): Promise<void> => {
    for (const fn of stoppers.splice(0).reverse()) await fn();
  };
  return { brain, clock, link, desk, taps, tools, context, setup, switches, http, stoppers, stop, ctx };
}

/** Waits until check() is true, looking every 10 ms, or fails after timeoutMs. */
export async function eventually(check: () => boolean | Promise<boolean>, timeoutMs = 3_000): Promise<void> {
  const until = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
  for (;;) {
    if (await check()) return;
    if (Date.now() > until) throw new Error('eventually: timed out');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
  }
}
```

### Task 2: Travel types, ids and the fake ixigo catalog

The fake ixigo is deterministic: the same search always returns the same flights, fares and ids, and ids carry the route and date so a hold can find its fare again. It is the demo's labelled "simulated" backend and the base the real ixigo mapping is tested against. It sells flights and stays; trains and buses come with the stretch Task 8b.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/travel/travel.ts` (P1's seam: the four placeholder types become real ones; `Hold` keeps master's `refundableUntil`; `Travel.offer` keeps master's exact signature; `Travel` gains `simulate`; nothing is renamed)
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/ids.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/ids.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Booking`, `Journey`, `Place` types from `@go/contract`.
- Produces (`travel.ts`): `TravelKind`, `TravelQuery` (zod and type), `Clock`, `MUST_HAVES`, `TravelOptionKind`, `TravelOption`, `TravelSearch`, `StatusQuery`, `StatusResult`, `Hold` (with `refundableUntil?: string`), `Confirmed`, `StoredOffer`, `TravelBackend`, `Travel` (master §6.3's methods, `offer(optionId)` with its exact signature, `confirm` safe to repeat, plus optional `simulate`), `OfferRef` (what `offer` answers). (`ids.ts`): `mintId(prefix)`, `stableId(prefix, ...parts)`. (`data.ts`): `FAKE_VENDOR`, `FakeIxigoStore` (with `setDelay`), `FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS`, `FAKE_IXIGO_TOOLS`, `FakeIxigoToolName`, `callFakeIxigo(store, name, args)`, `searchFlights`, `searchStays`, `findFare`, raw types `RawFlight`, `RawStay`, `RawBooking` (Task 8b adds `searchTrains`, `searchBuses`, `RawTrain`, `RawBus`).

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/ids.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { mintId, stableId } from './ids.js';

describe('ids', () => {
  it('mints fresh ids with the prefix, short enough for a contract Id', () => {
    const a = mintId('gt');
    const b = mintId('gt');
    expect(a).toMatch(/^gt_[0-9a-z]{20}$/);
    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
  });

  it('gives the same id for the same parts and a different one for different parts', () => {
    expect(stableId('c_opt', 'turn_1', 'r_1', '1,2,3')).toBe(stableId('c_opt', 'turn_1', 'r_1', '1,2,3'));
    expect(stableId('c_opt', 'turn_1', 'r_1', '1,2,3')).not.toBe(stableId('c_opt', 'turn_2', 'r_1', '1,2,3'));
    expect(stableId('go', 'a', 'bc')).not.toBe(stableId('go', 'ab', 'c'));
    expect(stableId('go', 'x').length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(64);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FAKE_VENDOR, FakeIxigoStore, callFakeIxigo, findFare, searchFlights, searchStays } from './data.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-07T12:00:00Z');
const FRIDAY = '2026-11-06';

describe('flights', () => {
  it('has Friday Delhi to Goa flights before and after 6 pm, sorted by time, priced in paise with local times', () => {
    const { flights } = searchFlights({ origin: 'Delhi', destination: 'Goa', date: FRIDAY });
    expect(flights).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(flights.map((f) => f.depart_at)).toEqual([...flights.map((f) => f.depart_at)].sort());
    const times = flights.map((f) => f.depart_at.slice(11, 16));
    expect(times).toContain('17:50');
    expect(times).toContain('18:10');
    expect(times.some((t) => t < '18:00') && times.some((t) => t >= '18:00')).toBe(true);
    for (const f of flights) {
      expect(Number.isInteger(f.fare_paise)).toBe(true);
      expect(f.depart_at.endsWith('+05:30')).toBe(true);
    }
    const sixE = flights.find((f) => f.flight_no === '2134')!;
    expect(sixE).toMatchObject({ fare_id: 'fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-6E2134', airline: 'IndiGo', fare_paise: 592000, arrive_at: '2026-11-06T20:45:00+05:30', duration_min: 155 });
  });

  it('is the same on every call and has nothing for a route it does not fly', () => {
    expect(searchFlights({ origin: 'DEL', destination: 'GOX', date: FRIDAY })).toEqual(searchFlights({ origin: 'delhi', destination: 'North Goa', date: FRIDAY }));
    expect(searchFlights({ origin: 'Delhi', destination: 'Paris', date: FRIDAY }).flights).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('stays', () => {
  it('has North Goa villas for 3 nights with whole-stay totals, pools, beach minutes and https images, and leaves out ones too small', () => {
    const r = searchStays({ location: 'North Goa', check_in: FRIDAY, check_out: '2026-11-09', guests: 5 });
    if ('error' in r) throw new Error(r.error);
    expect(r.stays.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
    for (const s of r.stays) {
      expect(s.region).toBe('North Goa');
      expect(s.nights).toBe(3);
      expect(s.total_paise).toBe(s.price_per_night_paise * 3);
      expect(s.image_url).toMatch(/^https:\/\//);
      expect(s.sleeps).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
    }
    expect(r.stays.find((s) => s.name === 'Villa Sol')).toMatchObject({ area: 'Anjuna', private_pool: true, beach_minutes: 8, price_per_night_paise: 1280000 });
    expect(r.stays.some((s) => s.name === 'Villa Coco')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses dates that make no stay', () => {
    expect(searchStays({ location: 'Goa', check_in: '2026-11-09', check_out: FRIDAY })).toEqual({ error: 'invalid_dates' });
  });
});

describe('the store: status, holds and bookings', () => {
  it('reports a flight on schedule, then late after setDelay', () => {
    const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: () => NOW });
    expect(store.flightStatus({ airline_code: '6E', flight_no: '2134', date: FRIDAY })).toMatchObject({ status: 'scheduled', delay_min: 0 });
    store.setDelay('6e', '2134', FRIDAY, 120);
    expect(store.flightStatus({ airline_code: '6E', flight_no: '2134', date: FRIDAY })).toMatchObject({
      status: 'delayed', delay_min: 120, scheduled_departure: '2026-11-06T18:10:00+05:30', estimated_departure: '2026-11-06T20:10:00+05:30',
    });
  });

  it('holds a fare for the travellers, confirms it once, and finds it by reference', () => {
    let now = NOW;
    const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: () => now });
    const hold = store.hold({ fare_id: 'fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-6E2134', lead_name: 'Rahul', travellers: 2 });
    if ('error' in hold) throw new Error(hold.error);
    expect(hold).toMatchObject({ amount_paise: 1184000, currency: 'INR', merchant: FAKE_VENDOR });
    const booking = store.confirm({ hold_id: hold.hold_id, payment_ref: 'fake_pay_1' });
    if ('error' in booking) throw new Error(booking.error);
    expect(booking).toMatchObject({ status: 'confirmed', kind: 'flight', title: 'IndiGo 6E 2134', vendor: FAKE_VENDOR, amount_paise: 1184000 });
    expect(booking.booking_ref).toMatch(/^IXG\d{6}$/);
    expect(booking.journey).toMatchObject({ mode: 'flight', from_code: 'DEL', to_code: 'GOI', number: '6E 2134' });
    expect(store.confirm({ hold_id: hold.hold_id, payment_ref: 'fake_pay_1' })).toEqual(booking);
    expect(store.pnr({ pnr: booking.booking_ref })).toMatchObject({ status: 'confirmed' });
    expect(store.cancel({ booking_ref: booking.booking_ref })).toMatchObject({ status: 'cancelled', refund_paise: 947200 });
    now = new Date(NOW.getTime() + 16 * 60_000);
    const late = store.hold({ fare_id: 'fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-6E2134', lead_name: 'Rahul', travellers: 1 });
    if ('error' in late) throw new Error(late.error);
    now = new Date(now.getTime() + 16 * 60_000);
    expect(store.confirm({ hold_id: late.hold_id, payment_ref: 'x' })).toEqual({ error: 'hold_expired' });
  });

  it('prices a stay hold as the whole stay, and finds a flight or a stay fare by its id', () => {
    const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: () => NOW });
    const hold = store.hold({ fare_id: 'st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5', lead_name: 'Rahul', travellers: 5 });
    if ('error' in hold) throw new Error(hold.error);
    expect(hold.amount_paise).toBe(3840000);
    expect(findFare('fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-6E2134')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'flight', unitPaise: 592000 });
    expect(findFare('st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'stay', unitPaise: 3840000 });
    expect(findFare('fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-XX0000')).toBeNull();
    expect(findFare('tr-NZM-MAO-20261106-22414-3A')).toBeNull(); // trains arrive with Task 8b
  });

  it('says on the hold until when a refundable villa is free to cancel, and nothing for one that is not', () => {
    const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: () => NOW });
    const sol = store.hold({ fare_id: 'st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5', lead_name: 'Rahul', travellers: 5 });
    if ('error' in sol) throw new Error(sol.error);
    expect(sol.refundable_until).toBe('2026-11-03T23:59:00+05:30');
    const azul = store.hold({ fare_id: 'st-casa-azul-assagao-20261106-20261109-5', lead_name: 'Rahul', travellers: 5 });
    if ('error' in azul) throw new Error(azul.error);
    expect(azul.refundable_until).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('callFakeIxigo', () => {
  it('runs a tool by name, and answers bad arguments and unknown tools as tool errors', () => {
    const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: () => NOW });
    const ok = callFakeIxigo(store, 'search_flights', { origin: 'Delhi', destination: 'Goa', date: FRIDAY });
    expect(ok.isError).toBe(false);
    expect((ok.value as { flights: unknown[] }).flights).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(callFakeIxigo(store, 'search_flights', { origin: 'Delhi', destination: 'Goa', date: 'Friday' })).toMatchObject({ isError: true, value: { error: 'invalid_arguments' } });
    expect(callFakeIxigo(store, 'book_everything', {})).toEqual({ value: { error: 'unknown_tool' }, isError: true });
    expect(callFakeIxigo(store, 'confirm_booking', { hold_id: 'hold_nope', payment_ref: 'x' })).toEqual({ value: { error: 'hold_not_found' }, isError: true });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/travel.ts
/**
 * Go's travel layer: one interface for search, status and booking, whatever answers behind it (ixigo's MCP
 * server, or the fake ixigo). Money is integer paise. Times keep their local offset, as the place uses them.
 */
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Booking, Journey, Place } from '@go/contract';

export const TravelKind = z.enum(['flights', 'trains', 'buses', 'stays']);
export type TravelKind = z.infer<typeof TravelKind>;

export const Clock = z.string().regex(/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/, 'Use 24-hour HH:MM, like 18:00.');
export const MUST_HAVES = ['pool', 'beach', 'breakfast', 'free_cancellation', 'non_stop'] as const;

/** One search. The constraints (times, price, must-haves, rating) are applied in code after the search. */
export const TravelQuery = z.object({
  from: z.string().min(2).max(60).optional(),
  to: z.string().min(2).max(60).optional(),
  date: z.iso.date().optional(),
  area: z.string().min(2).max(60).optional(),
  checkIn: z.iso.date().optional(),
  checkOut: z.iso.date().optional(),
  guests: z.int().min(1).max(30).optional(),
  departAfter: Clock.optional(),
  departBefore: Clock.optional(),
  arriveBefore: Clock.optional(),
  /** Per person for journeys, per night for stays. */
  maxPriceMinor: z.int().positive().optional(),
  mustHave: z.array(z.enum(MUST_HAVES)).max(5).optional(),
  minRating: z.number().min(0).max(5).optional(),
});
export type TravelQuery = z.infer<typeof TravelQuery>;

export type TravelOptionKind = 'flight' | 'train' | 'bus' | 'stay';

export interface TravelOption {
  /** "<backend>:<the backend's own id>", e.g. "fake:fl-DEL-GOI-20261106-6E2134". hold() routes on the prefix. */
  offerId: string;
  kind: TravelOptionKind;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  priceMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  /** What the price covers: "per person", "per night". */
  priceNote: string;
  /** Stays: the whole stay for the nights searched. */
  totalMinor?: number;
  journey?: Journey;
  place?: Place;
  /** The backend's image; cards always show Go's re-hosted copy. */
  imageUrl?: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  rating?: number;
  highlights: string[];
  refundableUntil?: string;
  /** Short facts the filters and the model read: pool, beach_minutes, sleeps, seats_left, class_code. */
  facts: Record<string, string | number | boolean>;
}

export interface TravelSearch { results: TravelOption[]; checkedAt: string }

export type StatusQuery =
  | { kind: 'flight'; carrierCode: string; number: string; date: string }
  | { kind: 'pnr'; pnr: string };

export type StatusResult =
  | {
      kind: 'flight';
      state: 'scheduled' | 'delayed' | 'cancelled' | 'departed' | 'landed';
      scheduledDepartAt: string;
      expectedDepartAt: string;
      delayMinutes: number;
      checkedAt: string;
    }
  | { kind: 'pnr'; state: 'confirmed' | 'waitlisted' | 'cancelled' | 'not_found'; detail: string; checkedAt: string };

export interface Hold {
  holdId: string;
  amountMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  merchant: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  /** Until when the booking is free to cancel, when the fare is refundable. P5 refuses a refundable-only mandate without it. */
  refundableUntil?: string;
  payment: { kind: 'fake' } | { kind: 'stripe_test' } | { kind: 'ixigo'; token: string };
}

export interface Confirmed {
  ref: string;
  vendor: string;
  title: string;
  kind: Booking['kind'];
  startsAt?: string;
  endsAt?: string;
  refundableUntil?: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  place?: Place;
  journey?: Journey;
}

/** One option Go showed on an options card, kept so a tap, a vote or a payment can name it later. */
export interface StoredOffer {
  optionId: string;
  tripId: string;
  cardId: string;
  resultsId: string;
  /** Its number on the card, from 1. */
  n: number;
  /** Null when the option came from a job's web browsing: it can be shown and voted on, not booked through Go. */
  offerId: string | null;
  kind: string;
  title: string;
  priceMinor: number | null;
  /** Stays: the whole stay, which is what a vote and a payment use. */
  totalMinor: number | null;
  currency: string | null;
  priceNote: string | null;
  checkedAt: string;
}

/** What answers behind the layer: the fake ixigo or ixigo's MCP server. */
export interface TravelBackend {
  search(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery): Promise<TravelSearch>;
  status(q: StatusQuery): Promise<StatusResult>;
  hold(offerId: string, lead: { name: string; personIds: string[] }): Promise<Hold>;
  confirm(holdId: string, paid: { approvalId: string; railRef: string }): Promise<Confirmed>;
}

/** brain.travel: the master plan's methods (search, status, offer, hold, confirm), plus the demo's flight-delay switch. */
export interface Travel {
  search(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery): Promise<TravelSearch>;
  status(q: StatusQuery): Promise<StatusResult>;
  /**
   * The offer behind an options card's option_id (which is also a vote option's option_ref); offerId null when the option
   * came from a job's web browsing (P5 refuses to book those). priceMinor is what a vote and a payment use: the whole stay
   * for a stay, the fare per person for a journey. Null when Go never showed that option.
   */
  offer(optionId: string): Promise<{ offerId: string | null; title: string; priceMinor: number | null; currency: string | null; kind: 'flight' | 'train' | 'bus' | 'stay'; link: string | null } | null>;
  /** Booking is two steps so card details only ever travel between the vault and the rail. Composer and P5 only. */
  hold(offerId: string, lead: { name: string; personIds: string[] }): Promise<Hold>;
  /** Safe to repeat: the same hold confirmed twice gives the same answer. */
  confirm(holdId: string, paid: { approvalId: string; railRef: string }): Promise<Confirmed>;
  /** Present while the fake ixigo runs: makes a flight late, for the demo's labelled "simulated" watch. */
  simulate?: { setFlightDelay(carrierCode: string, number: string, date: string, minutes: number): void };
}

/** What Travel.offer answers for an option Go showed. */
export type OfferRef = NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<Travel['offer']>>>;
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/ids.ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';

const ALPHABET = '0123456789abcdefghjkmnpqrstvwxyz';

function base32(buf: Uint8Array): string {
  let bits = 0;
  let value = 0;
  let out = '';
  for (const b of buf) {
    value = (value << 8) | b;
    bits += 8;
    while (bits >= 5) {
      bits -= 5;
      out += ALPHABET[(value >>> bits) & 31];
    }
    value &= (1 << bits) - 1;
  }
  if (bits > 0) out += ALPHABET[(value << (5 - bits)) & 31];
  return out;
}

/** A fresh id: the prefix, an underscore, 20 random characters. */
export function mintId(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}_${base32(randomBytes(13)).slice(0, 20)}`;
}

/** The same parts always give the same id, so a retried step writes the same thing once. */
export function stableId(prefix: string, ...parts: Array<string | number>): string {
  const hash = createHash('sha256').update(parts.join('\u0000')).digest();
  return `${prefix}_${base32(hash).slice(0, 24)}`;
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.ts
/**
 * The fake ixigo: deterministic flights into Goa, North and South Goa villas, live flight status,
 * and a two-step booking (hold, then confirm), answered in raw shapes like a partner API's. One table of tools
 * serves both the standalone MCP server (server.ts) and Go's in-process backend (mcp-caller.ts). Ids carry the route,
 * the date and the item, so a search and a hold read the same after a restart. Everything it sells is labelled
 * "ixigo (simulated)".
 */
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { z } from 'zod';

export const FAKE_VENDOR = 'ixigo (simulated)';
const IST = '+05:30';
const MAX_KEPT = 1_000;
const HOLD_MS = 15 * 60_000;

// ── time and small helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

function addDays(date: string, days: number): string {
  const d = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
const compactDate = (date: string): string => date.replaceAll('-', '');
const expandDate = (yyyymmdd: string): string => `${yyyymmdd.slice(0, 4)}-${yyyymmdd.slice(4, 6)}-${yyyymmdd.slice(6, 8)}`;
const at = (date: string, clock: string): string => `${date}T${clock}:00${IST}`;
const clockMinutes = (clock: string): number => Number(clock.slice(0, 2)) * 60 + Number(clock.slice(3, 5));
/** A Date as an ISO time in India, e.g. 2026-11-06T20:10:00+05:30. */
const toIst = (d: Date): string => `${new Date(d.getTime() + 330 * 60_000).toISOString().slice(0, 19)}${IST}`;

function leg(date: string, dep: string, arr: string, days: number): { depart_at: string; arrive_at: string; duration_min: number } {
  return { depart_at: at(date, dep), arrive_at: at(addDays(date, days), arr), duration_min: days * 1440 + clockMinutes(arr) - clockMinutes(dep) };
}

function nightsBetween(checkIn: string, checkOut: string): number {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(`${checkOut}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${checkIn}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000);
}

/** A small number from a string, the same every time. */
function seeded(text: string, mod: number): number {
  return createHash('sha256').update(text).digest().readUInt32BE(0) % mod;
}

function keepAtMost<K, V>(map: Map<K, V>, max: number): void {
  while (map.size > max) {
    const oldest = map.keys().next().value;
    if (oldest === undefined) return;
    map.delete(oldest);
  }
}

// ── flights ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const AIRPORTS: Record<string, string> = { DEL: 'Delhi', GOI: 'Goa (Dabolim)', GOX: 'North Goa (Mopa)', BOM: 'Mumbai', BLR: 'Bengaluru' };
const AIRPORT_WORDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  delhi: ['DEL'], 'new delhi': ['DEL'], goa: ['GOI', 'GOX'], 'north goa': ['GOX'], mopa: ['GOX'], 'south goa': ['GOI'], dabolim: ['GOI'],
  mumbai: ['BOM'], bombay: ['BOM'], bangalore: ['BLR'], bengaluru: ['BLR'],
};

export function airportCodes(place: string): string[] {
  const words = AIRPORT_WORDS[place.trim().toLowerCase()];
  if (words) return words;
  const code = place.trim().toUpperCase();
  return code in AIRPORTS ? [code] : [];
}

interface Flight { from: string; to: string; airline: string; code: string; number: string; dep: string; arr: string; fare: number }

const FLIGHTS: Flight[] = [
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '515', dep: '06:15', arr: '08:50', fare: 489000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'SpiceJet', code: 'SG', number: '8169', dep: '09:40', arr: '12:20', fare: 521000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'Air India', code: 'AI', number: '2985', dep: '13:05', arr: '15:40', fare: 615000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'Akasa Air', code: 'QP', number: '1342', dep: '16:30', arr: '19:05', fare: 548000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '2134', dep: '18:10', arr: '20:45', fare: 592000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'Air India', code: 'AI', number: '2981', dep: '19:45', arr: '22:20', fare: 598000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '6721', dep: '21:20', arr: '23:55', fare: 564000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOX', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '6153', dep: '07:30', arr: '10:05', fare: 535000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOX', airline: 'Akasa Air', code: 'QP', number: '1846', dep: '17:50', arr: '20:25', fare: 604000 },
  { from: 'DEL', to: 'GOX', airline: 'Air India', code: 'AI', number: '2847', dep: '20:30', arr: '23:05', fare: 642000 },
  { from: 'BOM', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '5394', dep: '07:05', arr: '08:15', fare: 312000 },
  { from: 'BOM', to: 'GOI', airline: 'Air India', code: 'AI', number: '663', dep: '18:40', arr: '19:50', fare: 365000 },
  { from: 'BOM', to: 'GOX', airline: 'Akasa Air', code: 'QP', number: '1121', dep: '11:20', arr: '12:30', fare: 298000 },
  { from: 'BLR', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '7146', dep: '06:40', arr: '07:55', fare: 341000 },
  { from: 'BLR', to: 'GOI', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '7158', dep: '19:25', arr: '20:40', fare: 389000 },
  { from: 'BLR', to: 'GOX', airline: 'Akasa Air', code: 'QP', number: '1428', dep: '20:10', arr: '21:25', fare: 402000 },
  { from: 'GOI', to: 'DEL', airline: 'IndiGo', code: '6E', number: '6722', dep: '15:10', arr: '17:45', fare: 612000 },
  { from: 'GOI', to: 'DEL', airline: 'Air India', code: 'AI', number: '2982', dep: '19:30', arr: '22:05', fare: 585000 },
  { from: 'GOX', to: 'DEL', airline: 'Akasa Air', code: 'QP', number: '1847', dep: '21:00', arr: '23:35', fare: 571000 },
];

export interface RawFlight {
  fare_id: string; airline: string; airline_code: string; flight_no: string;
  from_code: string; from_city: string; to_code: string; to_city: string;
  depart_at: string; arrive_at: string; duration_min: number; stops: number;
  fare_paise: number; cabin: 'economy'; refundable_until: string | null; seats_left: number;
}

function rawFlight(f: Flight, date: string): RawFlight {
  const fareId = `fl-${f.from}-${f.to}-${compactDate(date)}-${f.code}${f.number}`;
  return {
    fare_id: fareId,
    airline: f.airline,
    airline_code: f.code,
    flight_no: f.number,
    from_code: f.from,
    from_city: AIRPORTS[f.from]!,
    to_code: f.to,
    to_city: AIRPORTS[f.to]!,
    ...leg(date, f.dep, f.arr, f.arr < f.dep ? 1 : 0),
    stops: 0,
    fare_paise: f.fare,
    cabin: 'economy',
    // Air India sells a fare that is free to cancel up to two days before; the others do not.
    refundable_until: f.code === 'AI' ? at(addDays(date, -2), '23:59') : null,
    seats_left: 2 + seeded(fareId, 8),
  };
}

export function searchFlights(a: { origin: string; destination: string; date: string }): { flights: RawFlight[] } {
  const from = airportCodes(a.origin);
  const to = airportCodes(a.destination);
  const flights = FLIGHTS.filter((f) => from.includes(f.from) && to.includes(f.to)).map((f) => rawFlight(f, a.date));
  return { flights: flights.sort((x, y) => x.depart_at.localeCompare(y.depart_at)) };
}

// ── stays ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface Stay {
  slug: string; name: string; area: string; region: 'North Goa' | 'South Goa'; lat: number; lng: number; rating: number; reviews: number;
  sleeps: number; bedrooms: number; pool: boolean; beach: number; breakfast: boolean; night: number; freeCancel: boolean;
}

const STAYS: Stay[] = [
  { slug: 'villa-sol-anjuna', name: 'Villa Sol', area: 'Anjuna', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.5835, lng: 73.7417, rating: 4.7, reviews: 212, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 8, breakfast: true, night: 1280000, freeCancel: true },
  { slug: 'casa-azul-assagao', name: 'Casa Azul', area: 'Assagao', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.5976, lng: 73.7642, rating: 4.8, reviews: 168, sleeps: 8, bedrooms: 4, pool: true, beach: 15, breakfast: false, night: 1460000, freeCancel: false },
  { slug: 'the-palms-vagator', name: 'The Palms Villa', area: 'Vagator', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.6011, lng: 73.7364, rating: 4.5, reviews: 301, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 5, breakfast: true, night: 1220000, freeCancel: true },
  { slug: 'villa-verde-siolim', name: 'Villa Verde', area: 'Siolim', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.6215, lng: 73.765, rating: 4.4, reviews: 97, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 20, breakfast: false, night: 1150000, freeCancel: false },
  { slug: 'ocean-breeze-candolim', name: 'Ocean Breeze Villa', area: 'Candolim', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.518, lng: 73.762, rating: 4.3, reviews: 254, sleeps: 5, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 3, breakfast: true, night: 1310000, freeCancel: true },
  { slug: 'casa-mareia-morjim', name: 'Casa Mareia', area: 'Morjim', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.629, lng: 73.729, rating: 4.9, reviews: 88, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 2, breakfast: true, night: 1690000, freeCancel: true },
  { slug: 'saudade-parra', name: 'Saudade Villa', area: 'Parra', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.57, lng: 73.79, rating: 4.2, reviews: 64, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: false, beach: 18, breakfast: false, night: 980000, freeCancel: false },
  { slug: 'villa-coco-arpora', name: 'Villa Coco', area: 'Arpora', region: 'North Goa', lat: 15.564, lng: 73.766, rating: 4.6, reviews: 143, sleeps: 4, bedrooms: 2, pool: true, beach: 12, breakfast: true, night: 1040000, freeCancel: false },
  { slug: 'casa-serena-benaulim', name: 'Casa Serena', area: 'Benaulim', region: 'South Goa', lat: 15.258, lng: 73.928, rating: 4.6, reviews: 120, sleeps: 6, bedrooms: 3, pool: true, beach: 6, breakfast: true, night: 1180000, freeCancel: true },
  { slug: 'villa-mar-colva', name: 'Villa Mar', area: 'Colva', region: 'South Goa', lat: 15.279, lng: 73.922, rating: 4.4, reviews: 76, sleeps: 8, bedrooms: 4, pool: true, beach: 4, breakfast: false, night: 1390000, freeCancel: false },
];

export interface RawStay {
  stay_id: string; name: string; type: 'villa'; area: string; region: string; address: string; lat: number; lng: number;
  image_url: string; rating: number; reviews: number; sleeps: number; bedrooms: number; private_pool: boolean; beach_minutes: number;
  breakfast: boolean; price_per_night_paise: number; nights: number; total_paise: number; free_cancellation_until: string | null;
}

function rawStay(s: Stay, checkIn: string, checkOut: string, guests: number): RawStay {
  const nights = nightsBetween(checkIn, checkOut);
  return {
    stay_id: `st-${s.slug}-${compactDate(checkIn)}-${compactDate(checkOut)}-${guests}`,
    name: s.name, type: 'villa', area: s.area, region: s.region, address: `${s.area}, ${s.region}, Goa`, lat: s.lat, lng: s.lng,
    image_url: `https://picsum.photos/seed/go-${s.slug}/800/600`,
    rating: s.rating, reviews: s.reviews, sleeps: s.sleeps, bedrooms: s.bedrooms, private_pool: s.pool, beach_minutes: s.beach,
    breakfast: s.breakfast, price_per_night_paise: s.night, nights, total_paise: s.night * nights,
    free_cancellation_until: s.freeCancel ? at(addDays(checkIn, -3), '23:59') : null,
  };
}

export function searchStays(a: { location: string; check_in: string; check_out: string; guests?: number | undefined }): { stays: RawStay[] } | { error: string } {
  const nights = nightsBetween(a.check_in, a.check_out);
  if (nights < 1 || nights > 30) return { error: 'invalid_dates' };
  const where = a.location.trim().toLowerCase();
  const matches = STAYS.filter((s) => where === 'goa' || where === s.region.toLowerCase() || where === s.area.toLowerCase() || s.name.toLowerCase().includes(where));
  const fits = matches.filter((s) => a.guests === undefined || s.sleeps >= a.guests);
  return { stays: fits.map((s) => rawStay(s, a.check_in, a.check_out, a.guests ?? 0)) };
}

// ── finding a fare again by its id ───────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RawJourney {
  mode: 'flight' | 'train' | 'bus'; from_name: string; from_code: string | null; to_name: string; to_code: string | null;
  depart_at: string; arrive_at: string; carrier: string; carrier_code: string | null; number: string | null;
}
export interface RawPlace { name: string; address: string; lat: number; lng: number }
export interface FareFound {
  kind: 'flight' | 'train' | 'bus' | 'stay'; title: string; unitPaise: number; perTraveller: boolean; startsAt: string; endsAt: string;
  refundableUntil: string | null; imageUrl: string | null; journey: RawJourney | null; place: RawPlace | null;
}

export function findFare(fareId: string): FareFound | null {
  const parts = fareId.split('-');
  if (parts[0] === 'fl' && parts.length === 5) {
    const [, from, to, day, flightKey] = parts as [string, string, string, string, string];
    const f = FLIGHTS.find((x) => x.from === from && x.to === to && `${x.code}${x.number}` === flightKey);
    if (!f || !/^\d{8}$/.test(day)) return null;
    const r = rawFlight(f, expandDate(day));
    return {
      kind: 'flight', title: `${f.airline} ${f.code} ${f.number}`, unitPaise: f.fare, perTraveller: true, startsAt: r.depart_at, endsAt: r.arrive_at,
      refundableUntil: r.refundable_until, imageUrl: null, place: null,
      journey: { mode: 'flight', from_name: r.from_city, from_code: f.from, to_name: r.to_city, to_code: f.to, depart_at: r.depart_at, arrive_at: r.arrive_at, carrier: f.airline, carrier_code: f.code, number: `${f.code} ${f.number}` },
    };
  }
  if (parts[0] === 'st' && parts.length >= 5) {
    const guests = Number(parts.at(-1));
    const out = parts.at(-2) ?? '';
    const into = parts.at(-3) ?? '';
    const stay = STAYS.find((s) => s.slug === parts.slice(1, -3).join('-'));
    if (!stay || !/^\d{8}$/.test(out) || !/^\d{8}$/.test(into) || !Number.isInteger(guests)) return null;
    const r = rawStay(stay, expandDate(into), expandDate(out), guests);
    if (r.nights < 1) return null;
    return {
      kind: 'stay', title: stay.name, unitPaise: r.total_paise, perTraveller: false, startsAt: at(expandDate(into), '14:00'), endsAt: at(expandDate(out), '11:00'),
      refundableUntil: r.free_cancellation_until, imageUrl: r.image_url, journey: null,
      place: { name: stay.name, address: r.address, lat: stay.lat, lng: stay.lng },
    };
  }
  return null;
}

// ── the store: status, holds and bookings ────────────────────────────────────

export interface RawFlightStatus { flight: string; date: string; status: 'scheduled' | 'delayed'; scheduled_departure: string; estimated_departure: string; delay_min: number }
export interface RawHold { hold_id: string; amount_paise: number; currency: 'INR'; merchant: string; expires_at: string; refundable_until: string | null }
export interface RawBooking {
  booking_ref: string; status: 'confirmed' | 'cancelled'; kind: FareFound['kind']; title: string; vendor: string; amount_paise: number;
  starts_at: string; ends_at: string; refundable_until: string | null; image_url: string | null; place: RawPlace | null; journey: RawJourney | null;
}

interface HoldRecord { fareId: string; amount: number; expiresAtMs: number; bookingRef: string | null }

export class FakeIxigoStore {
  private readonly holds = new Map<string, HoldRecord>();
  private readonly bookings = new Map<string, RawBooking>();
  private readonly delays = new Map<string, number>();
  private n = 0;

  constructor(private readonly o: { now: () => Date }) {}

  /** Makes one flight late on one day, for the demo's simulated watch. */
  setDelay(carrierCode: string, number: string, date: string, minutes: number): void {
    this.delays.set(`${carrierCode.toUpperCase()}${number}@${date}`, minutes);
  }

  flightStatus(a: { airline_code: string; flight_no: string; date: string }): RawFlightStatus | { error: string } {
    const f = FLIGHTS.find((x) => x.code === a.airline_code.toUpperCase() && x.number === a.flight_no);
    if (!f) return { error: 'flight_not_found' };
    const delay = this.delays.get(`${f.code}${f.number}@${a.date}`) ?? 0;
    const scheduled = at(a.date, f.dep);
    return {
      flight: `${f.code} ${f.number}`, date: a.date, status: delay > 0 ? 'delayed' : 'scheduled', scheduled_departure: scheduled,
      estimated_departure: toIst(new Date(Date.parse(scheduled) + delay * 60_000)), delay_min: delay,
    };
  }

  hold(a: { fare_id: string; lead_name: string; travellers: number }): RawHold | { error: string } {
    const fare = findFare(a.fare_id);
    if (!fare) return { error: 'fare_not_found' };
    this.n += 1;
    const holdId = `hold_${String(this.n).padStart(6, '0')}${seeded(`${a.fare_id}/${this.n}`, 1000).toString().padStart(3, '0')}`;
    const amount = fare.perTraveller ? fare.unitPaise * a.travellers : fare.unitPaise;
    const expiresAtMs = this.o.now().getTime() + HOLD_MS;
    this.holds.set(holdId, { fareId: a.fare_id, amount, expiresAtMs, bookingRef: null });
    keepAtMost(this.holds, MAX_KEPT);
    // A refundable fare says until when on the hold, so the payer sees it before paying.
    return { hold_id: holdId, amount_paise: amount, currency: 'INR', merchant: FAKE_VENDOR, expires_at: toIst(new Date(expiresAtMs)), refundable_until: fare.refundableUntil };
  }

  confirm(a: { hold_id: string; payment_ref: string }): RawBooking | { error: string } {
    const h = this.holds.get(a.hold_id);
    if (!h) return { error: 'hold_not_found' };
    if (h.bookingRef) {
      const done = this.bookings.get(h.bookingRef);
      if (done) return done;
    }
    if (this.o.now().getTime() > h.expiresAtMs) return { error: 'hold_expired' };
    const fare = findFare(h.fareId);
    if (!fare) return { error: 'fare_not_found' };
    const ref = `IXG${100000 + seeded(a.hold_id, 900000)}`;
    const booking: RawBooking = {
      booking_ref: ref, status: 'confirmed', kind: fare.kind, title: fare.title, vendor: FAKE_VENDOR, amount_paise: h.amount,
      starts_at: fare.startsAt, ends_at: fare.endsAt, refundable_until: fare.refundableUntil, image_url: fare.imageUrl, place: fare.place, journey: fare.journey,
    };
    this.bookings.set(ref, booking);
    keepAtMost(this.bookings, MAX_KEPT);
    h.bookingRef = ref;
    return booking;
  }

  cancel(a: { booking_ref: string }): { booking_ref: string; status: 'cancelled'; refund_paise: number } | { error: string } {
    const b = this.bookings.get(a.booking_ref);
    if (!b) return { error: 'booking_not_found' };
    if (b.status === 'cancelled') return { error: 'already_cancelled' };
    b.status = 'cancelled';
    return { booking_ref: b.booking_ref, status: 'cancelled', refund_paise: Math.round(b.amount_paise * 0.8) };
  }

  pnr(a: { pnr: string }): { pnr: string; status: 'confirmed' | 'cancelled' | 'not_found'; detail: string } {
    const b = this.bookings.get(a.pnr);
    return b ? { pnr: a.pnr, status: b.status, detail: b.title } : { pnr: a.pnr, status: 'not_found', detail: 'No booking with that reference' };
  }
}

// ── the tool table ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const Day = z.iso.date();
export const FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS = {
  search_flights: z.object({ origin: z.string().min(2).max(60), destination: z.string().min(2).max(60), date: Day, adults: z.int().min(1).max(9).optional() }),
  search_stays: z.object({ location: z.string().min(2).max(60), check_in: Day, check_out: Day, guests: z.int().min(1).max(30).optional() }),
  flight_status: z.object({ airline_code: z.string().min(2).max(3), flight_no: z.string().min(1).max(5), date: Day }),
  pnr_status: z.object({ pnr: z.string().min(4).max(20) }),
  hold_booking: z.object({ fare_id: z.string().min(4).max(120), lead_name: z.string().min(1).max(80), travellers: z.int().min(1).max(30) }),
  confirm_booking: z.object({ hold_id: z.string().min(4).max(64), payment_ref: z.string().min(1).max(200) }),
  cancel_booking: z.object({ booking_ref: z.string().min(4).max(20) }),
} as const;
export type FakeIxigoToolName = keyof typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS;

export interface FakeIxigoTool { name: FakeIxigoToolName; description: string; annotations: { readOnlyHint?: boolean; destructiveHint?: boolean } }

export const FAKE_IXIGO_TOOLS: FakeIxigoTool[] = [
  { name: 'search_flights', description: 'Flights between two cities or airports on a date, with fares in paise. Read-only.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: true } },
  { name: 'search_stays', description: 'Villas and hotels in a place for check-in and check-out dates, with nightly and whole-stay prices in paise. Read-only.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: true } },
  { name: 'flight_status', description: 'Live status of a flight on a date: on schedule or late, with the expected departure. Read-only.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: true } },
  { name: 'pnr_status', description: 'The status of a booking by its reference. Read-only.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: true } },
  { name: 'hold_booking', description: 'Holds a fare for 15 minutes and says the amount to pay. Nothing is booked until confirm_booking.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false } },
  { name: 'confirm_booking', description: 'Confirms a held fare once it is paid, and returns the booking.', annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false } },
  { name: 'cancel_booking', description: 'Cancels a booking and refunds 80 percent.', annotations: { destructiveHint: true } },
];

function runTool(store: FakeIxigoStore, name: FakeIxigoToolName, a: Record<string, unknown>): unknown {
  switch (name) {
    case 'search_flights': return searchFlights(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.search_flights>);
    case 'search_stays': return searchStays(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.search_stays>);
    case 'flight_status': return store.flightStatus(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.flight_status>);
    case 'pnr_status': return store.pnr(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.pnr_status>);
    case 'hold_booking': return store.hold(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.hold_booking>);
    case 'confirm_booking': return store.confirm(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.confirm_booking>);
    case 'cancel_booking': return store.cancel(a as z.infer<typeof FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS.cancel_booking>);
  }
}

/** Runs one fake ixigo tool. A refusal is `{ error }` with isError, never a throw. */
export function callFakeIxigo(store: FakeIxigoStore, name: string, args: unknown): { value: unknown; isError: boolean } {
  if (!Object.hasOwn(FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS, name)) return { value: { error: 'unknown_tool' }, isError: true };
  const tool = name as FakeIxigoToolName;
  const parsed = FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS[tool].safeParse(args ?? {});
  if (!parsed.success) return { value: { error: 'invalid_arguments', detail: z.prettifyError(parsed.error) }, isError: true };
  const value = runTool(store, tool, parsed.data as Record<string, unknown>);
  const isError = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && 'error' in value;
  return { value, isError };
}
```

### Task 3: The fake ixigo as an MCP server

The same tool table, served over streamable HTTP with the MCP SDK, one fresh server per request (no session to leak), behind a bearer. Go's IxigoTravel is tested against it, and the demo can run it as its own process.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/server.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FakeIxigoStore`, `FAKE_IXIGO_ARGS`, `FAKE_IXIGO_TOOLS`, `callFakeIxigo` (Task 2).
- Produces: `buildFakeIxigoServer(store): McpServer`, `registerFakeIxigoRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, o: { store: FakeIxigoStore; token?: string }): Promise<void>` (routes `POST /mcp`, `GET /mcp` 405, `DELETE /mcp` 405, `GET /health`).
