### Task 6: The travel layer (constraints in code, a per-query cache, the switch)

`brain.travel` is this layer. It picks the backend per call from the `TRAVEL_MODE` switch, caches a search for 3 minutes (the cache holds the backend's answer; constraints are applied on every call, so "after 6 pm" on a cached search costs nothing), and routes a hold or a confirm by the offer's prefix. `offer(optionId)` answers in master §6.3's exact shape from the stored offers: a stay's `priceMinor` is the whole stay, which is what a vote and a payment use.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/filters.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/layer.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/filters.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/layer.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Switches` (P1); `TravelBackend`, `Travel`, `OfferRef`, `TravelQuery`, `TravelKind`, `TravelOption`, `TravelSearch` (Task 2); `TravelError`, `createFakeTravel` (Task 4); `localClock` (Task 5); `ResultsStore['offer']` (Task 5).
- Produces: `applyConstraints(kind, q, options): TravelOption[]`; `createTravelLayer(d: TravelLayerDeps): Travel`, `SEARCH_CACHE_MS`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/layer.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FakeClock, FakeSwitches } from '../tasks/testing/lane-brain.js';
import { createTravelLayer } from './layer.js';
import type { TravelBackend, TravelOption } from './travel.js';

const opt = (offerId: string, clock: string): TravelOption => ({
  offerId, kind: 'flight', title: offerId, priceMinor: 500000, currency: 'INR', priceNote: 'per person', highlights: [], facts: {},
  journey: { mode: 'flight', origin: { name: 'Delhi' }, destination: { name: 'Goa' }, depart_at: `2026-11-06T${clock}:00+05:30`, arrive_at: `2026-11-06T23:00:00+05:30` },
});

function backend(name: 'fake' | 'ixigo', clock: FakeClock) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const b: TravelBackend = {
    async search(kind, q) {
      calls.push(`${kind} ${JSON.stringify(q)}`);
      return { checkedAt: clock.now().toISOString(), results: [opt(`${name}:a`, '17:50'), opt(`${name}:b`, '18:10')] };
    },
    async status() {
      return { kind: 'pnr', state: 'confirmed', detail: name, checkedAt: clock.now().toISOString() };
    },
    async hold() {
      calls.push('hold');
      return { holdId: `${name}:h1`, amountMinor: 100, currency: 'INR', merchant: name, expiresAt: clock.now().toISOString(), payment: { kind: 'fake' } };
    },
    async confirm() {
      calls.push('confirm');
      return { ref: `${name}-ref`, vendor: name, title: 't', kind: 'flight' };
    },
  };
  return { b, calls };
}

function setup(withIxigo = true) {
  const clock = new FakeClock(Date.parse('2026-10-07T12:00:00Z'));
  const switches = new FakeSwitches();
  const fake = backend('fake', clock);
  const ixigo = backend('ixigo', clock);
  const delays: string[] = [];
  const layer = createTravelLayer({
    switches, now: clock.now, fake: fake.b, ixigo: withIxigo ? ixigo.b : null,
    results: {
      offer: async (id) => {
        const base = { tripId: 't_goa', cardId: 'c', resultsId: 'r', checkedAt: 'x' };
        if (id === 'opt_1') return { ...base, optionId: 'opt_1', n: 1, offerId: 'fake:a', kind: 'flight', title: 'a', priceMinor: 1, totalMinor: null, currency: 'INR', priceNote: 'per person' };
        if (id === 'opt_3') return { ...base, optionId: 'opt_3', n: 3, offerId: 'fake:st', kind: 'stay', title: 'Villa Sol', priceMinor: 1280000, totalMinor: 3840000, currency: 'INR', priceNote: 'per night' };
        if (id === 'opt_web') return { ...base, optionId: 'opt_web', n: 1, offerId: null, kind: 'stay', title: 'Casa Web', priceMinor: null, totalMinor: null, currency: null, priceNote: null };
        return null;
      },
    },
    simulate: { setFlightDelay: (c, n, d, m) => { delays.push(`${c}${n}@${d}+${m}`); } },
  });
  return { clock, switches, fake, ixigo, layer, delays };
}

describe('the travel layer', () => {
  it('caches a search for three minutes and applies constraints to the cached answer', async () => {
    const { clock, fake, layer } = setup();
    const all = await layer.search('flights', { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' });
    const late = await layer.search('flights', { from: 'delhi ', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06', departAfter: '18:00' });
    expect(all.results).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(late.results.map((o) => o.offerId)).toEqual(['fake:b']);
    expect(late.checkedAt).toBe(all.checkedAt);
    expect(fake.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    clock.advance(181_000);
    const fresh = await layer.search('flights', { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' });
    expect(fake.calls).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(fresh.checkedAt).not.toBe(all.checkedAt);
  });

  it('picks the backend on every call from TRAVEL_MODE, and stays on the fake when ixigo is not set up', async () => {
    const a = setup();
    await a.switches.set('TRAVEL_MODE', 'ixigo');
    expect((await a.layer.search('flights', { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' })).results[0]!.offerId).toBe('ixigo:a');
    await a.switches.set('TRAVEL_MODE', 'fake');
    expect((await a.layer.search('flights', { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' })).results[0]!.offerId).toBe('fake:a');
    const b = setup(false);
    await b.switches.set('TRAVEL_MODE', 'ixigo');
    expect((await b.layer.search('flights', { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' })).results[0]!.offerId).toBe('fake:a');
  });

  it('routes a hold and a confirm to the backend that made the offer, whatever the switch says now', async () => {
    const { layer, ixigo, switches } = setup();
    await switches.set('TRAVEL_MODE', 'fake');
    expect((await layer.hold('ixigo:a', { name: 'Rahul', personIds: ['p_rahul'] })).holdId).toBe('ixigo:h1');
    expect((await layer.confirm('ixigo:h1', { approvalId: 'ap_1', railRef: 'r' })).ref).toBe('ixigo-ref');
    expect(ixigo.calls).toEqual(['hold', 'confirm']);
    await expect(layer.hold('other:a', { name: 'Rahul', personIds: ['p_rahul'] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'unknown_offer' });
  });

  it("answers offer() from the stored offers in the master's shape, a stay at its whole-stay price, and passes the simulate switch through", async () => {
    const { layer, delays } = setup();
    expect(await layer.offer('opt_1')).toMatchObject({ offerId: 'fake:a', title: 'a', priceMinor: 1, currency: 'INR', link: null });
    expect(await layer.offer('opt_3')).toEqual({ offerId: 'fake:st', title: 'Villa Sol', priceMinor: 3840000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null });
    expect(await layer.offer('opt_web')).toMatchObject({ offerId: null, title: 'Casa Web', priceMinor: null, currency: null, link: null });
    expect(await layer.offer('opt_2')).toBeNull();
    layer.simulate?.setFlightDelay('6E', '2134', '2026-11-06', 120);
    expect(delays).toEqual(['6E2134@2026-11-06+120']);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/filters.ts
/**
 * Constraints people gave, applied in code to what the backend found. The model passes them as arguments; it is never
 * trusted to drop an option itself. Journeys sort by departure, stays by rating then price.
 */
import { localClock } from './cards.js';
import type { TravelKind, TravelOption, TravelQuery } from './travel.js';

/** "Near the beach" means this many minutes or less. */
const BEACH_NEAR_MINUTES = 10;

type MustHave = NonNullable<TravelQuery['mustHave']>[number];

function has(o: TravelOption, need: MustHave): boolean {
  switch (need) {
    case 'pool':
      return o.facts.pool === true;
    case 'beach':
      return typeof o.facts.beach_minutes === 'number' && o.facts.beach_minutes <= BEACH_NEAR_MINUTES;
    case 'breakfast':
      return o.facts.breakfast === true;
    case 'free_cancellation':
      return o.refundableUntil !== undefined;
    case 'non_stop':
      return o.journey?.stops === 0;
  }
}

function keeps(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery, o: TravelOption): boolean {
  const j = o.journey;
  if (q.departAfter && (!j || localClock(j.depart_at) < q.departAfter)) return false;
  if (q.departBefore && (!j || localClock(j.depart_at) > q.departBefore)) return false;
  if (q.arriveBefore && (!j || j.arrive_at.slice(0, 10) !== j.depart_at.slice(0, 10) || localClock(j.arrive_at) > q.arriveBefore)) return false;
  if (q.maxPriceMinor !== undefined && o.priceMinor > q.maxPriceMinor) return false;
  if (q.minRating !== undefined && (o.rating ?? 0) < q.minRating) return false;
  if (kind === 'stays' && q.guests !== undefined && typeof o.facts.sleeps === 'number' && o.facts.sleeps < q.guests) return false;
  return (q.mustHave ?? []).every((need) => has(o, need));
}

export function applyConstraints(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery, options: TravelOption[]): TravelOption[] {
  const kept = options.filter((o) => keeps(kind, q, o));
  if (kind === 'stays') return kept.sort((a, b) => (b.rating ?? 0) - (a.rating ?? 0) || a.priceMinor - b.priceMinor);
  return kept.sort((a, b) => (a.journey?.depart_at ?? '').localeCompare(b.journey?.depart_at ?? '') || a.priceMinor - b.priceMinor);
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/layer.ts
/**
 * brain.travel. Picks the backend on every call from the TRAVEL_MODE switch (the fake when ixigo is not set up),
 * caches each search for three minutes with the time it was checked, applies constraints in code on every call,
 * and routes a hold or a confirm to the backend that made the offer.
 */
import type { Switches } from '../admin/switches.js';
import { applyConstraints } from './filters.js';
import { TravelError } from './mcp-travel.js';
import type { ResultsStore } from './results.js';
import { TravelQuery, type OfferRef, type Travel, type TravelBackend, type TravelKind, type TravelOptionKind, type TravelSearch } from './travel.js';

export const SEARCH_CACHE_MS = 180_000;
const CACHE_MAX = 500;

export interface TravelLayerDeps {
  switches: Pick<Switches, 'get'>;
  now: () => Date;
  fake: TravelBackend;
  ixigo: TravelBackend | null;
  results: Pick<ResultsStore, 'offer'>;
  simulate?: NonNullable<Travel['simulate']>;
  cacheMs?: number;
}

/** The part of a query the backend sees; the rest are constraints Go applies itself. */
function backendQuery(q: TravelQuery): TravelQuery {
  return {
    ...(q.from ? { from: q.from } : {}),
    ...(q.to ? { to: q.to } : {}),
    ...(q.date ? { date: q.date } : {}),
    ...(q.area ? { area: q.area } : {}),
    ...(q.checkIn ? { checkIn: q.checkIn } : {}),
    ...(q.checkOut ? { checkOut: q.checkOut } : {}),
    ...(q.guests ? { guests: q.guests } : {}),
  };
}

const keyOf = (q: TravelQuery): string =>
  JSON.stringify(Object.entries(q).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([k, v]) => [k, typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim().toLowerCase() : v]));

export function createTravelLayer(d: TravelLayerDeps): Travel {
  const ttl = d.cacheMs ?? SEARCH_CACHE_MS;
  const cache = new Map<string, { at: number; value: TravelSearch }>();
  const pick = (): { name: 'fake' | 'ixigo'; b: TravelBackend } =>
    d.switches.get('TRAVEL_MODE') === 'ixigo' && d.ixigo ? { name: 'ixigo', b: d.ixigo } : { name: 'fake', b: d.fake };
  const byPrefix = (id: string): TravelBackend => {
    if (id.startsWith('fake:')) return d.fake;
    if (id.startsWith('ixigo:') && d.ixigo) return d.ixigo;
    throw new TravelError('unknown_offer', `No travel backend for ${id}.`);
  };
  return {
    async search(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery) {
      const parsed = TravelQuery.parse(q);
      const { name, b } = pick();
      const base = backendQuery(parsed);
      const key = `${name}|${kind}|${keyOf(base)}`;
      const nowMs = d.now().getTime();
      let hit = cache.get(key);
      if (!hit || nowMs - hit.at > ttl) {
        hit = { at: nowMs, value: await b.search(kind, base) };
        cache.delete(key);
        cache.set(key, hit);
        while (cache.size > CACHE_MAX) {
          const oldest = cache.keys().next().value;
          if (oldest === undefined) break;
          cache.delete(oldest);
        }
      }
      return { checkedAt: hit.value.checkedAt, results: applyConstraints(kind, parsed, hit.value.results) };
    },
    async status(q) {
      return pick().b.status(q);
    },
    async hold(offerId, lead) {
      return byPrefix(offerId).hold(offerId, lead);
    },
    async confirm(holdId, paid) {
      return byPrefix(holdId).confirm(holdId, paid);
    },
    async offer(optionId): Promise<OfferRef | null> {
      const o = await d.results.offer(optionId);
      if (!o) return null;
      // A vote and a payment use the whole stay; the card shows the nightly price.
      // kind lets P5 refuse what Go cannot book for a group yet (flights, trains, buses); link is ixigo's own page for
      // the offer, filled once ixigo's tools are mapped (Task 23), and null until then.
      return { offerId: o.offerId, title: o.title, priceMinor: o.totalMinor ?? o.priceMinor, currency: o.currency, kind: o.kind as TravelOptionKind, link: null };
    },
    ...(d.simulate ? { simulate: d.simulate } : {}),
  };
}
```

### Task 7: The composer's travel tools (search, show options, put to a vote)

Each search tool answers compact results for the model plus a ready card spec, and saves the whole set; `show_options` builds the card from the saved set (so prices on the card are the checked ones and the model never types a price) and puts it in `ctx.cards`, so it rides with the turn's message. `put_to_vote` opens a vote from a card once (a rerun of the same turn opens nothing new and shows the same vote card), with each vote option's `option_ref` set to the option's id (the card's `option_id`, never the travel offer id: P5 turns it into the offer with `brain.travel.offer(option_ref)`, which answers `offerId: null` for an option a job found by browsing). Ids come from `ctx.stableId`: `c_opt`, `go_vote`, `c_vote`.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/tools.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/votes.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/tools.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `QuickTool`, `TurnCtx` with `cards`, `stableId`, `signal` (P1); `ServerLink` (P1); `Travel`, `TravelQuery`, `Clock`, `MUST_HAVES` (Task 2); `stableId` (Task 2); `TravelError` (Task 4); `Images`, `ResultsStore`, `toResultOption`, `buildOptionsCard`, `clip`, `formatInr`, `subtitleFor` (Task 5); `createTravelLayer` (Task 6, tests).
- Produces: `travelTools(d: TravelToolDeps): QuickTool<any>[]` with names `search_flights`, `search_stays`, `show_options`, `put_to_vote` (Task 8b adds `search_trains` and `search_buses`, with `JourneyArgs` and `journeyQuery`); `FlightsArgs`, `StaysArgs`, `flightsQuery`, `staysQuery`, `compactOption`, `ageText`, `OPTION_KIND`; `openVoteFromCard(d, o: { tripId; cardId; key; requestId; voteCardId; question?; closesInHours? }): Promise<VoteOutcome>`, `VoteOutcome`, `VoteCard`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/tools.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import { withSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import { makeLaneBrain, type LaneBrain } from '../tasks/testing/lane-brain.js';
import { FakeIxigoStore } from './fake-ixigo/data.js';
import { createImages } from './images.js';
import { createTravelLayer } from './layer.js';
import { createFakeTravel } from './mcp-travel.js';
import { createResultsStore, type ResultsStore } from './results.js';
import { travelTools } from './tools.js';
import type { Travel } from './travel.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let lb: LaneBrain;
let results: ResultsStore;
let travel: Travel;

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: db.pool });
  results = createResultsStore(db.pool);
  const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: lb.clock.now });
  travel = createTravelLayer({ switches: lb.switches, now: lb.clock.now, fake: createFakeTravel({ store, now: lb.clock.now, payRail: () => 'fake' }), ixigo: null, results });
  for (const t of travelTools({ travel, results, images: createImages({ pool: db.pool, publicUrl: 'https://go.test' }), now: lb.clock.now })) lb.tools.register(t);
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await db.drop();
});

const FLIGHTS = { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06', depart_after: '18:00' };
const STAYS = { area: 'North Goa', check_in: '2026-11-06', check_out: '2026-11-09', guests: 5, must_have: ['pool', 'beach'] };

describe('search tools', () => {
  it("search_flights applies Arjun's 6 pm in code and answers compact, priced, dated results", async () => {
    const out = (await lb.tools.run('search_flights', FLIGHTS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_s1' }))) as any;
    expect(out.count).toBe(4);
    expect(out.checked).toBe('just now');
    expect(out.results[0]).toMatchObject({ n: 1, title: 'IndiGo 6E 2134', when: '6:10 pm to 8:45 pm, non-stop', price: '₹5,920 per person' });
    expect(out.results.map((r: { title: string }) => r.title)).not.toContain('Akasa Air QP 1846');
    expect(out.card).toEqual({ results_id: out.results_id, option_numbers: [1, 2, 3], title: 'Flights, Delhi to Goa' });
    const saved = await results.get('t_goa', out.results_id);
    expect(saved?.options.every((o) => (o.journey?.depart_at.slice(11, 16) ?? '') >= '18:00')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('says how old a cached check is', async () => {
    lb.clock.advance(120_000);
    const out = (await lb.tools.run('search_flights', FLIGHTS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_s2' }))) as any;
    expect(out.checked).toBe('2 min ago');
    lb.clock.advance(-120_000);
  });

  it('search_stays keeps villas with a pool near the beach for everyone, and answers plainly when nothing fits', async () => {
    const out = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', STAYS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_s3' }))) as any;
    expect(out.results.map((r: { title: string }) => r.title)).toEqual(['Casa Mareia', 'Villa Sol', 'The Palms Villa', 'Ocean Breeze Villa']);
    expect(out.results[1]).toMatchObject({ price: '₹12,800 per night', total: '₹38,400 for 3 nights' });
    const missing = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', { ...STAYS, max_price_per_night: 5000 }, lb.ctx())) as any;
    expect(missing.count).toBe(0);
    expect(missing.note).toContain('Nothing matched');
  });
});

describe('show_options', () => {
  it('puts a valid options card built from the saved set into ctx.cards, with re-hosted images, so it rides with the message', async () => {
    const found = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', STAYS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_o1' }))) as any;
    const ctx = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_o2' });
    const posts = lb.link.sent('post_message').length;
    const shown = (await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: found.results_id, option_numbers: [2, 3, 4], title: 'Villas with a pool in North Goa' }, ctx)) as any;
    expect(shown.card_id).toBe('c_opt_turn_o2_1');
    expect(ctx.cards).toHaveLength(1);
    const card = C.OptionsCard.parse(ctx.cards[0]);
    expect(card.card_id).toBe(shown.card_id);
    expect(card.options.map((o) => o.title)).toEqual(['Villa Sol', 'The Palms Villa', 'Ocean Breeze Villa']);
    expect(card.options[0]).toMatchObject({ price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', subtitle: 'Anjuna, ₹38,400 for 3 nights' });
    expect(card.options[0]!.image_url).toMatch(/^https:\/\/go\.test\/img\/u[0-9a-f]{32}\.jpg$/);
    expect(lb.link.sent('post_message')).toHaveLength(posts);
    expect(await travel.offer(card.options[0]!.option_id)).toEqual({ offerId: 'fake:st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5', title: 'Villa Sol', priceMinor: 3840000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null });
    expect(await results.offer(card.options[0]!.option_id)).toMatchObject({ n: 1, totalMinor: 3840000, priceMinor: 1280000 });
  });

  it('gives the same card when the same turn runs again, and keeps one set of offers', async () => {
    const found = (await lb.tools.run('search_flights', FLIGHTS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_r1' }))) as any;
    const args = { results_id: found.results_id, title: 'Friday flights, Delhi to Goa' };
    const first = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_r2' });
    const again = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_r2' });
    await lb.tools.run('show_options', args, first);
    await lb.tools.run('show_options', args, again);
    expect(again.cards).toEqual(first.cards);
    expect(await results.offersOnCard('c_opt_turn_r2_1')).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it("refuses results it does not have, or another trip's", async () => {
    const other = await results.save({ tripId: 't_other', optionKind: 'flight', source: 'search', query: {}, options: [], checkedAt: '2026-10-07T12:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: other.resultsId, title: 'x' }, lb.ctx())).toMatchObject({ error: 'unknown_results' });
    expect(await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: 'r_nope', title: 'x' }, lb.ctx())).toMatchObject({ error: 'unknown_results' });
  });
});

describe('put_to_vote', () => {
  it('opens one vote with option_ref set to each option id and the whole-stay price, and puts the vote card in ctx.cards', async () => {
    const found = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', STAYS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_v1' }))) as any;
    const showCtx = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_v2' });
    const shown = (await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: found.results_id, title: 'Villas' }, showCtx)) as any;
    const card = C.OptionsCard.parse(showCtx.cards[0]);
    const ctx = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_v3' });
    const out = (await lb.tools.run('put_to_vote', { card_id: shown.card_id, closes_in_hours: 2 }, ctx)) as any;
    const vote = lb.link.sent('open_vote').at(-1)!;
    expect(vote).toMatchObject({ request_id: 'go_vote_turn_v3_1', question: 'Where do we stay?', kind: 'choice', anonymous: false, closes_at: '2026-10-07T14:00:00.000Z' });
    expect((vote.options as Array<{ option_ref: string }>).map((o) => o.option_ref)).toEqual(card.options.map((o) => o.option_id));
    expect((vote.options as Array<{ price_minor: number }>)[0]!.price_minor).toBe(card.options[0]!.price_minor! * 3);
    expect(ctx.cards).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'vote', card_id: 'c_vote_turn_v3_1', decision_id: out.decision_id })]);
    expect((ctx.cards[0] as { fallback_text: string }).fallback_text).toMatch(/^Vote: Where do we stay\?/);
  });

  it('shows the same vote again when the same turn runs again, and refuses a second vote from another turn', async () => {
    const found = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', STAYS, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_w1' }))) as any;
    const shown = (await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: found.results_id, title: 'Villas' }, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_w2' }))) as any;
    const first = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_w3' });
    const a = (await lb.tools.run('put_to_vote', { card_id: shown.card_id }, first)) as any;
    const opened = lb.link.sent('open_vote').length;
    const rerun = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_w3' });
    const b = (await lb.tools.run('put_to_vote', { card_id: shown.card_id }, rerun)) as any;
    expect(b.decision_id).toBe(a.decision_id);
    expect(rerun.cards).toEqual(first.cards);
    expect(lb.link.sent('open_vote')).toHaveLength(opened);
    expect(await lb.tools.run('put_to_vote', { card_id: shown.card_id }, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_w4' }))).toMatchObject({ error: 'already_voting', decision_id: a.decision_id });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/votes.ts
/**
 * Opening a vote from an options card, once per card: a claim on the card's row (a turn id or a tap id) makes a second
 * tap a no-op, and the same key again (a rerun of the same turn) gets the same vote back. Each vote option's option_ref
 * is the option's id, so P5 finds the offer with brain.travel.offer(option_ref). A stay is put to the vote at its
 * whole-stay price, which is what gets paid. The caller shows the vote card: a turn puts it in ctx.cards, a tap posts it.
 */
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import { clip, formatInr } from './cards.js';
import type { OptionKind, ResultsStore } from './results.js';
import type { StoredOffer } from './travel.js';

export type VoteCard = Extract<C.Card, { kind: 'vote' }>;
export type VoteOutcome =
  | { ok: true; decisionId: string; voteCard: VoteCard }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'unknown_card' | 'already_voting' | 'too_few_options'; decisionId?: string };

const QUESTIONS: Record<OptionKind, string> = {
  stay: 'Where do we stay?', flight: 'Which flight?', train: 'Which train?', bus: 'Which bus?', place: 'Where do we go?', food: 'Where do we eat?', activity: 'What do we do?',
};

interface VoteOption { label: string; image_url?: string; price_minor?: number; currency?: string; option_ref: string; note?: string }

function voteOptions(offers: StoredOffer[], shown: Map<string, { image_url?: string | null | undefined; subtitle?: string | null | undefined }>): VoteOption[] {
  return offers.slice(0, 10).map((of) => {
    const c = shown.get(of.optionId);
    const price = of.totalMinor ?? of.priceMinor;
    return {
      label: clip(of.title, 120),
      ...(c?.image_url ? { image_url: c.image_url } : {}),
      ...(price !== null ? { price_minor: price, currency: of.currency ?? 'INR' } : {}),
      option_ref: of.optionId,
      ...(c?.subtitle ? { note: clip(c.subtitle, 120) } : {}),
    };
  });
}

function voteCardFor(id: string, decisionId: string, question: string, options: VoteOption[]): VoteCard {
  const listed = options.map((op) => (op.price_minor !== undefined ? `${op.label} ${formatInr(op.price_minor)}` : op.label)).join(', ');
  return { card_id: id, kind: 'vote', decision_id: decisionId, fallback_text: clip(`Vote: ${question} ${listed}. Open Go to vote.`, 300) };
}

export async function openVoteFromCard(
  d: { results: ResultsStore; link: Pick<ServerLink, 'request'>; now: () => Date },
  o: { tripId: string; cardId: string; key: string; requestId: string; voteCardId: string; question?: string; closesInHours?: number },
): Promise<VoteOutcome> {
  const row = await d.results.card(o.tripId, o.cardId);
  if (!row) return { ok: false, reason: 'unknown_card' };
  const offers = await d.results.offersOnCard(o.cardId);
  const shown = new Map(row.card.options.map((op) => [op.option_id, op]));
  const options = voteOptions(offers, shown);
  const question = clip(o.question ?? QUESTIONS[row.optionKind], 200);
  if (row.decisionId && !row.decisionId.startsWith('pending:')) {
    // The same key again is a rerun of the turn that opened it: show the same vote, open nothing new.
    if (row.voteKey === o.key) return { ok: true, decisionId: row.decisionId, voteCard: voteCardFor(o.voteCardId, row.decisionId, question, options) };
    return { ok: false, reason: 'already_voting', decisionId: row.decisionId };
  }
  if (offers.length < 2) return { ok: false, reason: 'too_few_options' };
  if (!(await d.results.claimVote(o.cardId, o.key))) return { ok: false, reason: 'already_voting' };
  try {
    const closesAt = new Date(d.now().getTime() + (o.closesInHours ?? 3) * 3_600_000).toISOString();
    const opened = await d.link.request('open_vote', {
      trip_id: o.tripId, request_id: o.requestId, question, kind: 'choice', anonymous: false, options, closes_at: closesAt,
    });
    await d.results.setDecision(o.cardId, opened.decision_id);
    return { ok: true, decisionId: opened.decision_id, voteCard: voteCardFor(o.voteCardId, opened.decision_id, question, options) };
  } catch (err) {
    await d.results.releaseVote(o.cardId, o.key);
    throw err;
  }
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/tools.ts
/**
 * The composer's travel tools. Searches answer compact results for the model and save the full set, so show_options
 * builds the card from what was checked, never from prices the model typed. Constraints people gave ("can't leave
 * before 6") arrive as arguments and are applied in code by the travel layer.
 */
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { QuickTool, TurnCtx } from '../composer/tools.js';
import { buildOptionsCard, clip, formatInr, subtitleFor } from './cards.js';
import { stableId } from './ids.js';
import type { Images } from './images.js';
import { TravelError } from './mcp-travel.js';
import { toResultOption, type OptionKind, type ResultOption, type ResultsStore } from './results.js';
import { Clock, MUST_HAVES, type Travel, type TravelKind, type TravelQuery } from './travel.js';
import { openVoteFromCard } from './votes.js';

export interface TravelToolDeps { travel: Travel; results: ResultsStore; images: Images; now: () => Date }

export const OPTION_KIND: Record<TravelKind, OptionKind> = { flights: 'flight', trains: 'train', buses: 'bus', stays: 'stay' };

const Where = z.string().min(2).max(60);
const Rupees = z.number().positive().max(10_000_000);
const toPaise = (rupees: number): number => Math.round(rupees * 100);

export const FlightsArgs = z.object({
  from: Where.describe('City or airport code, e.g. "Delhi" or "DEL"'),
  to: Where.describe('City or airport code, e.g. "Goa"'),
  date: z.iso.date().describe('YYYY-MM-DD'),
  depart_after: Clock.optional().describe('24-hour HH:MM. Someone who can only leave after 6 pm is "18:00".'),
  depart_before: Clock.optional(),
  arrive_before: Clock.optional(),
  max_price_per_person: Rupees.optional().describe('In rupees'),
  non_stop: z.boolean().optional(),
  travellers: z.int().min(1).max(9).optional(),
});
export const StaysArgs = z.object({
  area: Where.describe('A place or a part of Goa, e.g. "North Goa" or "Anjuna"'),
  check_in: z.iso.date(),
  check_out: z.iso.date(),
  guests: z.int().min(1).max(30).optional(),
  max_price_per_night: Rupees.optional().describe('In rupees, for the whole place per night'),
  must_have: z.array(z.enum(MUST_HAVES)).max(5).optional().describe('pool, beach (10 minutes or less), breakfast, free_cancellation'),
  min_rating: z.number().min(0).max(5).optional(),
});

export function flightsQuery(a: z.infer<typeof FlightsArgs>): TravelQuery {
  return {
    from: a.from,
    to: a.to,
    date: a.date,
    ...(a.depart_after ? { departAfter: a.depart_after } : {}),
    ...(a.depart_before ? { departBefore: a.depart_before } : {}),
    ...(a.arrive_before ? { arriveBefore: a.arrive_before } : {}),
    ...(a.max_price_per_person ? { maxPriceMinor: toPaise(a.max_price_per_person) } : {}),
    ...(a.non_stop ? { mustHave: ['non_stop' as const] } : {}),
    ...(a.travellers ? { guests: a.travellers } : {}),
  };
}

export function staysQuery(a: z.infer<typeof StaysArgs>): TravelQuery {
  return {
    area: a.area,
    checkIn: a.check_in,
    checkOut: a.check_out,
    ...(a.guests ? { guests: a.guests } : {}),
    ...(a.max_price_per_night ? { maxPriceMinor: toPaise(a.max_price_per_night) } : {}),
    ...(a.must_have ? { mustHave: a.must_have } : {}),
    ...(a.min_rating !== undefined ? { minRating: a.min_rating } : {}),
  };
}

export function ageText(checkedAt: string, now: Date): string {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, (now.getTime() - Date.parse(checkedAt)) / 1000);
  if (seconds < 60) return 'just now';
  const minutes = Math.round(seconds / 60);
  return minutes < 60 ? `${minutes} min ago` : `${Math.round(minutes / 60)} h ago`;
}

export function compactOption(n: number, o: ResultOption): Record<string, unknown> {
  const when = o.journey ? subtitleFor(o) : null;
  const nights = typeof o.facts.nights === 'number' ? o.facts.nights : null;
  return {
    n,
    title: o.title,
    ...(when ? { when } : {}),
    ...(typeof o.facts.area === 'string' ? { area: o.facts.area } : {}),
    ...(o.priceMinor !== undefined ? { price: `${formatInr(o.priceMinor)}${o.priceNote ? ` ${o.priceNote}` : ''}` } : {}),
    ...(o.totalMinor !== undefined && nights ? { total: `${formatInr(o.totalMinor)} for ${nights} nights` } : {}),
    ...(o.rating !== undefined ? { rating: o.rating } : {}),
    highlights: o.highlights,
  };
}

function suggestTitle(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery): string {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'flights':
      return clip(`Flights, ${q.from ?? ''} to ${q.to ?? ''}`, 80);
    case 'trains':
      return clip(`Trains, ${q.from ?? ''} to ${q.to ?? ''}`, 80);
    case 'buses':
      return clip(`Buses, ${q.from ?? ''} to ${q.to ?? ''}`, 80);
    case 'stays':
      return clip(`Stays in ${q.area ?? q.to ?? 'Goa'}`, 80);
  }
}

async function runSearch(kind: TravelKind, q: TravelQuery, ctx: TurnCtx, d: TravelToolDeps): Promise<unknown> {
  let found;
  try {
    found = await d.travel.search(kind, q);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof TravelError) return { count: 0, error: err.code, note: clip(err.message, 200) };
    throw err;
  }
  const options = found.results.slice(0, 10).map(toResultOption);
  if (options.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, checked: ageText(found.checkedAt, d.now()), note: 'Nothing matched. Say so in one line and suggest loosening one thing.' };
  }
  const set = await d.results.save({ tripId: ctx.tripId, optionKind: OPTION_KIND[kind], source: 'search', query: q, options, checkedAt: found.checkedAt });
  return {
    results_id: set.resultsId,
    checked: ageText(found.checkedAt, d.now()),
    count: options.length,
    results: options.map((o, i) => compactOption(i + 1, o)),
    card: { results_id: set.resultsId, option_numbers: options.slice(0, 3).map((_, i) => i + 1), title: suggestTitle(kind, q) },
    note: 'To show options, call show_options with this results_id. Quote prices only from these results, and say how long ago they were checked when it is not just now.',
  };
}

const SEARCH_RULE = 'Put every constraint people gave into the arguments: Go applies them to the results itself.';

const ShowArgs = z.object({
  results_id: z.string().min(3).max(64),
  option_numbers: z.array(z.int().min(1).max(10)).min(1).max(5).optional().describe('From the results, best first. Default: the first three.'),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(80),
});

const VoteArgs = z.object({
  card_id: z.string().min(3).max(64),
  question: z.string().min(3).max(200).optional(),
  closes_in_hours: z.number().min(0.5).max(72).optional(),
});

export function travelTools(d: TravelToolDeps): QuickTool<any>[] {
  const searchFlights: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof FlightsArgs>> = {
    name: 'search_flights',
    description: `Searches flights on one date. ${SEARCH_RULE} For example depart_after "18:00" when someone cannot leave before 6 pm.`,
    input: FlightsArgs,
    run: (a, ctx) => runSearch('flights', flightsQuery(a), ctx, d),
  };
  const searchStays: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof StaysArgs>> = {
    name: 'search_stays',
    description: `Searches villas and hotels for check-in and check-out dates. ${SEARCH_RULE} For a deep comparison (reviews, many sites), use start_task instead.`,
    input: StaysArgs,
    run: (a, ctx) => runSearch('stays', staysQuery(a), ctx, d),
  };
  const showOptions: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof ShowArgs>> = {
    name: 'show_options',
    description: 'Shows an options card from a results_id (a search, or a finished job), with up to 5 option_numbers, best first, and a short title. The card goes out with your message and shows the checked prices, so never type prices yourself. Add one short line about it.',
    input: ShowArgs,
    async run(a, ctx) {
      const set = await d.results.get(ctx.tripId, a.results_id);
      if (!set || set.options.length === 0) return { error: 'unknown_results', note: 'Search again, then show the new results.' };
      const numbers = [...new Set(a.option_numbers ?? set.options.slice(0, 3).map((_, i) => i + 1))].filter((n) => n <= set.options.length).slice(0, 5);
      if (numbers.length === 0) return { error: 'no_such_options', note: `Pick numbers from 1 to ${set.options.length}.` };
      const cardId = ctx.stableId('c_opt');
      const picks = await Promise.all(
        numbers.map(async (n, i) => {
          const option = set.options[n - 1]!;
          return { n: i + 1, optionId: stableId('opt', cardId, i + 1), option, imageUrl: option.imageUrl ? await d.images.rehostUrl(option.imageUrl) : null };
        }),
      );
      const card = buildOptionsCard({ cardId, title: a.title, optionKind: set.optionKind, checkedAt: set.checkedAt, picks });
      // Saved before it is shown, so a tap on it always finds its offers.
      await d.results.saveCard({ cardId, tripId: ctx.tripId, resultsId: set.resultsId, title: card.title, optionKind: set.optionKind, card }, picks, set.checkedAt);
      ctx.cards.push(card);
      return { card_id: cardId, shown: picks.length, checked: ageText(set.checkedAt, d.now()), note: 'The card goes out with your message. Add one short line; quote prices only from the card.' };
    },
  };
  const putToVote: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof VoteArgs>> = {
    name: 'put_to_vote',
    description: 'Opens a vote on the options of an options card Go showed (card_id). The vote card goes out with your message. Use it when the group should decide together.',
    input: VoteArgs,
    async run(a, ctx) {
      const r = await openVoteFromCard(
        { results: d.results, link: ctx.link, now: d.now },
        {
          tripId: ctx.tripId,
          cardId: a.card_id,
          key: ctx.turnId,
          requestId: ctx.stableId('go_vote'),
          voteCardId: ctx.stableId('c_vote'),
          ...(a.question ? { question: a.question } : {}),
          ...(a.closes_in_hours ? { closesInHours: a.closes_in_hours } : {}),
        },
      );
      if (!r.ok) return { error: r.reason, ...(r.decisionId ? { decision_id: r.decisionId } : {}) };
      ctx.cards.push(r.voteCard);
      return { decision_id: r.decisionId, note: 'The vote card goes out with your message. Add one short line.' };
    },
  };
  // Flights and stays first; the stretch Task 8b adds search_trains and search_buses after search_flights.
  return [searchFlights, searchStays, showOptions, putToVote];
}
```

### Task 8: Options taps and registerTravel

"Put it to a vote" on an options card opens the vote in code (fast, once, even with two taps at the same moment); "I'll take this" puts a `tap` item on the desk so the composer answers in the room. `registerTravel` wires the lane at boot and sets `brain.travel`.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/taps.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/register.ts`
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/travel/env.ts` (P1's stub, taken over: `TravelEnv` keeps its name and fields; `readTravelEnv` is added; `env.ts` is not touched, it already merges `TravelEnv`)
- Modify: `apps/brain/.env.example` (P1's file: the three travel lines, each with what it is for)
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/main.ts` (`registerTravel` joins `LANES` in the master's order: after `registerStats` when it is there, before `registerTasks`)
- Test: `apps/brain/src/travel/register.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Brain` (with `onStop`), `RegisterLane` (P1); `TapRouter`, `Desk`, `Context`, `Tools`, `Switches` through `Brain`; `createHttpMcpCaller` (Task 4); everything from Tasks 2 to 7.
- Produces: `registerTravel: RegisterLane` (sets `brain.travel`, registers four quick tools (six once Task 8b lands), taps `options/pick` and `options/vote`, the wake renderer for `tap` that answers null unless `payload.source === 'options'`, and `GET /img/:file`; with `IXIGO_MCP_URL` set, one `brain.onStop` stopper that closes the ixigo MCP connection); `registerTravelTaps(taps, d)`; `renderOptionsTap(item, pack): string | null`; `TravelEnv` (P1's name), `readTravelEnv(env)`, `TravelRuntime`. Desk ids: `travel:pick:<tap_id>`, `travel:vote_failed:<tap_id>`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/register.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import { withSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import { eventually, makeLaneBrain, type LaneBrain } from '../tasks/testing/lane-brain.js';
import { goaPack } from '../tasks/testing/goa.js';
import { registerTravel } from './register.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let lb: LaneBrain;
let card: C.Card;

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: db.pool });
  await registerTravel(lb.brain);
  const found = (await lb.tools.run('search_stays', { area: 'North Goa', check_in: '2026-11-06', check_out: '2026-11-09', guests: 5 }, lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_reg1' }))) as any;
  const ctx = lb.ctx({ turnId: 'turn_reg2' });
  await lb.tools.run('show_options', { results_id: found.results_id, title: 'Villas in North Goa' }, ctx);
  card = C.Card.parse(ctx.cards[0]);
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await db.drop();
});

const tap = (over: Partial<{ tap_id: string; action_id: string; person_id: string; option_id: string }>) => ({
  tap_id: 'tap_1', trip_id: 't_goa', card_id: card.card_id, action_id: 'pick', person_id: 'p_meher', ...over,
});

describe('registerTravel', () => {
  it('sets brain.travel and registers the four travel tools, the two options taps, the tap wake and the image route', async () => {
    expect(lb.brain.travel).toBeDefined();
    expect(lb.tools.list().map((t) => t.name)).toEqual(['search_flights', 'search_stays', 'show_options', 'put_to_vote']);
    expect([...lb.taps.handlers.keys()].sort()).toEqual(['options/pick', 'options/vote']);
    expect(lb.context.wakes.has('tap')).toBe(true);
    expect((await lb.http.inject({ method: 'GET', url: `/img/u${'0'.repeat(32)}.jpg` })).statusCode).toBe(404);
    expect(lb.brain.travel?.simulate).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('"I\'ll take this" puts a tap on the desk for the composer, and the wake line names the person and the option', async () => {
    if (card.kind !== 'options') throw new Error('expected an options card');
    const option = card.options[1]!;
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('options', 'pick', tap({ tap_id: 'tap_p1', option_id: option.option_id }), card)).toEqual({ result: 'done', toast: 'Noted. Go will reply in the room.' });
    const item = lb.desk.of('tap').at(-1)!;
    expect(item).toMatchObject({ id: 'travel:pick:tap_p1', ref: card.card_id, payload: { source: 'options', action: 'pick', optionId: option.option_id, personId: 'p_meher' } });
    const line = lb.context.wake(item, goaPack());
    expect(line).toContain('Meher');
    expect(line).toContain(option.title);
    // A tap from another card is not P4's to read: null, so P5's or P6's renderer (or P1's generic line) answers.
    expect(lb.context.wake({ ...item, payload: { source: 'pay', action: 'approve' } }, goaPack())).toBeNull();
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('options', 'pick', tap({ tap_id: 'tap_p2', option_id: 'opt_gone' }), card)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That option is no longer available' });
  });

  it('"Put it to a vote" opens one vote and posts its card, even when two people tap at the same moment', async () => {
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([
      lb.taps.tap('options', 'vote', tap({ tap_id: 'tap_v1', action_id: 'vote' }), card),
      lb.taps.tap('options', 'vote', tap({ tap_id: 'tap_v2', action_id: 'vote', person_id: 'p_kabir' }), card),
    ]);
    expect(a.result).toBe('done');
    expect(b.result).toBe('done');
    await eventually(() => lb.link.sent('post_message').some((p) => (p.card as { kind: string }).kind === 'vote'));
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
    expect(lb.link.sent('open_vote')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('options', 'vote', tap({ tap_id: 'tap_v3', action_id: 'vote' }), card)).toEqual({ result: 'done', toast: 'Already up for a vote' });
  });

  it('closes the ixigo connection through brain.onStop, and has nothing to stop with the fake alone', async () => {
    expect(lb.stoppers).toHaveLength(0);
    const withIxigo = makeLaneBrain({ pool: db.pool, env: { IXIGO_MCP_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/mcp', IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN: 'tok-ixigo-123456' } });
    await registerTravel(withIxigo.brain);
    expect(withIxigo.stoppers).toHaveLength(1);
    // The client connects on its first call, so closing one that never called resolves at once.
    await withIxigo.stop();
    expect(withIxigo.stoppers).toEqual([]);
    await withIxigo.http.close();
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/env.ts
import { z } from 'zod';

/**
 * Lane C (P4): the travel backend. P4 took this file over from P1's stub; P1's env.ts already merges this object's shape,
 * so it is never edited for a new variable. Every value is optional, and each one is also in apps/brain/.env.example.
 */
export const TravelEnv = z.object({
  /** Which backend answers travel searches. The TRAVEL_MODE switch can change it at runtime; it falls back to fake. */
  TRAVEL_MODE: z.enum(['ixigo', 'fake']).optional(),
  IXIGO_MCP_URL: z.url().optional(),
  IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN: z.string().min(1).optional(),
});

const TravelRuntime = TravelEnv.extend({ GO_PUBLIC_URL: z.url() });
export type TravelRuntime = z.infer<typeof TravelRuntime>;

/** What the travel lane reads from the merged env. */
export function readTravelEnv(env: unknown): TravelRuntime {
  return TravelRuntime.parse(env);
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/taps.ts
/**
 * Taps on Go's options cards. "Put it to a vote" opens the vote in code (fast and once); "I'll take this" goes on the
 * desk, so the composer answers the person in the room.
 */
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import type { DeskItem } from '../desk/desk.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../trips/pack.js';
import { clip } from './cards.js';
import { stableId } from './ids.js';
import type { ResultsStore } from './results.js';
import { openVoteFromCard } from './votes.js';

export interface TravelTapDeps {
  results: ResultsStore;
  link: Brain['door']['link'];
  desk: Brain['desk'];
  now: () => Date;
  log: Brain['log'];
}

export function registerTravelTaps(taps: Brain['taps'], d: TravelTapDeps): void {
  taps.on('options', 'vote', async (tap) => {
    const row = await d.results.card(tap.trip_id, tap.card_id);
    if (!row) return { result: 'refused', toast: 'Those options are no longer here' };
    if (row.decisionId) return { result: 'done', toast: 'Already up for a vote' };
    const failed = () =>
      d.desk.put({
        id: `travel:vote_failed:${tap.tap_id}`, tripId: tap.trip_id, kind: 'tap', ref: tap.card_id, dueAt: d.now(),
        payload: { source: 'options', action: 'vote_failed', cardId: tap.card_id, personId: tap.person_id },
      });
    // Outside a turn, ids come from the tap, so a repeat does each write once.
    const opening = async (): Promise<void> => {
      const r = await openVoteFromCard(
        { results: d.results, link: d.link, now: d.now },
        { tripId: tap.trip_id, cardId: tap.card_id, key: tap.tap_id, requestId: stableId('go_tap', tap.tap_id, 'vote'), voteCardId: stableId('c_vote', tap.tap_id) },
      );
      if (r.ok) {
        await d.link.request('post_message', { trip_id: tap.trip_id, request_id: stableId('go_tap', tap.tap_id, 'vote_card'), card: r.voteCard });
        return;
      }
      if (r.reason !== 'already_voting') await failed();
    };
    // The vote opens after the answer: two requests to the Server must not hold the 3 s tap answer.
    void opening()
      .catch(async (err: unknown) => {
        d.log.error({ err, cardId: tap.card_id }, 'travel: opening a vote from a tap failed');
        await failed();
      });
    return { result: 'done', toast: 'Putting it to a vote' };
  });

  taps.on('options', 'pick', async (tap) => {
    const offer = tap.option_id ? await d.results.offer(tap.option_id) : null;
    if (!offer || offer.cardId !== tap.card_id || offer.tripId !== tap.trip_id) return { result: 'refused', toast: 'That option is no longer available' };
    await d.desk.put({
      id: `travel:pick:${tap.tap_id}`, tripId: tap.trip_id, kind: 'tap', ref: tap.card_id, dueAt: d.now(),
      payload: { source: 'options', action: 'pick', cardId: tap.card_id, optionId: offer.optionId, n: offer.n, title: offer.title, personId: tap.person_id },
    });
    return { result: 'done', toast: 'Noted. Go will reply in the room.' };
  });
}

/** How an options tap reads in "why Go woke". Null for taps from other cards. */
export function renderOptionsTap(item: DeskItem, pack: TripPack): string | null {
  const p = item.payload as { source?: string; action?: string; cardId?: string; optionId?: string; title?: string; personId?: string };
  if (p.source !== 'options') return null;
  const who = pack.trip.people.find((x) => x.person_id === p.personId)?.name ?? 'Someone';
  if (p.action === 'pick') {
    return `${who} tapped "I'll take this" on "${clip(p.title ?? 'an option', 80)}" (option ${p.optionId}, card ${p.cardId}). Reply in the room: confirm it for them, or if the group should agree first, offer put_to_vote with card_id ${p.cardId}.`;
  }
  if (p.action === 'vote_failed') {
    return `${who} tapped "Put it to a vote" on card ${p.cardId}, but opening the vote failed. Try put_to_vote with card_id ${p.cardId}, or say in one line that it did not work.`;
  }
  return null;
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/register.ts
/**
 * Lane C's travel at boot: the fake ixigo and, when configured, ixigo's MCP server behind one layer (brain.travel),
 * the composer's travel tools, the options taps, the tap wake line and Go's image route.
 */
import type { RegisterLane } from '../app.js';
import { readTravelEnv } from './env.js';
import { FakeIxigoStore } from './fake-ixigo/data.js';
import { createImages, registerImageRoute } from './images.js';
import { createTravelLayer } from './layer.js';
import { createHttpMcpCaller } from './mcp-caller.js';
import { createFakeTravel, createIxigoTravel } from './mcp-travel.js';
import { createResultsStore } from './results.js';
import { registerTravelTaps, renderOptionsTap } from './taps.js';
import { travelTools } from './tools.js';

export const registerTravel: RegisterLane = async (brain) => {
  const env = readTravelEnv(brain.env);
  const store = new FakeIxigoStore({ now: brain.now });
  const fake = createFakeTravel({ store, now: brain.now, payRail: () => brain.switches.get('PAY_RAIL') });
  // The caller is made here, not inside createIxigoTravel, so its connection can be closed when Go stops.
  const ixigoCaller = env.IXIGO_MCP_URL
    ? createHttpMcpCaller({ url: env.IXIGO_MCP_URL, name: 'go-ixigo', ...(env.IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN ? { token: env.IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN } : {}) })
    : null;
  const ixigo = env.IXIGO_MCP_URL && ixigoCaller ? createIxigoTravel({ url: env.IXIGO_MCP_URL, now: brain.now, caller: ixigoCaller }) : null;
  if (ixigoCaller) brain.onStop(() => ixigoCaller.close());
  if (!ixigo) brain.log.info('travel: IXIGO_MCP_URL is not set, so searches use the fake ixigo whatever TRAVEL_MODE says');
  const results = createResultsStore(brain.pool);
  const images = createImages({ pool: brain.pool, publicUrl: env.GO_PUBLIC_URL });
  registerImageRoute(brain.http, { pool: brain.pool });
  const travel = createTravelLayer({
    switches: brain.switches,
    now: brain.now,
    fake,
    ixigo,
    results,
    simulate: { setFlightDelay: (carrierCode, number, date, minutes) => store.setDelay(carrierCode, number, date, minutes) },
  });
  for (const t of travelTools({ travel, results, images, now: brain.now })) brain.tools.register(t);
  registerTravelTaps(brain.taps, { results, link: brain.door.link, desk: brain.desk, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });
  // Null for taps on other cards (pay, call): the next lane's renderer, or P1's generic line, reads those.
  brain.context.addWake('tap', renderOptionsTap);
  brain.travel = travel;
};
```

### Task 8b (stretch: build after G3): Trains and buses

Build this only after G3, once Tasks 9 to 20 are merged (master §9 "Stretch": flights and stays first). It also touches Go's MCP server from Task 13. Until it lands, Go searches flights and stays only, and `Travel.search('trains' | 'buses')` answers a plain refusal (Task 4). This task adds the fake ixigo's trains and buses, their mapping, the composer's `search_trains` and `search_buses`, and the same two searches on Go's MCP server, so a job can take a journey's inventory from them as well. Nothing here books anything: booking is P5's, and flights for the whole group are not booked this week (master §9). The deployment's instructions (Task 12) already say "search_trains and search_buses when mcp_search lists them", so `setup-v1` does not need to run again.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.ts` (the train and bus catalog, their fares in `findFare`, two tools)
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/travel/ixigo-map.ts` (the two raw shapes, `CLASS_WORDS`, `trainOption`, `busOption`, `search.trains`, `search.buses`)
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/travel/tools.ts` (`JourneyArgs`, `journeyQuery`, the two quick tools)
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/mcp/server.ts` (the two MCP searches; `GO_MCP_TOOLS`)
- Test (modify): `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/server.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/mcp-caller.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/mcp-travel.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/filters.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/tools.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/travel/register.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/mcp/server.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2 to 8 and Task 13 as they are.
- Produces: `searchTrains`, `searchBuses`, `RawTrain`, `RawBus` (`data.ts`); `DEFAULT_IXIGO_MAP.search.trains` and `.buses`; `JourneyArgs`, `journeyQuery` (`tools.ts`); the quick tools `search_trains` and `search_buses` (in `travelTools`, right after `search_flights`); the MCP tools `search_trains` and `search_buses`; `GO_MCP_TOOLS` with eight names. No shared name changes: master §6.3's `Travel.search` already names all four kinds.

```ts
describe('trains and buses', () => {
  it('gives each class of a train as its own fare, arriving the next day', () => {
    const { trains } = searchTrains({ from_station: 'Delhi', to_station: 'Goa', date: FRIDAY });
    const rajdhani = trains.filter((t) => t.train_no === '22414');
    expect(rajdhani.map((t) => t.class_code).sort()).toEqual(['2A', '3A']);
    expect(rajdhani[0]!.arrive_at.startsWith('2026-11-07T10:20')).toBe(true);
    expect(trains.every((t) => /^(AVL|WL) \d+$/.test(t.availability))).toBe(true);
  });

    expect(searchBuses({ from_city: 'Mumbai', to_city: 'Goa', date: FRIDAY }).buses).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(searchBuses({ from_city: 'Delhi', to_city: 'Goa', date: FRIDAY }).buses).toEqual([]);

  it('finds a train fare and a bus fare by their ids, and holds a train per traveller', () => {

    expect(hold.amount_paise).toBe(824000);
  });
});
```

In `apps/brain/src/travel/fake-ixigo/data.ts`, change the header's first line to ` * The fake ixigo: deterministic flights, trains and buses into Goa, North and South Goa villas, live flight status,` and insert this block right after the `searchFlights` function (before `// ── stays`):
```ts
// ── trains ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const STATIONS: Record<string, string> = { NZM: 'Delhi Hazrat Nizamuddin', MAO: 'Madgaon', THVM: 'Thivim (North Goa)', CSMT: 'Mumbai CSMT' };
const STATION_WORDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  delhi: ['NZM'], 'new delhi': ['NZM'], goa: ['MAO', 'THVM'], madgaon: ['MAO'], margao: ['MAO'], thivim: ['THVM'], 'north goa': ['THVM'], mumbai: ['CSMT'],
};

function stationCodes(place: string): string[] {
  const words = STATION_WORDS[place.trim().toLowerCase()];
  if (words) return words;
  const code = place.trim().toUpperCase();
  return code in STATIONS ? [code] : [];
}

interface Train { from: string; to: string; no: string; name: string; dep: string; arr: string; days: number; fares: Record<string, number> }

const TRAINS: Train[] = [
  { from: 'NZM', to: 'MAO', no: '22414', name: 'Madgaon Rajdhani Express', dep: '10:55', arr: '10:20', days: 1, fares: { '3A': 412000, '2A': 565000 } },
  { from: 'NZM', to: 'MAO', no: '12450', name: 'Goa Sampark Kranti Express', dep: '06:00', arr: '12:40', days: 1, fares: { '3A': 301000, '2A': 425000 } },
  { from: 'NZM', to: 'THVM', no: '12780', name: 'Goa Express', dep: '15:00', arr: '15:45', days: 1, fares: { SL: 105000, '3A': 279000 } },
  { from: 'CSMT', to: 'THVM', no: '12051', name: 'Jan Shatabdi Express', dep: '05:10', arr: '12:45', days: 0, fares: { CC: 77500 } },
  { from: 'CSMT', to: 'THVM', no: '10103', name: 'Mandovi Express', dep: '07:10', arr: '17:30', days: 0, fares: { SL: 41000, '3A': 112000 } },
];

export interface RawTrain {
  fare_id: string; train_no: string; train_name: string; from_code: string; from_name: string; to_code: string; to_name: string;
  depart_at: string; arrive_at: string; duration_min: number; class_code: string; fare_paise: number; availability: string;
}

function rawTrain(t: Train, date: string, cls: string, fare: number): RawTrain {
  const fareId = `tr-${t.from}-${t.to}-${compactDate(date)}-${t.no}-${cls}`;
  const left = seeded(fareId, 30);
  return {
    fare_id: fareId, train_no: t.no, train_name: t.name, from_code: t.from, from_name: STATIONS[t.from]!, to_code: t.to, to_name: STATIONS[t.to]!,
    ...leg(date, t.dep, t.arr, t.days), class_code: cls, fare_paise: fare, availability: left > 3 ? `AVL ${left}` : `WL ${4 - left}`,
  };
}

export function searchTrains(a: { from_station: string; to_station: string; date: string }): { trains: RawTrain[] } {
  const from = stationCodes(a.from_station);
  const to = stationCodes(a.to_station);
  const trains: RawTrain[] = [];
  for (const t of TRAINS.filter((x) => from.includes(x.from) && to.includes(x.to))) {
    for (const [cls, fare] of Object.entries(t.fares)) trains.push(rawTrain(t, a.date, cls, fare));
  }
  return { trains: trains.sort((x, y) => x.depart_at.localeCompare(y.depart_at) || x.fare_paise - y.fare_paise) };
}

// ── buses ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const BUS_CITY_WORDS: Record<string, string> = {
  mumbai: 'Mumbai', bombay: 'Mumbai', goa: 'Goa', panaji: 'Goa', mapusa: 'Goa', 'north goa': 'Goa', bangalore: 'Bengaluru', bengaluru: 'Bengaluru', pune: 'Pune',
};

interface Bus { from: string; to: string; operator: string; type: string; dep: string; arr: string; fare: number; rating: number }

const BUSES: Bus[] = [
  { from: 'Mumbai', to: 'Goa', operator: 'Paulo Travels', type: 'Volvo AC Sleeper', dep: '20:30', arr: '07:30', fare: 145000, rating: 4.4 },
  { from: 'Mumbai', to: 'Goa', operator: 'Neeta Tours and Travels', type: 'AC Sleeper', dep: '21:15', arr: '08:45', fare: 125000, rating: 4.1 },
  { from: 'Mumbai', to: 'Goa', operator: 'Kadamba Transport (KTCL)', type: 'Non-AC Seater', dep: '18:00', arr: '06:30', fare: 98000, rating: 3.8 },
  { from: 'Bengaluru', to: 'Goa', operator: 'VRL Travels', type: 'AC Sleeper', dep: '21:00', arr: '08:00', fare: 139000, rating: 4.2 },
  { from: 'Pune', to: 'Goa', operator: 'Atmaram Travels', type: 'AC Sleeper', dep: '22:00', arr: '08:30', fare: 115000, rating: 4.0 },
];

export interface RawBus {
  fare_id: string; operator: string; bus_type: string; from_city: string; to_city: string;
  depart_at: string; arrive_at: string; duration_min: number; fare_paise: number; seats_left: number; rating: number;
}

const slug = (text: string): string => text.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '');
const busKey = (b: Bus): string => `${slug(b.operator)}-${slug(b.from)}-${slug(b.to)}`;

function rawBus(b: Bus, date: string): RawBus {
  const fareId = `bus-${busKey(b)}-${compactDate(date)}`;
  return {
    fare_id: fareId, operator: b.operator, bus_type: b.type, from_city: b.from, to_city: b.to,
    ...leg(date, b.dep, b.arr, b.arr < b.dep ? 1 : 0), fare_paise: b.fare, seats_left: 3 + seeded(fareId, 20), rating: b.rating,
  };
}

export function searchBuses(a: { from_city: string; to_city: string; date: string }): { buses: RawBus[] } {
  const from = BUS_CITY_WORDS[a.from_city.trim().toLowerCase()];
  const to = BUS_CITY_WORDS[a.to_city.trim().toLowerCase()];
  return { buses: BUSES.filter((b) => b.from === from && b.to === to).map((b) => rawBus(b, a.date)).sort((x, y) => x.depart_at.localeCompare(y.depart_at)) };
}
```

### Task 9: Go's /v1 client (the key pool, Retry-After, idempotency, live streams)

Go's own client, not `@vestra/sdk` 0.1.8: the SDK waits out a 429 for up to 5 minutes (a quick tool must answer in seconds), keeps one global clock for its waits (tests here inject one per client), knows nothing of a pool of keys or of streams per key, and is a private tarball fetched from a URL, which makes `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` depend on vestra.ai serving the same bytes. The pieces worth having are copied in Go's words: the webhook check (Task 16) and the SSE frame reader (below).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/sse.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/v1-client.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/sse.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/v1-client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from other lanes.
- Produces: `SseFrame { id: number | null; event: string; data: unknown }`, `parseSseFrames(buffer): { frames; rest }`; `V1Error(status, code, message, retryAfterMs)`, `V1Method`, `V1CallOptions { body?; query?; idempotencyKey?; admin?; signal? }`, `V1Stream { keyIndex; frames: AsyncGenerator<SseFrame>; close() }`, `V1Client { call<T>(method, path, opts?); bytes(path, opts?); stream(path, { lastEventId, signal?, quietMs? }); openStreams(): number[] }`, `createV1Client(o: V1ClientOptions)`, `MAX_STREAMS_PER_KEY`, `STREAM_QUIET_MS`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/sse.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseSseFrames } from './sse.js';

describe('parseSseFrames', () => {
  it('reads whole frames, keeps the unfinished rest, and reads the data as the whole event object', () => {
    const buf = 'id: 3\nevent: task.started\ndata: {"seq":3,"type":"task.started","data":{"task_id":"task_1"}}\n\n: comment\n\nevent: ping\ndata: {"type":"ping"}\n\nid: 4\nevent: task.pro';
    const { frames, rest } = parseSseFrames(buf);
    expect(frames).toEqual([
      { id: 3, event: 'task.started', data: { seq: 3, type: 'task.started', data: { task_id: 'task_1' } } },
      { id: null, event: 'ping', data: { type: 'ping' } },
    ]);
    expect(rest).toBe('id: 4\nevent: task.pro');
  });

  it('reads CRLF line ends and data spread over lines', () => {
    const { frames } = parseSseFrames('id: 9\r\nevent: x\r\ndata: {"a":\r\ndata: 1}\r\n\r\n');
    expect(frames).toEqual([{ id: 9, event: 'x', data: { a: 1 } }]);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/v1-client.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { V1Error, createV1Client } from './v1-client.js';

const K = (c: string) => `vk_test_${c.repeat(43)}`;
const KEYS = [K('a'), K('b'), K('c')];
const ADMIN = K('z');

interface Seen { url: URL; method: string; headers: Record<string, string>; body: unknown }
function fakeFetch(answer: (seen: Seen, n: number) => Response | Promise<Response>) {
  const calls: Seen[] = [];
  const f = (async (input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const seen: Seen = {
      url: new URL(String(input)),
      method: init?.method ?? 'GET',
      headers: (init?.headers ?? {}) as Record<string, string>,
      body: typeof init?.body === 'string' ? JSON.parse(init.body) : undefined,
    };
    calls.push(seen);
    return answer(seen, calls.length);
  }) as typeof fetch;
  return { f, calls };
}
const json = (status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', ...headers } });
const keyOf = (s: Seen) => s.headers.authorization?.replace('Bearer ', '');

function clockAndSleep() {
  const t = { now: 1_000_000, slept: [] as number[] };
  return { t, nowMs: () => t.now, sleep: async (ms: number) => { t.slept.push(ms); t.now += ms; } };
}

describe('requests', () => {
  it('spreads requests round the key pool', async () => {
    const { f, calls } = fakeFetch(() => json(200, { ok: true }));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test/', keys: KEYS, fetch: f });
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) await v1.call('GET', '/v1/conversations/conv_1');
    expect(calls.map(keyOf)).toEqual([K('a'), K('b'), K('c'), K('a')]);
    expect(calls[0]!.url.toString()).toBe('https://api.test/v1/conversations/conv_1');
  });

  it("rests a key for its Retry-After and moves to the next key at once", async () => {
    const c = clockAndSleep();
    const { f, calls } = fakeFetch((_s, n) => (n === 1 ? json(429, { error: { code: 'rate_limited', message: 'slow down' } }, { 'retry-after': '2' }) : json(200, {})));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    await v1.call('GET', '/v1/tasks/task_1');
    await v1.call('GET', '/v1/tasks/task_1');
    await v1.call('GET', '/v1/tasks/task_1');
    expect(calls.map(keyOf)).toEqual([K('a'), K('b'), K('c'), K('b')]);
    expect(c.t.slept).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('waits out the shortest rest when every key rests, and gives up when the wait is too long', async () => {
    const c = clockAndSleep();
    const { f } = fakeFetch((_s, n) => (n <= 2 ? json(429, { error: { code: 'rate_limited' } }, { 'retry-after': '1' }) : json(200, { ok: 1 })));
    const two = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS.slice(0, 2), fetch: f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    expect(await two.call('GET', '/v1/x')).toEqual({ ok: 1 });
    expect(c.t.slept).toEqual([1000]);
    const slow = fakeFetch(() => json(429, { error: { code: 'rate_limited' } }, { 'retry-after': '30' }));
    const one = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS.slice(0, 1), fetch: slow.f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    await expect(one.call('GET', '/v1/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429, code: 'rate_limited' });
  });

  it('retries a write only when it carries an idempotency key, and sends the key', async () => {
    const c = clockAndSleep();
    const keyed = fakeFetch((_s, n) => (n === 1 ? json(503, { error: { code: 'database_unavailable' } }) : json(201, { id: 'conv_1' })));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: keyed.f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    expect(await v1.call('POST', '/v1/conversations', { body: { user_id: 'u' }, idempotencyKey: 'go-conv-u' })).toEqual({ id: 'conv_1' });
    expect(keyed.calls).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(keyed.calls[1]!.headers['idempotency-key']).toBe('go-conv-u');
    expect(keyed.calls[1]!.body).toEqual({ user_id: 'u' });
    const bare = fakeFetch(() => json(503, { error: { code: 'database_unavailable' } }));
    const v2 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: bare.f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    await expect(v2.call('POST', '/v1/sessions', { body: {} })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, code: 'database_unavailable' });
    expect(bare.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('waits and sends again when the conversation is busy', async () => {
    const c = clockAndSleep();
    const { f, calls } = fakeFetch((_s, n) => (n === 1 ? json(409, { error: { code: 'conversation_busy' } }) : json(202, { id: 'turn_1' })));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f, nowMs: c.nowMs, sleep: c.sleep });
    expect(await v1.call('POST', '/v1/conversations/conv_1/messages', { body: {}, idempotencyKey: 'go-msg-1' })).toEqual({ id: 'turn_1' });
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(c.t.slept).toEqual([1000]);
  });

  it('uses the admin key only for admin calls, and refuses one when there is no admin key', async () => {
    const { f, calls } = fakeFetch(() => json(200, {}));
    await createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, adminKey: ADMIN, fetch: f }).call('POST', '/v1/users', { admin: true, body: {} });
    expect(keyOf(calls[0]!)).toBe(ADMIN);
    await expect(createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f }).call('POST', '/v1/users', { admin: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'admin_key_missing' });
  });

  it("turns /v1's error body into a V1Error with its code", async () => {
    const { f } = fakeFetch(() => json(402, { error: { type: 'cap_reached', code: 'wallet_empty', message: 'Top up.' } }));
    const err = await createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f }).call('POST', '/v1/x', { body: {} }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(V1Error);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 402, code: 'wallet_empty', message: 'Top up.' });
  });
});

const frame = (seq: number, type: string, data: object) => `id: ${seq}\nevent: ${type}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ seq, type, data, created_at: '2026-10-07T12:00:00Z' })}\n\n`;
const sse = (chunks: string[], keepOpen = false) =>
  new Response(
    new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      start(c) {
        for (const chunk of chunks) c.enqueue(new TextEncoder().encode(chunk));
        if (!keepOpen) c.close();
      },
    }),
    { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' } },
  );

describe('streams', () => {
  it('opens from the start (after=0) or from Last-Event-ID, and yields frames', async () => {
    const { f, calls } = fakeFetch(() => sse([frame(1, 'task.started', { task_id: 'task_1' }), frame(2, 'task.progress', { task_id: 'task_1', todos: [] })]));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f });
    const s = await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/conv_1/events', { lastEventId: null });
    const got = [];
    for await (const fr of s.frames) got.push(fr);
    expect(got.map((g) => [g.id, g.event])).toEqual([[1, 'task.started'], [2, 'task.progress']]);
    expect(calls[0]!.url.searchParams.get('after')).toBe('0');
    expect(calls[0]!.headers.accept).toBe('text/event-stream');
    await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/conv_1/events', { lastEventId: 7 });
    expect(calls[1]!.headers['last-event-id']).toBe('7');
    expect(calls[1]!.url.searchParams.has('after')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('puts a stream on the key with the fewest open, counts them, and frees them on close', async () => {
    const { f } = fakeFetch(() => sse([], true));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS.slice(0, 2), fetch: f, maxStreamsPerKey: 1 });
    const a = await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/c1/events', { lastEventId: 0 });
    const b = await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/c2/events', { lastEventId: 0 });
    expect([a.keyIndex, b.keyIndex]).toEqual([0, 1]);
    expect(v1.openStreams()).toEqual([1, 1]);
    await expect(v1.stream('/v1/conversations/c3/events', { lastEventId: 0 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'too_many_streams' });
    a.close();
    b.close();
    expect(v1.openStreams()).toEqual([0, 0]);
  });

  it('moves to another key when /v1 says a key has too many streams, and says stream_expired on 410', async () => {
    const busy = fakeFetch((s) => (keyOf(s) === K('a') ? json(429, { error: { code: 'too_many_streams' } }) : sse([], true)));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS.slice(0, 2), fetch: busy.f });
    const s = await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/c1/events', { lastEventId: 3 });
    expect(s.keyIndex).toBe(1);
    s.close();
    const gone = fakeFetch(() => json(410, { error: { code: 'stream_expired', message: 'too old' } }));
    await expect(createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: gone.f }).stream('/v1/conversations/c1/events', { lastEventId: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 410, code: 'stream_expired' });
  });

  it('gives up on a stream that says nothing at all, not even a ping', async () => {
    const { f } = fakeFetch(() => sse([], true));
    const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: 'https://api.test', keys: KEYS, fetch: f });
    const s = await v1.stream('/v1/conversations/c1/events', { lastEventId: 0, quietMs: 30 });
    const read = async () => {
      for await (const f of s.frames) void f;
    };
    await expect(read()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'stream_quiet' });
    expect(v1.openStreams()[s.keyIndex]).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/sse.ts
/** One event from a /v1 stream: `id` is the resume point, `event` the type, `data` the parsed JSON (the whole event object). */
export interface SseFrame { id: number | null; event: string; data: unknown }

/** Splits an SSE buffer into complete frames and the unfinished rest. Comment lines are skipped. */
export function parseSseFrames(buffer: string): { frames: SseFrame[]; rest: string } {
  const parts = buffer.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n\n');
  const rest = parts.pop() ?? '';
  const frames: SseFrame[] = [];
  for (const part of parts) {
    let id: number | null = null;
    let event = 'message';
    const data: string[] = [];
    for (const line of part.split('\n')) {
      if (!line || line.startsWith(':')) continue;
      const at = line.indexOf(':');
      const field = at === -1 ? line : line.slice(0, at);
      const value = at === -1 ? '' : line.slice(at + 1).replace(/^ /, '');
      if (field === 'id') id = value !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(value)) ? Number(value) : null;
      else if (field === 'event') event = value;
      else if (field === 'data') data.push(value);
    }
    if (data.length === 0) continue;
    const raw = data.join('\n');
    let parsed: unknown = raw;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      parsed = raw;
    }
    frames.push({ id, event, data: parsed });
  }
  return { frames, rest };
}
```
