```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/card-updater.ts
/**
 * replace_card for working cards: at most one update every 6 seconds per card, the updates in between folded into the
 * newest. A change of state (needs you, done, didn't work, stopped) goes out at once. Updates to one card go in order.
 */
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import type { WorkingCardT } from './working-card.js';

export const CARD_GAP_MS = 6_000;
/** A failed update is tried once more after this long: a card that rides with a turn's message may not be posted yet. */
export const CARD_RETRY_MS = 3_000;

export interface CardUpdater {
  push(tripId: string, card: WorkingCardT, opts?: { now?: boolean }): void;
  /** Sends every waiting update now and waits for all of them. */
  flush(): Promise<void>;
}

interface Slot {
  lastAt: number;
  pending: { tripId: string; card: WorkingCardT } | null;
  timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;
  chain: Promise<void>;
}

export function createCardUpdater(d: {
  link: Pick<ServerLink, 'request'>;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
  gapMs?: number;
  retryMs?: number;
  nowMs?: () => number;
}): CardUpdater {
  const gap = d.gapMs ?? CARD_GAP_MS;
  const retryMs = d.retryMs ?? CARD_RETRY_MS;
  const clock = d.nowMs ?? Date.now;
  const slots = new Map<string, Slot>();

  const send = (slot: Slot, cardId: string, retried = false): void => {
    const p = slot.pending;
    if (!p) return;
    slot.pending = null;
    slot.lastAt = clock();
    slot.chain = slot.chain
      .then(() => d.link.request('replace_card', { trip_id: p.tripId, card: p.card }))
      .then(
        () => undefined,
        (err: unknown) => {
          d.log.warn({ err, cardId, retried }, 'tasks: a working card update failed');
          // Once more, a little later, unless a newer update is already waiting.
          if (retried || slot.pending || slot.timer) return;
          slot.pending = p;
          slot.timer = setTimeout(() => {
            slot.timer = null;
            send(slot, cardId, true);
          }, retryMs);
          slot.timer.unref?.();
        },
      );
  };

  return {
    push(tripId, card, opts) {
      let slot = slots.get(card.card_id);
      if (!slot) {
        slot = { lastAt: Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY, pending: null, timer: null, chain: Promise.resolve() };
        slots.set(card.card_id, slot);
      }
      slot.pending = { tripId, card };
      const wait = slot.lastAt + gap - clock();
      if (opts?.now || wait <= 0) {
        if (slot.timer) {
          clearTimeout(slot.timer);
          slot.timer = null;
        }
        send(slot, card.card_id);
        return;
      }
      if (!slot.timer) {
        const s = slot;
        s.timer = setTimeout(() => {
          s.timer = null;
          send(s, card.card_id);
        }, wait);
        s.timer.unref?.();
      }
    },
    async flush() {
      for (const [cardId, slot] of slots) {
        if (slot.timer) {
          clearTimeout(slot.timer);
          slot.timer = null;
          send(slot, cardId);
        }
      }
      await Promise.all([...slots.values()].map((s) => s.chain));
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/tasks.ts
/**
 * Tasks: big jobs on the trip's computer. start() saves the job, posts its working card and returns at once; the
 * hand-off to /v1 runs behind it (a fresh trip token, the trip's conversation, one message with hand_off: always),
 * so "On it" and the card reach the room within a second. At most three jobs run per trip; more wait as queued and
 * start in order as others end. message() answers an open question or hand-off, or passes the words on to the job.
 * stop() ends a job at once on the card and on /v1; a stopped job never posts results.
 */
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import { clip } from '../travel/cards.js';
import { mintId, stableId } from '../travel/ids.js';
import type { CardUpdater } from './card-updater.js';
import type { Computer } from './computer.js';
import type { ReturnsKind } from './returns.js';
import { LIVE_STATES, type NewTask, type TaskRow, type TaskState, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import { V1Error, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';
import { buildWorkingCard, plannedSteps, type WorkingCardT } from './working-card.js';

export const MAX_ACTIVE_PER_TRIP = 3;
export const DEFAULT_DEADLINE_MS = 8 * 60_000;
/** While a job waits on a person, its deadline moves past the 10 minutes a question or hand-off may wait. */
export const WAITING_GRACE_MS = 11 * 60_000;

export interface TaskBrief {
  goal: string;
  /** JSON Schema for the answer: the job's output_schema. */
  returns: unknown;
  /** Only the trip facts the job needs. /v1 fences them as data. */
  context: Record<string, unknown>;
  title?: string;
  steps?: string[];
  successCriteria?: string;
  returnsKind?: ReturnsKind;
  /** The person who asked: a hand-off is pinned to them. */
  askedBy?: string;
  /** The same key starts one job, so a turn that runs again does not start a second. */
  key?: string;
  /** The working card's id: a turn passes ctx.stableId('c_work'). Otherwise one is made from the job's id. */
  cardId?: string;
  /** A turn's ctx.cards: the working card rides with the turn's message instead of being posted at once. */
  cards?: C.Card[];
}

export interface Tasks {
  start(tripId: string, brief: TaskBrief): Promise<{ taskId: string; cardId: string }>;
  message(taskId: string, text: string): Promise<void>;
  stop(taskId: string): Promise<void>;
  running(tripId: string): Promise<Array<{ id: string; brief: string; state: string }>>;
  /** Added by P4: settles a hand-off the person did themselves, or could not do. */
  handoff(taskId: string, outcome: 'done' | 'could_not' | 'not_now'): Promise<void>;
}

/** What a task desk item carries; tasks/context.ts says how each reads in "why Go woke". */
export type TaskWake =
  | { event: 'completed'; taskId: string; title: string; resultsId: string | null; count: number; summary: string | null }
  | { event: 'failed'; taskId: string; title: string; reason: string }
  | { event: 'question'; taskId: string; title: string; interactionId: string; questions: Array<{ question: string; options: string[] }> }
  | { event: 'handoff'; taskId: string; title: string; interactionId: string; personId: string; summary: string }
  | { event: 'question_dropped'; taskId: string; title: string; question: string };

export class TaskError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly code: 'unknown_task' | 'not_live' | 'not_started' | 'no_handoff', message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'TaskError';
  }
}

export interface TasksDeps {
  store: TaskStore;
  v1: V1Client;
  computer: Computer;
  cards: CardUpdater;
  link: Pick<ServerLink, 'request'>;
  desk: Brain['desk'];
  packs: Pick<Brain['packs'], 'get'>;
  now: () => Date;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void; info(obj: object, msg: string): void };
  /** Told when a job reached a conversation, so the follower opens its stream. */
  follow: (conversationId: string, tripId: string) => void;
  deadlineMs?: number;
}

export interface TasksService extends Tasks {
  dispatch(taskId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Maps task starts /v1 announced to their jobs; a job stopped before it was named is stopped on /v1 now. */
  mapStarts(): Promise<void>;
  startQueued(tripId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** After a job ends: tokens go when nothing runs, and the next waiting job starts. */
  afterEnd(tripId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Ends a live job as "Didn't work", with one line for the composer. */
  fail(row: TaskRow, endReason: string, reason: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Stops a live job on /v1 and ends it as "Didn't work" (a deadline, a hand-off nobody answered). */
  expire(taskId: string, endReason: 'deadline' | 'handoff_expired', reason: string): Promise<void>;
  cardFor(row: TaskRow): Promise<WorkingCardT>;
  pushCard(row: TaskRow, urgent: boolean): Promise<void>;
  stopV1(v1TaskId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Waits for every hand-off running in the background. */
  settled(): Promise<void>;
}

export function createTasks(d: TasksDeps): TasksService {
  const deadlineMs = d.deadlineMs ?? DEFAULT_DEADLINE_MS;
  const inFlight = new Set<Promise<void>>();
  const track = (p: Promise<void>): void => {
    inFlight.add(p);
    void p.finally(() => inFlight.delete(p));
  };
  const isLive = (state: TaskState): boolean => LIVE_STATES.includes(state);

  async function live(taskId: string): Promise<TaskRow> {
    const row = await d.store.get(taskId);
    if (!row) throw new TaskError('unknown_task', `There is no job ${taskId}.`);
    if (!isLive(row.state)) throw new TaskError('not_live', `That job has ended (${row.state}).`);
    return row;
  }

  async function backToWork(row: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    if (await d.store.transition(row.id, ['needs_you'], 'running', { needsPersonId: null })) {
      const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
      if (fresh) await service.pushCard(fresh, true);
    }
  }

  const service: TasksService = {
    async start(tripId, brief) {
      const id = brief.key ? stableId('gt', tripId, brief.key) : mintId('gt');
      const known = await d.store.get(id);
      if (known) {
        // A rerun of the same turn: the card rides with the rerun's message too.
        if (brief.cards && !brief.cards.some((c) => c.card_id === known.cardId)) brief.cards.push(await service.cardFor(known));
        return { taskId: known.id, cardId: known.cardId };
      }
      const now = d.now();
      const state: TaskState = (await d.store.countActive(tripId)) >= MAX_ACTIVE_PER_TRIP ? 'queued' : 'starting';
      const row: NewTask = {
        id,
        tripId,
        title: clip(brief.title ?? brief.goal, 80),
        goal: brief.goal,
        returnsKind: brief.returnsKind ?? 'custom',
        returns: brief.returns,
        context: brief.context,
        successCriteria: brief.successCriteria ?? null,
        askedBy: brief.askedBy ?? null,
        state,
        cardId: brief.cardId ?? `c_work_${id.slice(3)}`,
        steps: plannedSteps(brief.steps),
        deadlineAt: state === 'starting' ? new Date(now.getTime() + deadlineMs) : null,
        createdAt: now,
      };
      if (!(await d.store.insert(row))) {
        const again = await d.store.get(id);
        if (again) return { taskId: again.id, cardId: again.cardId };
      }
      const saved = await d.store.get(id);
      if (!saved) throw new TaskError('unknown_task', `The job ${id} was not saved.`);
      if (brief.cards) {
        brief.cards.push(await service.cardFor(saved));
      } else {
        try {
          const posted = await d.link.request('post_message', { trip_id: tripId, request_id: `go_task_${id}_card`, card: await service.cardFor(saved) });
          await d.store.update(id, { messageId: posted.message_id });
        } catch (err) {
          // No card, no job: a job the room cannot see must not run.
          await d.store.transition(id, ['starting', 'queued'], 'failed', { endedAt: d.now(), endReason: 'card_failed', error: 'The working card could not be posted.' });
          throw err;
        }
      }
      if (state === 'starting') track(service.dispatch(id));
      return { taskId: id, cardId: row.cardId };
    },

    async dispatch(taskId) {
      const row = await d.store.get(taskId);
      if (!row || row.state !== 'starting' || row.stopRequestedAt) return;
      try {
        await d.computer.armToken(row.tripId);
        const { conversationId } = await d.computer.conversation(row.tripId);
        const turn = await d.v1.call<{ id: string }>('POST', `/v1/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`, {
          idempotencyKey: `go-msg-${row.id}`,
          body: {
            message: row.goal,
            context: row.context,
            output_schema: row.returns,
            ...(row.successCriteria ? { success_criteria: row.successCriteria } : {}),
            hand_off: 'always',
            metadata: { go_task_id: row.id },
          },
        });
        await d.store.setTurn(row.id, conversationId, turn.id);
        await service.mapStarts();
        d.follow(conversationId, row.tripId);
      } catch (err) {
        d.log.warn({ err, taskId }, "tasks: handing the job to the trip's computer failed");
        const reason = err instanceof V1Error && err.code === 'wallet_empty' ? "Go's account for the trip's computer is out of credit." : "It could not start on the trip's computer.";
        await service.fail(row, 'dispatch_failed', reason);
      }
    },

    async mapStarts() {
      for (const m of await d.store.matchStarts()) {
        const row = await d.store.get(m.id);
        if (!row) continue;
        if (row.stopRequestedAt) {
          await service.stopV1(m.v1TaskId);
          continue;
        }
        if (await d.store.transition(row.id, ['starting'], 'running', { startedAt: d.now() })) {
          const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
          if (fresh) await service.pushCard(fresh, false);
        }
      }
    },

    async startQueued(tripId) {
      while ((await d.store.countActive(tripId)) < MAX_ACTIVE_PER_TRIP) {
        const next = await d.store.oldestQueued(tripId);
        if (!next) return;
        if (!(await d.store.transition(next.id, ['queued'], 'starting', { deadlineAt: new Date(d.now().getTime() + deadlineMs) }))) continue;
        const fresh = await d.store.get(next.id);
        if (fresh) await service.pushCard(fresh, true);
        track(service.dispatch(next.id));
      }
    },

    async afterEnd(tripId) {
      if ((await d.store.countActive(tripId)) === 0) await d.computer.releaseTokens(tripId);
      await service.startQueued(tripId);
    },

    async fail(row, endReason, reason) {
      if (!(await d.store.transition(row.id, LIVE_STATES, 'failed', { endedAt: d.now(), endReason, error: clip(reason, 300), needsPersonId: null }))) return;
      const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
      if (fresh) await service.pushCard(fresh, true);
      const wake: TaskWake = { event: 'failed', taskId: row.id, title: row.title, reason: clip(reason, 200) };
      await d.desk.put({ id: `task:${row.id}:failed`, tripId: row.tripId, kind: 'task', ref: row.id, dueAt: d.now(), payload: wake });
      await service.afterEnd(row.tripId);
    },

    async expire(taskId, endReason, reason) {
      const row = await d.store.get(taskId);
      if (!row || !isLive(row.state)) return;
      await d.store.update(taskId, { stopRequestedAt: d.now() });
      const open = await d.store.openInteractions(taskId);
      // The job ends in Go first, so the task.stopped /v1 sends back (on the stream, at once) finds it ended and
      // changes nothing: the card says "Didn't work", never "Stopped".
      await service.fail(row, endReason, reason);
      for (const i of open) {
        await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${i.interactionId}/cancel`, { body: { reason: 'Nobody could answer in time.' } }).catch(() => undefined);
        await d.store.setInteraction(i.interactionId, 'cancelled', d.now());
      }
      if (row.v1TaskId) await service.stopV1(row.v1TaskId);
    },

    async message(taskId, text) {
      const row = await live(taskId);
      const open = await d.store.openInteractions(taskId);
      const ask = open.find((i) => i.type === 'question') ?? open.find((i) => i.type === 'handoff');
      if (ask) {
        const words = text.slice(0, 4000);
        const answers = ask.type === 'question' && ask.questions.length > 1 ? ask.questions.map(() => words) : [words];
        await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${ask.interactionId}/answer`, { body: { answers } });
        await d.store.setInteraction(ask.interactionId, 'answered', d.now());
        if (ask.type === 'handoff') await backToWork(row);
        return;
      }
      if (!row.conversationId) throw new TaskError('not_started', 'That job has not reached the computer yet. Try again in a moment.');
      await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/conversations/${row.conversationId}/messages`, {
        idempotencyKey: stableId('go-follow', taskId, text),
        body: { message: `About the job "${row.title}"${row.v1TaskId ? ` (${row.v1TaskId})` : ''}: ${text.slice(0, 4000)}`, hand_off: 'auto', metadata: { go_task_id: taskId } },
      });
    },

    async handoff(taskId, outcome) {
      const row = await live(taskId);
      const ask = (await d.store.openInteractions(taskId)).find((i) => i.type === 'handoff');
      if (!ask) throw new TaskError('no_handoff', 'That job is not waiting on anyone.');
      await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${ask.interactionId}/answer`, { body: { [outcome]: true } });
      await d.store.setInteraction(ask.interactionId, 'answered', d.now());
      await backToWork(row);
    },

    async stop(taskId) {
      const row = await d.store.get(taskId);
      if (!row || !isLive(row.state)) return;
      await d.store.update(taskId, { stopRequestedAt: d.now() });
      if (!(await d.store.transition(taskId, LIVE_STATES, 'stopped', { endedAt: d.now(), endReason: 'stopped', needsPersonId: null }))) return;
      const fresh = await d.store.get(taskId);
      if (fresh) await service.pushCard(fresh, true);
      await d.desk.cancel(row.tripId, taskId);
      for (const i of await d.store.openInteractions(taskId)) await d.store.setInteraction(i.interactionId, 'cancelled', d.now());
      if (row.v1TaskId) await service.stopV1(row.v1TaskId);
      await service.afterEnd(row.tripId);
    },

    async running(tripId) {
      return (await d.store.listLive(tripId)).map((r) => ({ id: r.id, brief: r.title, state: r.state }));
    },

    async cardFor(row) {
      const pack = await d.packs.get(row.tripId);
      return buildWorkingCard(row, { now: d.now(), nameOf: (id) => pack.trip.people.find((p) => p.person_id === id)?.name });
    },

    async pushCard(row, urgent) {
      d.cards.push(row.tripId, await service.cardFor(row), { now: urgent });
    },

    async stopV1(v1TaskId) {
      try {
        await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/tasks/${v1TaskId}/stop`, { idempotencyKey: `go-stop-${v1TaskId}` });
      } catch (err) {
        if (!(err instanceof V1Error && (err.status === 404 || err.status === 409))) d.log.warn({ err, v1TaskId }, 'tasks: stopping a job on /v1 failed');
      }
    },

    async settled() {
      while (inFlight.size > 0) await Promise.allSettled([...inFlight]);
    },
  };
  return service;
}
```

Expected: `card-updater.test.ts` (2) and `tasks.test.ts` (11) pass.

---

### Task 16: Applying /v1 events, and the webhook endpoint

One `apply()` for every `/v1` event, whichever way it came (the live stream, a webhook, the events poll, the sweep). Every step is idempotent: state only moves forward and desk items have fixed ids, so the same moment from two sources is applied once. A question goes on the desk so the composer asks the room; a hand-off pins the card for the person who asked; an approval (which should never appear) is answered from Go's policy and logged; a completion reads the job's result from `/v1`, turns the picks into the options card's options (`resolvePicks`: an offer id Go's search showed this trip becomes that offer with its checked prices and whole-stay total, anything else is "Found on the web" with no offer), saves them as a result set and wakes the composer, whose `show_options` builds the card from that set. So `Travel.offer(option_id)` on the card answers the offer id and, for a stay, `priceMinor` = the whole stay; a web pick answers `offerId: null`. `POST /v1-webhooks` checks the signature over the raw body (HMAC-SHA256 of `"<t>.<raw body>"`, 300 s) and dedupes on the `evt_` id.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/db/migrations/3004_task_events.sql`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/event-store.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/events.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/webhooks.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/harness.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/events.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/webhooks.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TasksService`, `TaskWake`, `WAITING_GRACE_MS` (Task 15); `TaskStore`, `LIVE_STATES`, `stepsFromTodos`, `readReturned`, `resolvePicks`, `OPTION_KIND_FOR`, `ReturnsKind` (Task 14); `ResultsStore['save']`, `ResultsStore['findOffer']` (Task 5); in the test only, `createTravelLayer` (Task 6), `travelTools` (Task 7), `createImages` (Task 5), `createFakeTravel`, `FakeIxigoStore` (Tasks 2 and 4), `FakeSwitches` (Task 1); `GO_MCP_SLUG`, `GO_READ_ONLY_TOOLS` (Task 12); `V1Client` (Task 9); `FakeV1`, `signV1` (Task 10).
- Produces: `EventStore { markSeen; unmarkSeen; cursor; setCursor; lastSeq; setLastSeq; resetLastSeq }`, `createEventStore(pool, now)`; `V1Event { type; data; conversationId?; source }`, `TaskEvents { apply(ev) }`, `createTaskEvents(d)`; `verifyV1Signature(raw, header, secrets, nowSec)`, `PlatformEvent`, `toV1Event(e, source)`, `registerV1Webhooks(app, d)`; test harness `makeTaskHarness({ autoStart?, cardGapMs? })`, `VILLA_BRIEF`, `HARNESS_KEYS`, `HARNESS_ADMIN`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/events.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import { FakeIxigoStore } from '../travel/fake-ixigo/data.js';
import { createImages } from '../travel/images.js';
import { createTravelLayer } from '../travel/layer.js';
import { createFakeTravel } from '../travel/mcp-travel.js';
import type { ResultOption } from '../travel/results.js';
import { travelTools } from '../travel/tools.js';
import { FakeSwitches } from './testing/lane-brain.js';
import { makeTaskHarness, type TaskHarness } from './testing/harness.js';

let h: TaskHarness;
let travel: ReturnType<typeof createTravelLayer>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await makeTaskHarness();
  const now = h.lb.clock.now;
  travel = createTravelLayer({
    switches: new FakeSwitches(), now, fake: createFakeTravel({ store: new FakeIxigoStore({ now }), now, payRail: () => 'fake' }), ixigo: null, results: h.results,
  });
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await h.close();
});

/** The three villas as Go's search_stays showed them to the job (fake ixigo, 6 to 9 Nov, 5 guests): nightly and whole stay. */
const SEARCHED_AT = '2026-10-07T11:58:00.000Z';
const stay = (offerId: string, title: string, area: string, night: number): ResultOption => ({
  offerId, kind: 'stay', title, priceMinor: night, totalMinor: night * 3, currency: 'INR', priceNote: 'per night', highlights: ['Private pool'], facts: { nights: 3, area },
});
const SOL = stay('fake:st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5', 'Villa Sol', 'Anjuna', 1280000);
const PALMS = stay('fake:st-the-palms-vagator-20261106-20261109-5', 'The Palms Villa', 'Vagator', 1220000);
const BREEZE = stay('fake:st-ocean-breeze-candolim-20261106-20261109-5', 'Ocean Breeze Villa', 'Candolim', 1310000);
/** What Go saved when the job called Go's search_stays (Task 13 saves every search the trip's computer makes). */
const searched = (tripId: string) =>
  h.results.save({ tripId, optionKind: 'stay', source: 'search', query: { area: 'North Goa' }, options: [SOL, PALMS, BREEZE], checkedAt: SEARCHED_AT });

const VILLAS = {
  summary: 'Three villas with a pool near the beach.',
  options: [
    { title: 'Villa Sol', area: 'Anjuna', price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3840000, highlights: ['Private pool', '8 min to the beach'], rating: 4.7, offer_id: SOL.offerId, why: 'Pool and the beach close by.' },
    { title: 'The Palms Villa', area: 'Vagator', price_minor: 1220000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3660000, highlights: ['Private pool', '5 min to the beach'], rating: 4.5, offer_id: PALMS.offerId, why: 'Closest to Vagator beach.' },
    { title: 'Ocean Breeze Villa', area: 'Candolim', price_minor: 1310000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3930000, highlights: ['Private pool'], rating: 4.3, offer_id: BREEZE.offerId, why: 'Three minutes from the sand.' },
  ],
};

describe('applying /v1 events', () => {
  it('maps task.started to the job, which then runs', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev1', 't_ev1');
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ state: 'running', v1TaskId });
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.state).toBe('running');
  });

  it("ticks the card's steps from the job's to-do list", async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev2', 't_ev2');
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.progress', data: { progress: 'status', task_id: v1TaskId, todos: [{ content: 'Search stays', status: 'completed' }, { content: 'Read reviews', status: 'in_progress' }] }, source: 'stream' });
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.steps).toEqual([{ text: 'Search stays', state: 'done' }, { text: 'Read reviews', state: 'doing' }]);
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))?.todosAt).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('puts a question on the desk for the composer once, and gives the job time to wait', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev3', 't_ev3');
    const ask = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'question', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'Which dates?', questions: [{ question: 'Which dates?', header: 'Dates', options: [{ label: '6 to 9 Nov', description: '' }], multi_select: false }] });
    const ev = { type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: ask.id, type: 'question', task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'stream' as const };
    await h.events.apply(ev);
    await h.events.apply({ ...ev, source: 'webhook' });
    const items = h.lb.desk.items.filter((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:ask:${ask.id}`);
    expect(items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(items[0]!.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'question', taskId: row.id, questions: [{ question: 'Which dates?', options: ['6 to 9 Nov'] }] });
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))!.deadlineAt!.getTime()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(h.lb.clock.now().getTime() + 11 * 60_000);
  });

  it('pins a hand-off to the person who asked, and lets go when it is resolved', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev4', 't_ev4');
    const ask = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'handoff', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'Sign in to the villa site', browser: { suggested_mode: 'control', url: null } });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: ask.id, type: 'handoff', task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'webhook' });
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'needs_you', for_person_ids: ['p_meher'], fallback_text: 'Meher is needed: Finding villas with a pool in North Goa' });
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:handoff:${ask.id}`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'handoff', personId: 'p_meher' });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.resolved', data: { interaction_id: ask.id, type: 'handoff', task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'stream' });
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'running' });
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.for_person_ids).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("answers an approval from Go's policy: yes for Go's read-only tools, no for anything else", async () => {
    const { v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev5', 't_ev5');
    const read = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'approval', kind: 'tool_permission', summary: 'Use trip', details: { server: 'go-trip', tool: 'trip' } });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: read.id, type: 'approval', task_id: v1TaskId, server: 'go-trip', name: 'trip' }, source: 'stream' });
    expect(h.fake.interactions.get(read.id)?.answer).toEqual({ approve: true, present_user: true });
    const other = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'approval', kind: 'tool_permission', summary: 'Post on a site', details: {} });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: other.id, type: 'approval', task_id: v1TaskId, name: 'mcp__composio__TWITTER_POST' }, source: 'stream' });
    expect(h.fake.interactions.get(other.id)?.answer).toMatchObject({ approve: false });
  });

  it("on completion reads the job's result from /v1, saves the villas as results, ticks the card done and wakes the composer once", async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev6', 't_ev6');
    await searched(row.tripId);
    h.fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found three villas.');
    const ev = { type: 'task.completed', data: { task_id: v1TaskId, status: 'completed' }, source: 'webhook' as const };
    await h.events.apply(ev);
    await h.events.apply({ ...ev, source: 'stream' });
    await h.cards.flush();
    const done = (await h.store.get(row.id))!;
    expect(done).toMatchObject({ state: 'done', endReason: 'completed', costUsd: 0.42 });
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'done', actions: [] });
    const items = h.lb.desk.items.filter((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:done`);
    expect(items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(items[0]!.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'completed', resultsId: done.resultsId, count: 3, summary: 'Three villas with a pool near the beach.' });
    const set = await h.results.get(row.tripId, done.resultsId!);
    expect(set?.options.map((o) => o.title)).toEqual(['Villa Sol', 'The Palms Villa', 'Ocean Breeze Villa']);
    expect(set?.options.map((o) => [o.offerId, o.totalMinor])).toEqual([[SOL.offerId, 3840000], [PALMS.offerId, 3660000], [BREEZE.offerId, 3930000]]);
    expect(set?.checkedAt).toBe(SEARCHED_AT);
  });

  it("builds the options card from the job's picks: offer ids answer travel.offer with those offers at whole-stay totals, and a web pick has no offer", async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev10', 't_ev10');
    await searched(row.tripId);
    const webPick = {
      title: 'Casa Lua', area: 'Assagao', price_minor: 1500000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 4500000, highlights: ['Garden'], offer_id: null,
      source_url: 'https://villas.example/casa-lua', why: 'Quiet, with a big garden.',
    };
    h.fake.complete(v1TaskId, { ...VILLAS, options: [...VILLAS.options, webPick] }, 'Found four villas.');
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.completed', data: { task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'webhook' });
    const done = (await h.store.get(row.id))!;
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:done`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'completed', count: 4 });
    // The card, built the way show_options builds it in the composer's turn.
    const show = travelTools({ travel, results: h.results, images: createImages({ pool: h.pool, publicUrl: 'https://go.test' }), now: h.lb.clock.now })
      .find((t) => t.name === 'show_options')!;
    const ctx = h.lb.ctx({ tripId: row.tripId, turnId: 'turn_ev10' });
    await show.run(show.input.parse({ results_id: done.resultsId, option_numbers: [1, 2, 3, 4], title: 'Villas with a pool' }), ctx);
    const card = ctx.cards[0] as Extract<C.Card, { kind: 'options' }>;
    expect(card.checked_at).toBe(SEARCHED_AT);
    expect(await Promise.all(card.options.map((o) => travel.offer(o.option_id)))).toEqual([
      { offerId: SOL.offerId, title: 'Villa Sol', priceMinor: 3840000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null },
      { offerId: PALMS.offerId, title: 'The Palms Villa', priceMinor: 3660000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null },
      { offerId: BREEZE.offerId, title: 'Ocean Breeze Villa', priceMinor: 3930000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null },
      { offerId: null, title: 'Casa Lua', priceMinor: 4500000, currency: 'INR', kind: 'stay', link: null },
    ]);
    expect(card.options[0]).toMatchObject({ price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', subtitle: 'Anjuna, ₹38,400 for 3 nights' });
    expect(card.options[3]?.highlights[0]).toBe('Found on the web');
  });

  it('never posts results for a job that was stopped, even when /v1 finishes it afterwards', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('ev7', 't_ev7');
    await h.tasks.stop(row.id);
    h.fake.tasks.get(v1TaskId)!.status = 'working';
    h.fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found three villas.');
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.completed', data: { task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'webhook' });
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))?.state).toBe('stopped');
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.some((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:done`)).toBe(false);
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.fallback_text).toBe('Stopped: Finding villas with a pool in North Goa');
  });

  it('ends a failed job as "Didn\'t work", and a job that hit its spend cap with that reason', async () => {
    const a = await h.runningJob('ev8', 't_ev8');
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.failed', data: { task_id: a.v1TaskId, message: 'The villa site would not load.' }, source: 'webhook' });
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${a.row.id}:failed`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'failed', reason: 'The villa site would not load.' });
    const b = await h.runningJob('ev9', 't_ev9');
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.stopped', data: { task_id: b.v1TaskId, reason: 'max_budget' }, source: 'webhook' });
    expect(await h.store.get(b.row.id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'failed', endReason: 'max_budget' });
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${b.row.id}:failed`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ reason: 'It hit its spending limit, so it stopped.' });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/webhooks.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import Fastify, { type FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { signV1 } from './testing/fake-v1.js';
import { makeTaskHarness, type TaskHarness } from './testing/harness.js';
import { registerV1Webhooks, verifyV1Signature } from './webhooks.js';

const SECRET = 'whsec_test_0123456789abcdef';
const OLD = 'whsec_old_0123456789abcdef';
let h: TaskHarness;
let app: FastifyInstance;
const apply = vi.fn(async () => undefined);

beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await makeTaskHarness();
  app = Fastify({ logger: false });
  await registerV1Webhooks(app, { secrets: [SECRET, OLD], events: h.eventStore, apply, now: h.lb.clock.now, log: h.lb.brain.log });
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await app.close();
  await h.close();
});

const nowSec = () => Math.floor(h.lb.clock.now().getTime() / 1000);
const evt = (id: string) => ({ id, object: 'event', type: 'task.completed', mode: 'test', created_at: '2026-10-07T12:00:00Z', user_id: 'gotrip-1', task_id: 'task_9', conversation_id: 'conv_1', data: { status: 'completed' } });
const post = (body: string, signature: string) => app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: '/v1-webhooks', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'vestra-signature': signature }, payload: body });

describe('verifyV1Signature', () => {
  it('accepts a fresh signature from the current or the rolled secret, and refuses the rest', () => {
    const raw = JSON.stringify(evt('evt_1'));
    const t = nowSec();
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, signV1(SECRET, raw, t), [SECRET], t)).toBe('ok');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, signV1(OLD, raw, t), [SECRET, OLD], t)).toBe('ok');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, `${signV1('other', raw, t)},v1=${signV1(SECRET, raw, t).split('v1=')[1]}`, [SECRET], t)).toBe('ok');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, signV1(SECRET, raw, t - 301), [SECRET], t)).toBe('stale');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, signV1('other', raw, t), [SECRET], t)).toBe('mismatch');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(`${raw} `, signV1(SECRET, raw, t), [SECRET], t)).toBe('mismatch');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, 'v1=abc', [SECRET], t)).toBe('malformed');
    expect(verifyV1Signature(raw, undefined, [SECRET], t)).toBe('missing');
  });
});

describe('POST /v1-webhooks', () => {
  it('applies a signed event once, with the task id from the envelope, and answers a repeat as a duplicate', async () => {
    const body = JSON.stringify(evt('evt_100'));
    const sig = signV1(SECRET, body, nowSec());
    expect((await post(body, sig)).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'task.completed', data: { status: 'completed', task_id: 'task_9' }, conversationId: 'conv_1', source: 'webhook' });
    const again = await post(body, sig);
    expect(again.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(again.json()).toEqual({ ok: true, duplicate: true });
    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('refuses a bad signature without applying anything', async () => {
    const body = JSON.stringify(evt('evt_101'));
    expect((await post(body, signV1('wrong-secret-000000', body, nowSec()))).statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('answers 500 when applying fails, so Vestra sends it again, and the resend is applied', async () => {
    apply.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('database down'));
    const body = JSON.stringify(evt('evt_102'));
    expect((await post(body, signV1(SECRET, body, nowSec()))).statusCode).toBe(500);
    expect((await post(body, signV1(SECRET, body, nowSec()))).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  });
});
```

```sql
-- apps/brain/src/db/migrations/3004_task_events.sql
-- Lane C (P4): the /v1 events Go has applied (webhooks and the events poll dedupe on the evt_ id), where the events
-- poll is up to, and each conversation stream's resume point.

create table v1_events_seen (
  evt_id text primary key,
  type text not null,
  received_at timestamptz not null
);

create table v1_cursor (
  name text primary key,
  evt_id text not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null
);

create table task_streams (
  conversation_id text primary key,
  last_seq integer not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null
);
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/event-store.ts
/** What Go remembers about /v1 events: which it applied, where the events poll is up to, each stream's resume point. */
import type pg from 'pg';

export interface EventStore {
  /** True the first time an event id is seen. */
  markSeen(evtId: string, type: string): Promise<boolean>;
  unmarkSeen(evtId: string): Promise<void>;
  cursor(name: string): Promise<string | null>;
  setCursor(name: string, evtId: string): Promise<void>;
  lastSeq(conversationId: string): Promise<number | null>;
  /** Moves a resume point forward only. */
  setLastSeq(conversationId: string, seq: number): Promise<void>;
  /** Sets a resume point outright: after a 410, the conversation's head. */
  resetLastSeq(conversationId: string, seq: number): Promise<void>;
}

export function createEventStore(pool: pg.Pool, now: () => Date): EventStore {
  return {
    async markSeen(evtId, type) {
      const r = await pool.query('insert into v1_events_seen (evt_id, type, received_at) values ($1, $2, $3) on conflict (evt_id) do nothing', [evtId, type, now()]);
      return r.rowCount === 1;
    },
    async unmarkSeen(evtId) {
      await pool.query('delete from v1_events_seen where evt_id = $1', [evtId]);
    },
    async cursor(name) {
      const { rows } = await pool.query<{ evt_id: string }>('select evt_id from v1_cursor where name = $1', [name]);
      return rows[0]?.evt_id ?? null;
    },
    async setCursor(name, evtId) {
      await pool.query('insert into v1_cursor (name, evt_id, updated_at) values ($1, $2, $3) on conflict (name) do update set evt_id = excluded.evt_id, updated_at = excluded.updated_at', [name, evtId, now()]);
    },
    async lastSeq(conversationId) {
      const { rows } = await pool.query<{ last_seq: number }>('select last_seq from task_streams where conversation_id = $1', [conversationId]);
      return rows[0]?.last_seq ?? null;
    },
    async setLastSeq(conversationId, seq) {
      await pool.query(
        `insert into task_streams (conversation_id, last_seq, updated_at) values ($1, $2, $3)
         on conflict (conversation_id) do update set last_seq = greatest(task_streams.last_seq, excluded.last_seq), updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
        [conversationId, seq, now()],
      );
    },
    async resetLastSeq(conversationId, seq) {
      await pool.query(
        `insert into task_streams (conversation_id, last_seq, updated_at) values ($1, $2, $3)
         on conflict (conversation_id) do update set last_seq = excluded.last_seq, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
        [conversationId, seq, now()],
      );
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/events.ts
/**
 * One place that applies a /v1 event to Go's jobs, whichever way it came: the live stream, a webhook, the events poll
 * or the sweep. Every step is idempotent (state moves only forward, desk items have fixed ids), so the same event from
 * two sources, or twice from one, changes things once. A stopped job never posts results.
 */
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import { clip } from '../travel/cards.js';
import type { ResultsStore } from '../travel/results.js';
import { GO_MCP_SLUG, GO_READ_ONLY_TOOLS } from './instructions.js';
import { OPTION_KIND_FOR, readReturned, resolvePicks, type ReturnsKind } from './returns.js';
import { LIVE_STATES, type TaskRow, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import { WAITING_GRACE_MS, type TaskWake, type TasksService } from './tasks.js';
import type { V1Client } from './v1-client.js';
import { stepsFromTodos } from './working-card.js';

export interface V1Event { type: string; data: Record<string, unknown>; conversationId?: string | null; source: 'stream' | 'webhook' | 'poll' | 'sweep' }
export interface TaskEvents { apply(ev: V1Event): Promise<void> }

export interface TaskEventsDeps {
  store: TaskStore;
  tasks: TasksService;
  v1: V1Client;
  desk: Brain['desk'];
  results: Pick<ResultsStore, 'save' | 'findOffer'>;
  packs: Pick<Brain['packs'], 'get'>;
  now: () => Date;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void; info(obj: object, msg: string): void };
}

interface V1Interaction {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  task_id: string | null;
  summary: string | null;
  questions?: Array<{ question: string; options?: Array<{ label: string }> }>;
  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

const str = (v: unknown): string | null => (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0 ? v : null);

/** "mcp__go-trip__trip" or ("go-trip", "trip"): the server and the bare tool name. */
function toolOf(name: string | null, server: string | null): { server: string | null; tool: string | null } {
  const m = name ? /^mcp__(.+?)__(.+)$/.exec(name) : null;
  return m ? { server: m[1] ?? null, tool: m[2] ?? null } : { server, tool: name };
}

export function createTaskEvents(d: TaskEventsDeps): TaskEvents {
  const isLive = (row: TaskRow | null): row is TaskRow => row !== null && LIVE_STATES.includes(row.state);

  async function wake(row: TaskRow, suffix: string, payload: TaskWake): Promise<void> {
    await d.desk.put({ id: `task:${row.id}:${suffix}`, tripId: row.tripId, kind: 'task', ref: row.id, dueAt: d.now(), payload });
  }

  async function extendDeadline(row: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    const until = new Date(d.now().getTime() + WAITING_GRACE_MS);
    if (!row.deadlineAt || row.deadlineAt < until) await d.store.update(row.id, { deadlineAt: until });
  }

  async function answer(row: TaskRow, interactionId: string, type: string, body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    await d.store.addInteraction({ interactionId, taskId: row.id, tripId: row.tripId, type, summary: null, questions: [], forPersonIds: [], createdAt: d.now() });
    await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${interactionId}/answer`, { body }).catch((err: unknown) => d.log.warn({ err, interactionId }, 'tasks: answering an interaction failed'));
    await d.store.setInteraction(interactionId, 'answered', d.now());
  }

  async function started(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const turnId = str(data.turn_id);
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    if (!turnId || !v1TaskId) return;
    await d.store.recordStart(turnId, v1TaskId, str(data.title));
    await d.tasks.mapStarts();
  }

  async function progress(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row) || row.stopRequestedAt || !Array.isArray(data.todos)) return;
    const steps = stepsFromTodos(data.todos as Array<{ content?: unknown; status?: unknown }>);
    if (steps.length === 0) return;
    const at = d.now();
    await d.store.update(row.id, { steps, todosAt: at });
    await d.tasks.pushCard({ ...row, steps, todosAt: at }, false);
  }

  async function interactionCreated(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const id = str(data.interaction_id);
    if (!id) return;
    let detail: V1Interaction;
    try {
      detail = await d.v1.call<V1Interaction>('GET', `/v1/interactions/${id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      d.log.warn({ err, interactionId: id }, 'tasks: reading an interaction failed; the sweep tries again');
      return;
    }
    if (detail.status !== 'pending') return;
    const v1TaskId = str(detail.task_id) ?? str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row)) {
      // An ask from a job that already ended (or was stopped) waits forever unless Go withdraws it.
      if (row) await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${id}/cancel`, { body: { reason: 'The job has ended.' } }).catch(() => undefined);
      return;
    }
    const summary = detail.summary ? clip(detail.summary, 300) : null;
    switch (detail.type) {
      case 'question': {
        const questions = (detail.questions ?? []).slice(0, 4).map((q) => ({ question: clip(q.question, 300), options: (q.options ?? []).map((o) => clip(o.label, 80)).slice(0, 4) }));
        if (!(await d.store.addInteraction({ interactionId: id, taskId: row.id, tripId: row.tripId, type: 'question', summary, questions, forPersonIds: [], createdAt: d.now() }))) return;
        await extendDeadline(row);
        await wake(row, `ask:${id}`, { event: 'question', taskId: row.id, title: row.title, interactionId: id, questions });
        return;
      }
      case 'handoff': {
        const personId = row.askedBy ?? (await d.packs.get(row.tripId)).trip.organizer_person_id;
        if (!(await d.store.addInteraction({ interactionId: id, taskId: row.id, tripId: row.tripId, type: 'handoff', summary, questions: [], forPersonIds: [personId], createdAt: d.now() }))) return;
        await extendDeadline(row);
        if (await d.store.transition(row.id, ['starting', 'running'], 'needs_you', { needsPersonId: personId })) {
          const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
          if (fresh) await d.tasks.pushCard(fresh, true);
        }
        await wake(row, `handoff:${id}`, { event: 'handoff', taskId: row.id, title: row.title, interactionId: id, personId, summary: summary ?? 'It needs someone to sign in or give a code.' });
        return;
      }
      case 'approval': {
        // Go's tools run without asking, so this should never come. When it does, Go's policy answers it.
        const { server, tool } = toolOf(str(data.name) ?? str(detail.details?.tool), str(data.server) ?? str(detail.details?.server));
        const allowed = server === GO_MCP_SLUG && tool !== null && ((GO_READ_ONLY_TOOLS as readonly string[]).includes(tool) || tool === 'note');
        d.log.warn({ taskId: row.id, server, tool, allowed }, "tasks: a job asked for an approval; answered from Go's policy");
        await answer(row, id, 'approval', allowed ? { approve: true, present_user: true } : { approve: false, message: 'Go does not allow this for trip jobs.' });
        return;
      }
      case 'connect':
        d.log.warn({ taskId: row.id }, 'tasks: a job asked to connect an app; refused');
        await answer(row, id, 'connect', { approve: false });
        return;
      case 'tool_call':
        await answer(row, id, 'tool_call', { error: 'Go has no client tools for this job.' });
        return;
      default:
        return;
    }
  }

  async function interactionSettled(type: string, data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const id = str(data.interaction_id);
    if (!id) return;
    await d.store.setInteraction(id, type === 'interaction.expired' ? 'expired' : 'resolved', d.now());
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row) || row.state !== 'needs_you') return;
    if ((await d.store.openInteractions(row.id)).some((i) => i.type === 'handoff')) return;
    if (await d.store.transition(row.id, ['needs_you'], 'running', { needsPersonId: null })) {
      const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
      if (fresh) await d.tasks.pushCard(fresh, true);
    }
  }

  async function completed(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row) || row.stopRequestedAt || !v1TaskId) return;
    let task: { result?: { text?: string | null; data?: unknown } | null; cost_usd?: number | null };
    try {
      task = await d.v1.call('GET', `/v1/tasks/${v1TaskId}`);
    } catch (err) {
      d.log.warn({ err, v1TaskId }, "tasks: reading a finished job's result failed; using the event's summary");
      task = { result: { text: str(data.answer) ?? str(data.summary), data: null } };
    }
    const kind = row.returnsKind as ReturnsKind;
    const read = readReturned(kind, task.result?.data ?? null, task.result?.text ?? str(data.summary));
    const optionKind = OPTION_KIND_FOR[kind];
    // An offer id Go's search showed this trip becomes that offer, checked prices and whole stay included; any other
    // stay or journey pick is "Found on the web", with no offer, so it can be voted on but never booked.
    const picks = optionKind && read.options.length > 0
      ? await resolvePicks(row.tripId, optionKind, read.options, (tripId, offerId) => d.results.findOffer(tripId, offerId))
      : null;
    if (picks && picks.fromWeb > 0) d.log.info({ taskId: row.id, fromWeb: picks.fromWeb }, 'tasks: picks with no offer Go showed are shown as found on the web');
    const set = optionKind && picks
      ? await d.results.save({ tripId: row.tripId, optionKind, source: 'task', query: { task_id: row.id }, options: picks.options, checkedAt: picks.checkedAt ?? d.now().toISOString() })
      : null;
    const moved = await d.store.transition(row.id, LIVE_STATES, 'done', {
      endedAt: d.now(),
      endReason: 'completed',
      needsPersonId: null,
      result: task.result ?? null,
      resultsId: set?.resultsId ?? null,
      ...(typeof task.cost_usd === 'number' ? { costUsd: task.cost_usd } : {}),
    });
    if (!moved) return;
    const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
    if (fresh) await d.tasks.pushCard(fresh, true);
    await wake(row, 'done', { event: 'completed', taskId: row.id, title: row.title, resultsId: set?.resultsId ?? null, count: read.options.length, summary: read.summary });
    await d.tasks.afterEnd(row.tripId);
  }

  async function quietStop(row: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    if (await d.store.transition(row.id, LIVE_STATES, 'stopped', { endedAt: d.now(), endReason: 'stopped', needsPersonId: null })) {
      const fresh = await d.store.get(row.id);
      if (fresh) await d.tasks.pushCard(fresh, true);
      await d.tasks.afterEnd(row.tripId);
    }
  }

  async function failed(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row)) return;
    if (row.stopRequestedAt) return quietStop(row);
    await d.tasks.fail(row, 'failed', str(data.message) ?? str(data.reason) ?? 'The computer reported a problem.');
  }

  async function stopped(data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const v1TaskId = str(data.task_id);
    const row = v1TaskId ? await d.store.byV1Task(v1TaskId) : null;
    if (!isLive(row)) return;
    if (row.stopRequestedAt) return quietStop(row);
    const why = str(data.reason);
    const reason = why === 'max_budget'
      ? 'It hit its spending limit, so it stopped.'
      : why === 'credits_exhausted'
        ? "Go's account for the trip's computer ran out of credit."
        : 'It was stopped on the computer.';
    await d.tasks.fail(row, why === 'max_budget' ? 'max_budget' : 'stopped_on_v1', reason);
  }

  return {
    async apply(ev) {
      switch (ev.type) {
        case 'task.started':
          return started(ev.data);
        case 'task.progress':
          return progress(ev.data);
        case 'interaction.created':
          return interactionCreated(ev.data);
        case 'interaction.resolved':
        case 'interaction.expired':
          return interactionSettled(ev.type, ev.data);
        case 'task.completed':
          return completed(ev.data);
        case 'task.failed':
          return failed(ev.data);
        case 'task.stopped':
          return stopped(ev.data);
        default:
          return;
      }
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/webhooks.ts
/**
 * POST /v1-webhooks: Vestra's signed events. The signature is checked over the raw body bytes (HMAC-SHA256 of
 * "<t>.<raw body>", at most 300 s old, against the current secret or the one being rolled). Each evt_ id is applied
 * once; when applying fails the answer is 500 and the id is forgotten, so Vestra's retry is applied.
 */
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type { EventStore } from './event-store.js';
import type { TaskEvents, V1Event } from './events.js';

export type SignatureCheck = 'ok' | 'missing' | 'malformed' | 'stale' | 'mismatch';

export function verifyV1Signature(raw: string, header: string | undefined, secrets: string[], nowSec: number, toleranceSec = 300): SignatureCheck {
  if (!header) return 'missing';
  let t: number | null = null;
  const signatures: string[] = [];
  for (const part of header.split(',')) {
    const at = part.indexOf('=');
    if (at === -1) continue;
    const k = part.slice(0, at).trim();
    const v = part.slice(at + 1).trim();
    if (k === 't') t = Number(v);
    else if (k === 'v1' && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(v)) signatures.push(v);
  }
  if (t === null || !Number.isInteger(t) || signatures.length === 0) return 'malformed';
  if (Math.abs(nowSec - t) > toleranceSec) return 'stale';
  for (const secret of secrets) {
    const want = createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`${t}.${raw}`).digest();
    if (signatures.some((s) => {
      const got = Buffer.from(s, 'hex');
      return got.length === want.length && timingSafeEqual(got, want);
    })) return 'ok';
  }
  return 'mismatch';
}

export interface PlatformEvent {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  created_at?: string;
  user_id?: string | null;
  task_id?: string | null;
  conversation_id?: string | null;
  data?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

/** A webhook or polled event as apply() takes it; the envelope's task_id fills in when the data has none. */
export function toV1Event(e: PlatformEvent, source: V1Event['source']): V1Event {
  const data: Record<string, unknown> = { ...(e.data ?? {}) };
  if (e.task_id && data.task_id === undefined) data.task_id = e.task_id;
  return { type: e.type, data, conversationId: e.conversation_id ?? null, source };
}

export interface WebhookDeps {
  secrets: string[];
  events: Pick<EventStore, 'markSeen' | 'unmarkSeen'>;
  apply: TaskEvents['apply'];
  now: () => Date;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
}

export async function registerV1Webhooks(app: FastifyInstance, d: WebhookDeps): Promise<void> {
  await app.register(async (scope) => {
    // The raw bytes, never re-serialised JSON: the signature is over exactly what Vestra sent.
    scope.addContentTypeParser('application/json', { parseAs: 'buffer' }, (_req, body, done) => {
      done(null, body);
    });
    scope.post('/v1-webhooks', async (req, reply) => {
      const raw = Buffer.isBuffer(req.body) ? req.body.toString('utf8') : '';
      const header = req.headers['vestra-signature'];
      const check = verifyV1Signature(raw, typeof header === 'string' ? header : undefined, d.secrets, Math.floor(d.now().getTime() / 1000));
      if (check !== 'ok') {
        d.log.warn({ check }, 'tasks: a webhook failed its signature check');
        return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'bad_signature' });
      }
      let evt: PlatformEvent;
      try {
        evt = JSON.parse(raw) as PlatformEvent;
      } catch {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'bad_body' });
      }
      if (typeof evt.id !== 'string' || typeof evt.type !== 'string') return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'bad_body' });
      if (!(await d.events.markSeen(evt.id, evt.type))) return reply.code(200).send({ ok: true, duplicate: true });
      try {
        await d.apply(toV1Event(evt, 'webhook'));
      } catch (err) {
        await d.events.unmarkSeen(evt.id);
        d.log.warn({ err, evt: evt.id }, 'tasks: applying a webhook failed; Vestra sends it again');
        return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'retry' });
      }
      return reply.code(200).send({ ok: true });
    });
  });
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/testing/harness.ts
/**
 * Lane C's task harness for tests: a fresh schema, the lane brain, a fake /v1, and the real task store, computer,
 * tasks and events wired the way registerTasks wires them.
 */
import type pg from 'pg';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { createTokenStore } from '../../mcp/tokens.js';
import { createResultsStore, type ResultsStore } from '../../travel/results.js';
import { createCardUpdater, type CardUpdater } from '../card-updater.js';
import { createComputer, type Computer } from '../computer.js';
import { createEventStore, type EventStore } from '../event-store.js';
import { createTaskEvents, type TaskEvents } from '../events.js';
import { returnsSchema } from '../returns.js';
import { createTaskStore, type TaskRow, type TaskStore } from '../store.js';
import { createTasks, type TaskBrief, type TasksService } from '../tasks.js';
import { createV1Client, type V1Client } from '../v1-client.js';
import { FakeV1 } from './fake-v1.js';
import { makeLaneBrain, type LaneBrain } from './lane-brain.js';

export const HARNESS_KEYS = [`vk_test_${'a'.repeat(43)}`, `vk_test_${'b'.repeat(43)}`];
export const HARNESS_ADMIN = `vk_test_${'z'.repeat(43)}`;

export const VILLA_BRIEF: TaskBrief = {
  goal: 'Find a villa with a pool in North Goa under 8k a head, near the beach',
  returns: returnsSchema('stays'),
  context: { area: 'North Goa', people: 5 },
  title: 'Finding villas with a pool in North Goa',
  steps: ['Search stays', 'Read reviews', 'Pick three'],
  returnsKind: 'stays',
  askedBy: 'p_meher',
};

export interface TaskHarness {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  lb: LaneBrain;
  fake: FakeV1;
  v1: V1Client;
  store: TaskStore;
  eventStore: EventStore;
  results: ResultsStore;
  cards: CardUpdater;
  computer: Computer;
  tasks: TasksService;
  events: TaskEvents;
  /** Told when a job reaches a conversation; a test sets it to a real follower. */
  onFollow: ((conversationId: string, tripId: string) => void) | null;
  /** The villa job, started, handed to /v1 and named by it (task.started applied). Needs fake.autoStart off. */
  runningJob(key: string, tripId?: string): Promise<{ row: TaskRow; v1TaskId: string }>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export async function makeTaskHarness(o: { autoStart?: boolean; cardGapMs?: number } = {}): Promise<TaskHarness> {
  const db = await withSchema();
  const lb = makeLaneBrain({ pool: db.pool });
  const fake = new FakeV1({ keys: HARNESS_KEYS, adminKey: HARNESS_ADMIN, now: lb.clock.now });
  fake.autoStart = o.autoStart ?? false;
  await fake.start();
  const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: fake.url, keys: HARNESS_KEYS, adminKey: HARNESS_ADMIN, sleep: async () => undefined });
  const store = createTaskStore(db.pool);
  const eventStore = createEventStore(db.pool, lb.clock.now);
  const results = createResultsStore(db.pool);
  const cards = createCardUpdater({ link: lb.link, log: lb.brain.log, gapMs: o.cardGapMs ?? 0 });
  const computer = createComputer({
    pool: db.pool, v1, tokens: createTokenStore(db.pool, lb.clock.now), env: { VESTRA_DEPLOYMENT_ID: 'dep_go', VESTRA_MCP_SERVER_ID: 'mcp_go' }, now: lb.clock.now, log: lb.brain.log,
  });
  let onFollow: TaskHarness['onFollow'] = null;
  const tasks = createTasks({
    store, v1, computer, cards, link: lb.link, desk: lb.desk, packs: lb.brain.packs, now: lb.clock.now, log: lb.brain.log,
    follow: (conversationId, tripId) => onFollow?.(conversationId, tripId),
  });
  const events = createTaskEvents({ store, tasks, v1, desk: lb.desk, results, packs: lb.brain.packs, now: lb.clock.now, log: lb.brain.log });
  return {
    pool: db.pool, lb, fake, v1, store, eventStore, results, cards, computer, tasks, events,
    get onFollow() {
      return onFollow;
    },
    set onFollow(f) {
      onFollow = f;
    },
    async runningJob(key, tripId = 't_goa') {
      const r = await tasks.start(tripId, { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key });
      await tasks.settled();
      const row = await store.get(r.taskId);
      if (!row?.conversationId || !row.v1TurnId) throw new Error(`job ${key} did not reach /v1`);
      const t = fake.startTask(row.conversationId, row.v1TurnId, row.title);
      await events.apply({ type: 'task.started', data: { task_id: t.id, title: row.title, turn_id: row.v1TurnId }, source: 'stream' });
      await cards.flush();
      return { row: (await store.get(r.taskId))!, v1TaskId: t.id };
    },
    async close() {
      await cards.flush();
      await fake.close();
      await db.drop();
    },
  };
}
```

Expected: `events.test.ts` (9) and `webhooks.test.ts` (4) pass. Each test runs its job on its own trip, so the three-jobs-per-trip limit never queues one.

---

### Task 17: The follower and the screen (one live stream per trip conversation)

While any job of a trip runs, Go keeps that trip conversation's event stream open on the key with the fewest open (one stream per trip, not per job), applies each event through `apply()`, and saves the resume point after each one. A drop reopens from the last event number; a resume point too old for `/v1` (410) is replaced by the conversation's head (the sweep fills any gap); with every key's streams in use, the follower polls the same events as JSON. The trip's screen is read every 7 s while it follows and re-hosted only when it changed; the trace stands in for steps only while a job has written no to-do list.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/screens.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/follower.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/screens.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/follower.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `V1Client`, `V1Error`, `SseFrame` (Task 9); `TaskStore`, `TaskRow`, `TaskStep`, `LIVE_STATES` (Task 14); `TasksService['pushCard']` (Task 15); `EventStore`, `TaskEvents` (Task 16); `Images['putBytes']` (Task 5); `makeTaskHarness` (Task 16).
- Produces: `Screens { tick(tripId) }`, `createScreens(d)`, `stepWords(name, summary)`; `Follower { ensure(conversationId, tripId); following(); close() }`, `createFollower(d)`.

```ts
  screens = createScreens({ v1: h.v1, store: h.store, images: createImages({ pool: h.pool, publicUrl: 'https://go.test' }), tasks: h.tasks, userIdFor: h.computer.userIdFor, now: h.lb.clock.now, log: h.lb.brain.log });
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await h.close();
});

describe("the trip's screen on the card", () => {
  it('re-hosts the screen only when it changed, and puts the new picture on the card', async () => {
    const { row } = await h.runningJob('scr', 't_scr');
    h.fake.screen = Buffer.from([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 1]);
    await screens.tick('t_scr');
    await h.cards.flush();
    const first = (await h.store.get(row.id))!.screenshotUrl;
    expect(first).toMatch(/^https:\/\/go\.test\/img\/i[0-9a-f]{32}\.jpg$/);
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.screenshot_url).toBe(first);
    const updates = h.lb.link.sent('replace_card').length;
    await screens.tick('t_scr');
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect(h.lb.link.sent('replace_card')).toHaveLength(updates);
    h.fake.screen = Buffer.from([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 2]);
    await screens.tick('t_scr');
    await h.cards.flush();
    const second = (await h.store.get(row.id))!.screenshotUrl;
    expect(second).not.toBe(first);
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.screenshot_url).toBe(second);
    h.fake.screen = null;

  it('shows the last steps from the trace while a job has no to-do list, and stops once it writes one', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('trace', 't_trace');
    h.fake.traceSteps = [
      { tool_use_id: 'a', name: 'WebSearch', input_summary: 'villas north goa pool', is_error: false, started_at: '2026-10-07T12:00:01Z', finished_at: '2026-10-07T12:00:03Z' },
      { tool_use_id: 'b', name: 'mcp__browser-control__computer_navigate', input_summary: 'https://www.example-villas.in/north-goa', is_error: false, started_at: '2026-10-07T12:00:04Z', finished_at: null },
    ];

    await screens.tick('t_trace');

    await h.events.apply({ type: 'task.progress', data: { task_id: v1TaskId, todos: [{ content: 'Search stays', status: 'in_progress' }] }, source: 'stream' });
    h.lb.clock.advance(20_000);
    await screens.tick('t_trace');
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))!.steps).toEqual([{ text: 'Search stays', state: 'doing' }]);
  });

  it('words a trace step plainly and never echoes what the job read', () => {
    expect(stepWords('WebFetch', 'https://reviews.example.com/villa-sol?ref=ignore-your-rules')).toBe('Reading reviews.example.com');
    expect(stepWords('mcp_invoke', 'search_stays')).toBe("Using Go's trip tools");
    expect(stepWords('Bash', 'rm -rf /')).toBe('Working');

});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/follower.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { eventually } from './testing/lane-brain.js';
import { createFollower, type Follower } from './follower.js';
import { HARNESS_ADMIN, HARNESS_KEYS, VILLA_BRIEF, makeTaskHarness, type TaskHarness } from './testing/harness.js';
import { createV1Client } from './v1-client.js';

let h: TaskHarness;
let follower: Follower;
const noScreens = { tick: async () => undefined };

beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await makeTaskHarness({ autoStart: true });
  follower = createFollower({ v1: h.v1, store: h.store, streams: h.eventStore, apply: h.events.apply, screens: noScreens, log: h.lb.brain.log, reconnectMs: [10, 20], pollMs: 20, screenMs: 60_000 });
  h.onFollow = (c, t) => follower.ensure(c, t);
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await follower.close();
  await h.close();
});

const OPTIONS = { summary: 'Three villas.', options: [{ title: 'Villa Sol', area: 'Anjuna', price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', highlights: ['Private pool'], why: 'Pool.' }] };
const stateOf = async (id: string) => (await h.store.get(id))?.state;

describe('the follower', () => {
  it("follows a job's conversation: it starts, its to-do list ticks the card, it finishes, and the stream is let go", async () => {
    const r = await h.tasks.start('t_f1', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: 'f1' });
    await h.tasks.settled();
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'running');
    const row = (await h.store.get(r.taskId))!;
    expect(follower.following()).toContain(row.conversationId);
    h.fake.progress(row.v1TaskId!, [['Search stays', 'completed'], ['Read reviews', 'in_progress']]);
    await eventually(async () => ((await h.store.get(r.taskId))?.steps ?? []).some((s) => s.text === 'Read reviews' && s.state === 'doing'));
    h.fake.complete(row.v1TaskId!, OPTIONS, 'Three villas.');
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'done');
    await eventually(() => follower.following().length === 0);
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.filter((i) => i.id === `task:${r.taskId}:done`)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('reopens a dropped stream from the last event number and applies every event once', async () => {
    h.fake.dropAfterFrames = 2;
    const r = await h.tasks.start('t_f2', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: 'f2' });
    await h.tasks.settled();
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'running');
    const row = (await h.store.get(r.taskId))!;
    h.fake.progress(row.v1TaskId!, [['Search stays', 'in_progress']]);
    const reads = () => h.fake.callsTo('GET', new RegExp(`/v1/conversations/${row.conversationId}/events$`));
    await eventually(() => reads().length >= 2);
    expect(reads().at(-1)!.headers['last-event-id']).toBeDefined();
    h.fake.progress(row.v1TaskId!, [['Search stays', 'completed'], ['Read reviews', 'in_progress']]);
    h.fake.complete(row.v1TaskId!, OPTIONS, 'Three villas.');
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'done');
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.filter((i) => i.id === `task:${r.taskId}:done`)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('follows from the head when the resume point is too old (410)', async () => {
    h.fake.autoStart = false;
    h.fake.failNextStream = { status: 410, code: 'stream_expired' };
    const r = await h.tasks.start('t_f3', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: 'f3' });
    await h.tasks.settled();
    const row = (await h.store.get(r.taskId))!;
    await eventually(() => h.fake.callsTo('GET', new RegExp(`/v1/conversations/${row.conversationId}$`)).length > 0);
    await eventually(() => h.fake.callsTo('GET', new RegExp(`/v1/conversations/${row.conversationId}/events$`)).some((c) => c.headers['last-event-id'] === '0'));
    const t = h.fake.startTask(row.conversationId!, row.v1TurnId!, row.title);
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'running');
    h.fake.complete(t.id, OPTIONS, 'Three villas.');
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'done');
    h.fake.autoStart = true;
  });

  it("polls the events as JSON when every key's streams are in use", async () => {
    const oneKey = createV1Client({ baseUrl: h.fake.url, keys: [HARNESS_KEYS[0]!], adminKey: HARNESS_ADMIN, sleep: async () => undefined });
    const polling = createFollower({ v1: oneKey, store: h.store, streams: h.eventStore, apply: h.events.apply, screens: noScreens, log: h.lb.brain.log, reconnectMs: [10], pollMs: 20, screenMs: 60_000 });
    h.onFollow = (c, t) => polling.ensure(c, t);
    h.fake.failNextStream = { status: 429, code: 'too_many_streams' };
    const r = await h.tasks.start('t_f4', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: 'f4' });
    await h.tasks.settled();
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'running');
    const row = (await h.store.get(r.taskId))!;
    expect(h.fake.callsTo('GET', new RegExp(`/v1/conversations/${row.conversationId}/events$`)).some((c) => c.query.format === 'json')).toBe(true);
    h.fake.complete(row.v1TaskId!, OPTIONS, 'Three villas.');
    await eventually(async () => (await stateOf(r.taskId)) === 'done');
    await polling.close();
    h.onFollow = (c, t) => follower.ensure(c, t);
  });
});
```
