```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/screens.ts
/**
 * The trip's screen on the working cards: read while a job runs, re-hosted only when its bytes changed, so a card's
 * picture changes only with the screen. While a job has written no to-do list 20 s after it started, its last steps
 * from /v1's trace stand in (read at most every 15 s), in Go's own words: nothing the job read is echoed.
 */
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { clip } from '../travel/cards.js';
import type { Images } from '../travel/images.js';
import { LIVE_STATES, type TaskRow, type TaskStep, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import type { TasksService } from './tasks.js';
import { V1Error, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';

export interface ScreensDeps {
  v1: V1Client;
  store: TaskStore;
  images: Pick<Images, 'putBytes'>;
  tasks: Pick<TasksService, 'pushCard'>;
  userIdFor: (tripId: string) => string;
  now: () => Date;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
  traceAfterMs?: number;
  traceEveryMs?: number;
}
export interface Screens { tick(tripId: string): Promise<void> }

/** A trace step in plain words. Only the host of a URL is shown; a summary's other text never is. */
export function stepWords(name: string | null, summary: string | null): string {
  const n = (name ?? '').toLowerCase();
  const host = summary ? /https?:\/\/([^/\s?#]+)/.exec(summary)?.[1] : undefined;
  if (n.includes('websearch') || n.includes('web_search')) return 'Searching the web';
  if (n.includes('webfetch') || n.includes('web_fetch')) return host ? clip(`Reading ${host}`, 80) : 'Reading a page';
  if (n.includes('navigate')) return host ? clip(`Opening ${host}`, 80) : 'Opening a page';
  if (n.includes('mcp_invoke') || n.includes('mcp_search')) return "Using Go's trip tools";
  if (n.includes('click')) return 'Clicking through a page';
  if (n.includes('computer_type')) return 'Filling in a form';
  if (n.includes('screenshot') || n.includes('snapshot')) return 'Looking at the page';
  return 'Working';
}

interface TraceStep { name?: string | null; input_summary?: string | null; is_error?: boolean; finished_at?: string | null }

export function createScreens(d: ScreensDeps): Screens {
  const lastHash = new Map<string, string>();
  const traceAt = new Map<string, number>();

  async function traceFallback(r: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    const nowMs = d.now().getTime();
    if (r.todosAt || !r.startedAt || !r.v1TaskId || nowMs - r.startedAt.getTime() < (d.traceAfterMs ?? 20_000)) return;
    if (nowMs - (traceAt.get(r.id) ?? 0) < (d.traceEveryMs ?? 15_000)) return;
    traceAt.set(r.id, nowMs);
    try {
      const trace = await d.v1.call<{ steps?: TraceStep[] }>('GET', `/v1/tasks/${r.v1TaskId}/trace`);
      const steps = (trace.steps ?? []).slice(-4).map((s): TaskStep => ({
        text: stepWords(s.name ?? null, s.input_summary ?? null),
        state: s.is_error ? 'failed' : s.finished_at ? 'done' : 'doing',
      }));
      if (steps.length === 0) return;
      const fresh = await d.store.get(r.id);
      if (!fresh || fresh.todosAt || !LIVE_STATES.includes(fresh.state)) return;
      await d.store.update(r.id, { steps });
      await d.tasks.pushCard({ ...fresh, steps }, false);
    } catch (err) {
      d.log.warn({ err, taskId: r.id }, "tasks: reading a job's trace failed");
    }
  }

  return {
    async tick(tripId) {
      const rows = (await d.store.listLive(tripId)).filter((r) => r.state === 'running' || r.state === 'needs_you');
      if (rows.length === 0) {
        lastHash.delete(tripId);
        return;
      }
      try {
        const shot = await d.v1.bytes(`/v1/users/${d.userIdFor(tripId)}/screenshot`);
        const hash = createHash('sha256').update(shot.bytes).digest('hex');
        if (shot.bytes.length > 0 && hash !== lastHash.get(tripId)) {
          lastHash.set(tripId, hash);
          const url = await d.images.putBytes(shot.bytes, 'image/jpeg');
          for (const r of rows) {
            if (r.screenshotUrl === url) continue;
            await d.store.update(r.id, { screenshotUrl: url });
            await d.tasks.pushCard({ ...r, screenshotUrl: url }, false);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (!(err instanceof V1Error && err.code === 'browser_not_open')) d.log.warn({ err, tripId }, "tasks: reading the trip's screen failed");
      }
      for (const r of rows) await traceFallback(r);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/follower.ts
/**
 * One live event stream per trip conversation while any of its jobs runs, resumed from the last event number after
 * a drop. A resume point too old for /v1 (410) is replaced by the conversation's head (the sweep fills the gap); with
 * every key's streams in use, the same events are polled as JSON. Every event goes through apply(), so webhooks and
 * the sweep can deliver the same moments without doubling anything. The screen is read while a conversation is followed.
 */
import type { EventStore } from './event-store.js';
import type { TaskEvents } from './events.js';
import type { Screens } from './screens.js';
import type { SseFrame } from './sse.js';
import type { TaskStore } from './store.js';
import { V1Error, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';

export interface FollowerDeps {
  v1: V1Client;
  store: Pick<TaskStore, 'hasActiveIn'>;
  streams: Pick<EventStore, 'lastSeq' | 'setLastSeq' | 'resetLastSeq'>;
  apply: TaskEvents['apply'];
  screens: Screens;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
  reconnectMs?: number[];
  quietMs?: number;
  pollMs?: number;
  screenMs?: number;
}
export interface Follower {
  ensure(conversationId: string, tripId: string): void;
  following(): string[];
  /** Aborts every stream and wait, waits for the loops to end, and follows nothing after. registerTasks calls it from brain.onStop. */
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

const RECONNECT_MS = [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000];
const POLL_SPELL_MS = 60_000;

const sleep = (ms: number, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> =>
  new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (signal.aborted) return resolve();
    const t = setTimeout(resolve, ms);
    t.unref();
    signal.addEventListener('abort', () => {
      clearTimeout(t);
      resolve();
    }, { once: true });
  });

export function createFollower(d: FollowerDeps): Follower {
  const loops = new Map<string, { abort: AbortController; done: Promise<void> }>();
  const reconnect = d.reconnectMs ?? RECONNECT_MS;
  let closed = false;

  async function handle(conversationId: string, f: SseFrame): Promise<void> {
    if (f.event === 'ping') return;
    if (f.event === 'error') {
      const e = f.data as { data?: { code?: unknown } } | null;
      throw new V1Error(0, typeof e?.data?.code === 'string' ? e.data.code : 'stream_error', 'The stream ended with an error.');
    }
    const e = f.data as { type?: unknown; data?: unknown } | null;
    if (typeof e?.type === 'string') {
      const data = e.data && typeof e.data === 'object' ? (e.data as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
      await d.apply({ type: e.type, data, conversationId, source: 'stream' });
    }
    if (f.id !== null) await d.streams.setLastSeq(conversationId, f.id);
  }

  /** A spell of polling the events as JSON, for when no key has a stream free. */
  async function poll(conversationId: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
    const until = Date.now() + POLL_SPELL_MS;
    while (!signal.aborted && Date.now() < until && (await d.store.hasActiveIn(conversationId))) {
      const after = (await d.streams.lastSeq(conversationId)) ?? 0;
      const page = await d.v1.call<{ data: Array<{ seq: number; type: string; data?: Record<string, unknown> }> }>('GET', `/v1/conversations/${conversationId}/events`, {
        query: { format: 'json', after, limit: 100 },
      });
      for (const e of page.data) {
        await d.apply({ type: e.type, data: e.data ?? {}, conversationId, source: 'stream' });
        await d.streams.setLastSeq(conversationId, e.seq);
      }
      await sleep(d.pollMs ?? 5_000, signal);
    }
  }

  async function run(conversationId: string, tripId: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
    const screenTimer = setInterval(() => {
      void d.screens.tick(tripId).catch(() => undefined);
    }, d.screenMs ?? 7_000);
    screenTimer.unref();
    let failures = 0;
    try {
      while (!signal.aborted && (await d.store.hasActiveIn(conversationId))) {
        try {
          const s = await d.v1.stream(`/v1/conversations/${conversationId}/events`, {
            lastEventId: await d.streams.lastSeq(conversationId),
            signal,
            ...(d.quietMs ? { quietMs: d.quietMs } : {}),
          });
          try {
            for await (const f of s.frames) {
              failures = 0;
              await handle(conversationId, f);
              const ending = f.event === 'task.completed' || f.event === 'task.failed' || f.event === 'task.stopped';
              if (ending && !(await d.store.hasActiveIn(conversationId))) break;
            }
          } finally {
            s.close();
          }
        } catch (err) {
          if (signal.aborted) break;
          if (err instanceof V1Error && err.code === 'stream_expired') {
            const head = await d.v1.call<{ last_event_seq: number }>('GET', `/v1/conversations/${conversationId}`);
            await d.streams.resetLastSeq(conversationId, head.last_event_seq);
            d.log.warn({ conversationId }, 'tasks: the stream resume point was too old; following from the head, the sweep fills the gap');
            continue;
          }
          if (err instanceof V1Error && err.code === 'too_many_streams') {
            await poll(conversationId, signal);
            continue;
          }
          d.log.warn({ err, conversationId }, 'tasks: the event stream dropped; reopening it');
        }
        if (signal.aborted || !(await d.store.hasActiveIn(conversationId))) break;
        failures += 1;
        await sleep(reconnect[Math.min(failures - 1, reconnect.length - 1)] ?? 1_000, signal);
      }
    } finally {
      clearInterval(screenTimer);
    }
  }

  const follower: Follower = {
    ensure(conversationId, tripId) {
      // Once Go is stopping, a job's follow() or the sweep must not open a new stream.
      if (closed || loops.has(conversationId)) return;
      const abort = new AbortController();
      const done = run(conversationId, tripId, abort.signal)
        .catch((err: unknown) => d.log.warn({ err, conversationId }, 'tasks: following a conversation stopped'))
        .finally(async () => {
          loops.delete(conversationId);
          // A job that started while this loop was ending must not go unfollowed.
          if (!abort.signal.aborted && (await d.store.hasActiveIn(conversationId).catch(() => false))) follower.ensure(conversationId, tripId);
        });
      loops.set(conversationId, { abort, done });
    },
    following: () => [...loops.keys()],
    async close() {
      closed = true;
      for (const l of loops.values()) l.abort.abort();
      await Promise.allSettled([...loops.values()].map((l) => l.done));
    },
  };
  return follower;
}
```

---

### Task 18: The sweep (every 2 minutes, /v1 is the source of truth)

Whatever the stream and the webhooks missed is read back from `/v1` here: the events after the last one Go saw, jobs that never reached `/v1` (dispatched again; the idempotency keys make that safe), jobs `/v1` started without Go hearing (`GET /v1/events?type=task.started`), each running job's status and to-do list (`GET /v1/tasks/{id}`), asks nobody delivered (`GET /v1/conversations/{id}`), and a follower for every conversation with a running job. Then the clocks: a job past its deadline is stopped ("Took too long"), a question nobody answered in 10 minutes is cancelled with a line for the composer, and a hand-off nobody answered in 10 minutes ends the job as "Didn't work".

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/sweep.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/sweep.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `V1Client`, `V1Error` (Task 9); `Computer['userIdFor']` (Task 11); `TaskStore`, `TaskRow`, `LIVE_STATES`, `ACTIVE_STATES` (Task 14); `TasksService`, `TaskWake` (Task 15); `EventStore`, `TaskEvents`, `PlatformEvent`, `toV1Event` (Task 16); `stepsFromTodos` (Task 14); `makeTaskHarness` (Task 16).
- Produces: `createSweep(d: SweepDeps): Sweep { runOnce(): Promise<SweepReport>; start(everyMs?); stop(): Promise<void> }` (the timer is `unref()`ed; `stop()` clears it and waits for a sweep in flight, and registerTasks calls it from `brain.onStop`), `SWEEP_EVERY_MS`, `ASK_TTL_MS`, `SweepReport { polled; checked; expired; dropped }`.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/sweep.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createSweep, type Sweep } from './sweep.js';
import { VILLA_BRIEF, makeTaskHarness, type TaskHarness } from './testing/harness.js';

let h: TaskHarness;
let sweep: Sweep;
const followed: string[] = [];
const VILLAS = { summary: 'Three villas.', options: [{ title: 'Villa Sol', area: 'Anjuna', price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', highlights: ['Private pool'], why: 'Pool.' }] };

beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await makeTaskHarness();
  sweep = createSweep({
    v1: h.v1, store: h.store, eventStore: h.eventStore, tasks: h.tasks, events: h.events, computer: h.computer, follow: (c) => { followed.push(c); },
    desk: h.lb.desk, packs: h.lb.brain.packs, now: h.lb.clock.now, log: h.lb.brain.log,
  });
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await h.close();
});

describe('the sweep', () => {
  it('finishes a job whose end came by no stream and no webhook, reading its status from /v1', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('sw1', 't_sw1');
    h.fake.finishSilently(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Three villas.');
    const report = await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(report.checked).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))?.state).toBe('done');
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.filter((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:done`)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('applies /v1 events after the last one it saw, once, and follows every conversation with a running job', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('sw2', 't_sw2');
    await sweep.runOnce();
    h.fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Three villas.');
    const first = await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(first.polled).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))?.state).toBe('done');
    expect((await sweep.runOnce()).polled).toBe(0);
    const live = await h.runningJob('sw2b', 't_sw2b');
    await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(followed).toContain(live.row.conversationId);
  });

  it("maps a job /v1 started when Go never heard task.started", async () => {
    const r = await h.tasks.start('t_sw3', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: 'sw3' });
    await h.tasks.settled();
    const row = (await h.store.get(r.taskId))!;
    const t = h.fake.startTask(row.conversationId!, row.v1TurnId!, row.title);
    await h.eventStore.setCursor('events', h.fake.platformEvents.at(-1)!.id);
    h.lb.clock.advance(61_000);
    await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(await h.store.get(r.taskId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'running', v1TaskId: t.id });
  });

  it('stops a job past its deadline and says it took too long', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('sw4', 't_sw4');
    h.lb.clock.advance(9 * 60_000);
    const report = await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(report.expired).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    await h.cards.flush();
    expect(await h.store.get(row.id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'failed', endReason: 'deadline' });
    expect(h.fake.tasks.get(v1TaskId)?.stopped).toBe(true);
    expect(h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)?.fallback_text).toBe('Took too long: Finding villas with a pool in North Goa');
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:failed`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ reason: 'It took too long, so Go stopped it.' });
  });

  it('cancels a question nobody answered in 10 minutes, with a line for the composer, and the job carries on', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('sw5', 't_sw5');
    const ask = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'question', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'Which dates?', questions: [{ question: 'Which dates?', header: 'Dates', options: [{ label: '6 to 9 Nov', description: '' }], multi_select: false }] });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: ask.id, type: 'question', task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'webhook' });
    h.lb.clock.advance(10.5 * 60_000);
    const report = await sweep.runOnce();
    expect(report.dropped).toBe(1);
    expect(h.fake.interactions.get(ask.id)?.status).toBe('cancelled');
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:dropped:${ask.id}`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'question_dropped', question: 'Which dates?' });
    expect((await h.store.get(row.id))?.state).toBe('running');
  });

  it('ends a job whose hand-off nobody answered in 10 minutes as "Didn\'t work", unpinned, naming who it waited for', async () => {
    const { row, v1TaskId } = await h.runningJob('sw6', 't_sw6');
    const ask = h.fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'handoff', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'Sign in to the villa site', browser: { suggested_mode: 'control', url: null } });
    await h.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: ask.id, type: 'handoff', task_id: v1TaskId }, source: 'webhook' });
    h.lb.clock.advance(10.5 * 60_000);
    await sweep.runOnce();
    await h.cards.flush();
    const card = h.lb.link.card(row.cardId)!;
    expect(card).toMatchObject({ state: 'failed', fallback_text: "Didn't work: Finding villas with a pool in North Goa", actions: [] });
    expect(card.for_person_ids).toBeUndefined();
    expect(h.fake.interactions.get(ask.id)?.status).toBe('cancelled');
    expect(h.fake.tasks.get(v1TaskId)?.stopped).toBe(true);
    expect(h.lb.desk.items.find((i) => i.id === `task:${row.id}:failed`)?.payload).toMatchObject({ event: 'failed', reason: "Meher wasn't around to help, so Go stopped it." });
  });

  it('starts a waiting job when a slot is free, even if the job that ended forgot to start it', async () => {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) ids.push((await h.tasks.start('t_sw7', { ...VILLA_BRIEF, key: `sw7_${i}` })).taskId);
    await h.tasks.settled();
    expect((await h.store.get(ids[3]!))?.state).toBe('queued');
    await h.store.transition(ids[0]!, ['starting', 'running'], 'done', { endedAt: h.lb.clock.now() });
    await sweep.runOnce();
    await h.tasks.settled();
    expect((await h.store.get(ids[3]!))?.state).toBe('starting');
    expect(h.fake.callsTo('POST', /\/messages$/).some((c) => c.headers['idempotency-key'] === `go-msg-${ids[3]}`)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/sweep.ts
/**
 * The sweep, every 2 minutes. /v1 is the source of truth for a job, so whatever the stream and the webhooks missed is
 * read back here: /v1's events after the last one Go saw, jobs that never reached /v1, jobs /v1 started without Go
 * hearing, each running job's status and to-do list, asks nobody delivered, and a follower for each conversation with
 * a running job. Then the clocks: past its deadline a job is stopped; a question nobody answered in 10 minutes is
 * cancelled; a hand-off nobody answered in 10 minutes ends the job as "Didn't work".
 */
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import { clip } from '../travel/cards.js';
import type { Computer } from './computer.js';
import type { EventStore } from './event-store.js';
import type { TaskEvents } from './events.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATES, LIVE_STATES, type TaskRow, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import type { TaskWake, TasksService } from './tasks.js';
import { V1Error, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';
import { toV1Event, type PlatformEvent } from './webhooks.js';
import { stepsFromTodos } from './working-card.js';

export const SWEEP_EVERY_MS = 120_000;
/** How long a question or hand-off may wait on the room. */
export const ASK_TTL_MS = 10 * 60_000;
/** A job still not handed to /v1 after this long is handed again. */
const DISPATCH_GRACE_MS = 30_000;
/** A job /v1 has not named after this long is looked up in /v1's events. */
const START_GRACE_MS = 60_000;
const EVENTS_PAGE = 100;
const EVENT_PAGES = 5;

export interface SweepDeps {
  v1: V1Client;
  store: TaskStore;
  eventStore: EventStore;
  tasks: TasksService;
  events: TaskEvents;
  computer: Pick<Computer, 'userIdFor'>;
  follow: (conversationId: string, tripId: string) => void;
  desk: Brain['desk'];
  packs: Pick<Brain['packs'], 'get'>;
  now: () => Date;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
  askTtlMs?: number;
}
export interface SweepReport { polled: number; checked: number; expired: number; dropped: number }
export interface Sweep {
  runOnce(): Promise<SweepReport>;
  start(everyMs?: number): void;
  /** Clears the timer and waits for a sweep in flight, so nothing reads the pool after Go stops. */
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}

const str = (v: unknown): string | null => (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0 ? v : null);

export function createSweep(d: SweepDeps): Sweep {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  let running: Promise<unknown> | null = null;

  async function newest(): Promise<string | null> {
    const page = await d.v1.call<{ data: PlatformEvent[] }>('GET', '/v1/events', { query: { limit: 1 } });
    return page.data[0]?.id ?? null;
  }

  /** /v1's events after the cursor, oldest first, each applied once (webhooks share the seen list). */
  async function pollEvents(): Promise<number> {
    const cursor = await d.eventStore.cursor('events');
    if (!cursor) {
      // First run: start from now. What came before is covered by reading each job's status.
      const id = await newest();
      if (id) await d.eventStore.setCursor('events', id);
      return 0;
    }
    let after = cursor;
    let applied = 0;
    for (let page = 0; page < EVENT_PAGES; page += 1) {
      let got: { data: PlatformEvent[] };
      try {
        got = await d.v1.call<{ data: PlatformEvent[] }>('GET', '/v1/events', { query: { after, limit: EVENTS_PAGE } });
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof V1Error && err.status === 404) {
          // The cursor's event is past /v1's 30 days: start again from now.
          const id = await newest();
          if (id) await d.eventStore.setCursor('events', id);
          return applied;
        }
        throw err;
      }
      for (const e of got.data) {
        if (await d.eventStore.markSeen(e.id, e.type)) {
          try {
            await d.events.apply(toV1Event(e, 'poll'));
            applied += 1;
          } catch (err) {
            await d.eventStore.unmarkSeen(e.id);
            throw err;
          }
        }
        after = e.id;
        await d.eventStore.setCursor('events', after);
      }
      if (got.data.length < EVENTS_PAGE) break;
    }
    return applied;
  }

  /** task.started for this trip's user since the job was made, from /v1's events. */
  async function findStart(row: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    const found = await d.v1.call<{ data: PlatformEvent[] }>('GET', '/v1/events', {
      query: { type: 'task.started', user_id: d.computer.userIdFor(row.tripId), created_after: new Date(row.createdAt.getTime() - 10_000).toISOString(), limit: 50 },
    });
    for (const e of found.data) {
      const turnId = str(e.data?.turn_id);
      const v1TaskId = str(e.data?.task_id) ?? str(e.task_id);
      if (turnId && v1TaskId) await d.store.recordStart(turnId, v1TaskId, str(e.data?.title));
    }
    await d.tasks.mapStarts();
  }

  /** The job's status and to-do list, as /v1 has them. */
  async function checkJob(row: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    if (!row.v1TaskId) return;
    const task = await d.v1.call<{ status: string; progress?: Array<{ content: string; status: string }> }>('GET', `/v1/tasks/${row.v1TaskId}`);
    if (task.status === 'done') {
      await d.events.apply({ type: 'task.completed', data: { task_id: row.v1TaskId }, source: 'sweep' });
      return;
    }
    if (task.status === 'failed') {
      await d.events.apply({ type: 'task.failed', data: { task_id: row.v1TaskId, message: 'The computer reported a problem.' }, source: 'sweep' });
      return;
    }
    if (task.progress && task.progress.length > 0 && JSON.stringify(stepsFromTodos(task.progress)) !== JSON.stringify(row.steps)) {
      await d.events.apply({ type: 'task.progress', data: { task_id: row.v1TaskId, todos: task.progress }, source: 'sweep' });
    }
  }

  /** Asks /v1 has open on a conversation that Go never heard about. */
  async function missedAsks(conversationId: string): Promise<void> {
    const conv = await d.v1.call<{ open_interactions?: Array<{ interaction_id: string; task_id: string | null }> }>('GET', `/v1/conversations/${conversationId}`);
    const known = new Set((await d.store.allOpenInteractions()).map((i) => i.interactionId));
    for (const ask of conv.open_interactions ?? []) {
      if (known.has(ask.interaction_id)) continue;
      await d.events.apply({ type: 'interaction.created', data: { interaction_id: ask.interaction_id, ...(ask.task_id ? { task_id: ask.task_id } : {}) }, source: 'sweep' });
    }
  }

  async function settleOldAsks(): Promise<{ expired: number; dropped: number }> {
    const ttl = d.askTtlMs ?? ASK_TTL_MS;
    const nowMs = d.now().getTime();
    let expired = 0;
    let dropped = 0;
    for (const ask of await d.store.allOpenInteractions()) {
      if (nowMs - ask.createdAt.getTime() < ttl) continue;
      const row = await d.store.get(ask.taskId);
      if (!row || !LIVE_STATES.includes(row.state)) {
        await d.store.setInteraction(ask.interactionId, 'cancelled', d.now());
        continue;
      }
      if (ask.type === 'handoff') {
        const pack = await d.packs.get(row.tripId);
        const who = pack.trip.people.find((p) => p.person_id === ask.forPersonIds[0])?.name ?? 'The person it needed';
        await d.tasks.expire(row.id, 'handoff_expired', `${who} wasn't around to help, so Go stopped it.`);
        expired += 1;
      } else if (ask.type === 'question') {
        await d.v1.call('POST', `/v1/interactions/${ask.interactionId}/cancel`, { body: { reason: 'Nobody answered in time.' } }).catch(() => undefined);
        await d.store.setInteraction(ask.interactionId, 'cancelled', d.now());
        const wake: TaskWake = { event: 'question_dropped', taskId: row.id, title: row.title, question: clip(ask.questions[0]?.question ?? ask.summary ?? 'its question', 300) };
        await d.desk.put({ id: `task:${row.id}:dropped:${ask.interactionId}`, tripId: row.tripId, kind: 'task', ref: row.id, dueAt: d.now(), payload: wake });
        dropped += 1;
      }
    }
    return { expired, dropped };
  }

  async function runOnce(): Promise<SweepReport> {
    const report: SweepReport = { polled: 0, checked: 0, expired: 0, dropped: 0 };
    try {
      report.polled = await pollEvents();
    } catch (err) {
      d.log.warn({ err }, "tasks: the sweep could not read /v1's events");
    }
    const live = await d.store.listLive();
    for (const tripId of new Set(live.filter((r) => r.state === 'queued').map((r) => r.tripId))) await d.tasks.startQueued(tripId);
    const conversations = new Map<string, string>();
    const nowMs = d.now().getTime();
    for (const seen of live) {
      try {
        let row = await d.store.get(seen.id);
        if (!row || !LIVE_STATES.includes(row.state)) continue;
        if (row.state === 'starting' && !row.v1TurnId && nowMs - row.createdAt.getTime() > DISPATCH_GRACE_MS) {
          await d.tasks.dispatch(row.id);
          row = await d.store.get(row.id);
        }
        if (row?.v1TurnId && !row.v1TaskId && nowMs - row.createdAt.getTime() > START_GRACE_MS) {
          await findStart(row);
          row = await d.store.get(row.id);
        }
        if (row?.v1TaskId && (row.state === 'running' || row.state === 'needs_you')) {
          await checkJob(row);
          report.checked += 1;
          row = await d.store.get(row.id);
        }
        if (row && LIVE_STATES.includes(row.state) && row.deadlineAt && row.deadlineAt.getTime() < nowMs) {
          await d.tasks.expire(row.id, 'deadline', 'It took too long, so Go stopped it.');
          report.expired += 1;
          continue;
        }
        if (row?.conversationId && ACTIVE_STATES.includes(row.state)) conversations.set(row.conversationId, row.tripId);
      } catch (err) {
        d.log.warn({ err, taskId: seen.id }, 'tasks: the sweep could not check a job');
      }
    }
    for (const [conversationId, tripId] of conversations) {
      try {
        await missedAsks(conversationId);
        d.follow(conversationId, tripId);
      } catch (err) {
        d.log.warn({ err, conversationId }, 'tasks: the sweep could not read a conversation');
      }
    }
    const asks = await settleOldAsks();
    report.expired += asks.expired;
    report.dropped = asks.dropped;
    return report;
  }

  return {
    runOnce,
    start(everyMs = SWEEP_EVERY_MS) {
      if (timer || everyMs <= 0) return;
      timer = setInterval(() => {
        if (running) return;
        running = runOnce()
          .catch((err: unknown) => d.log.warn({ err }, 'tasks: a sweep failed'))
          .finally(() => {
            running = null;
          });
      }, everyMs);
      timer.unref();
    },
    async stop() {
      if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
      timer = null;
      await running;
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 19: The job tools, Watch and Stop, the tasks block, and registerTasks

The composer's four job tools (`start_task` puts the working card in `ctx.cards`, so "On it" and the card go out as one message; ids from `ctx.stableId`), the working card's two buttons (Watch mints a fresh 15-minute view session per tap and answers `open`, never posting the link; it asks for `control` only for the person a hand-off waits on and falls back to `view`), the `tasks` block of "right now" and the `task` wake lines (everything a job wrote is fenced as data), and `createTasksLane`, which wires the lane at boot and sets `brain.tasks`.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/tools.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/taps.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/context.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/register.ts`
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/main.ts` (`registerTasks` joins `LANES` right after `registerTravel`)
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/register.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/tasks/context.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Brain`, `RegisterLane`, `QuickTool`, `TurnCtx` (with `cards`, `stableId`), `NowProvider`, `DeskItem`, `TripPack`, `Trip` (P1); everything from Tasks 9 to 18; `createImages` (Task 5); `registerGoMcp`, `mcpCallRecorder`, `createTokenStore` (Tasks 11, 13); `createResultsStore` (Task 5).
- Produces: `taskTools({ tasks }): QuickTool<any>[]` (`start_task`, `message_task`, `stop_task`, `settle_handoff`); `registerTaskTaps(taps, d)`; `tasksNow(d): NowProvider` (name `tasks`, order 30); `renderTaskWake(item, pack): string`; `fence(text)`; `createTasksLane(brain, o?: TasksLaneOptions): Promise<TasksLane | null>` (registers one `brain.onStop` stopper: the sweep, then the follower's streams, then the card updater's timers); `registerTasks: RegisterLane`.

```ts
    await lb.taps.tap('working', 'watch', tap(card, 'watch', 'p_kabir'), card);
    expect(sessions().at(-1)!.body.screen).toBe('view');
    fake.controlAvailable = false;
  });

  it('stops a live job from its card, and refuses a finished one or one from another trip', async () => {
    const { id, card } = await job('turn_s1');
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('working', 'stop', tap(card, 'stop'), card)).toEqual({ result: 'done', toast: 'Stopping it' });
    expect((await lane.store.get(id))?.state).toBe('stopped');
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('working', 'stop', tap(card, 'stop'), card)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That job has already finished' });
    expect(await lb.taps.tap('working', 'watch', { ...tap(card, 'watch'), trip_id: 't_other' }, card)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That job is not here any more' });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/tools.ts
/**
 * The composer's job tools. start_task hands a big job to the trip's computer; its working card goes out with the
 * turn's message ("On it" and the card together), and the job's answer comes back later as a desk item.
 * message_task passes words to a running job (an answer to its question, a code for its hand-off, or more to do),
 * stop_task stops it, and settle_handoff says the person did the sign-in themselves, or could not.
 */
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { QuickTool } from '../composer/tools.js';
import type { Trip } from '../door/methods.js';
import { RETURN_KINDS, returnsSchema } from './returns.js';
import { TaskError, type TasksService } from './tasks.js';

const TaskId = z.string().regex(/^gt_[0-9a-z]{6,40}$/, 'A job id from the jobs list, like gt_...');

const StartArgs = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3).max(80).describe('What the working card says, e.g. "Finding villas with a pool in North Goa"'),
  goal: z.string().min(10).max(4000).describe('The whole job in plain words, with every constraint people gave'),
  returns: z.enum(RETURN_KINDS).describe('What comes back: options of one kind, or "answer" for a written answer'),
  steps: z.array(z.string().min(2).max(60)).min(1).max(6).optional().describe('Your plan in 2 to 6 short steps, shown on the card until the job writes its own'),
  context: z
    .record(z.string(), z.union([z.string().max(500), z.number(), z.boolean(), z.array(z.string().max(200)).max(20)]))
    .optional()
    .describe('Only the trip facts the job needs'),
  success_criteria: z.string().min(5).max(500).optional(),
  asked_by: z.string().min(1).max(80).optional().describe('The name of the person who asked'),
});
const MessageArgs = z.object({ task_id: TaskId, text: z.string().min(1).max(2000) });
const StopArgs = z.object({ task_id: TaskId });
const HandoffArgs = z.object({ task_id: TaskId, outcome: z.enum(['done', 'could_not', 'not_now']) });

function personNamed(trip: Trip, name: string): string | null {
  const want = name.trim().toLowerCase();
  const exact = trip.people.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase() === want);
  if (exact.length === 1) return exact[0]!.person_id;
  const starts = trip.people.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().startsWith(want));
  return starts.length === 1 ? starts[0]!.person_id : null;
}

async function asAnswer(run: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await run();
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof TaskError) return { error: err.code, note: err.message };
    throw err;
  }
}

export function taskTools(d: { tasks: TasksService }): QuickTool<any>[] {
  const startTask: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof StartArgs>> = {
    name: 'start_task',
    description:
      "Hands a big job to the trip's computer: anything that needs websites, many steps, long reading or comparing, or more than about 10 seconds. " +
      'Never for booking or paying. The working card goes out with your message: say one short line, like "On it."',
    input: StartArgs,
    async run(a, ctx) {
      const trip = ctx.pack.trip;
      const askedBy = a.asked_by ? personNamed(trip, a.asked_by) : null;
      const context = { ...(a.context ?? {}), trip: { destination: trip.destination?.name ?? null, dates: trip.dates ?? null, people: trip.people.length } };
      const r = await d.tasks.start(ctx.tripId, {
        goal: a.goal,
        returns: returnsSchema(a.returns),
        context,
        title: a.title,
        returnsKind: a.returns,
        key: ctx.stableId('task'),
        cardId: ctx.stableId('c_work'),
        cards: ctx.cards,
        ...(a.steps ? { steps: a.steps } : {}),
        ...(a.success_criteria ? { successCriteria: a.success_criteria } : {}),
        ...(askedBy ? { askedBy } : {}),
      });
      const state = (await d.tasks.running(ctx.tripId)).find((t) => t.id === r.taskId)?.state;
      return state === 'queued'
        ? { task_id: r.taskId, card_id: r.cardId, queued: true, note: 'Three jobs are already running here; this one starts when one finishes. Say so in one short line.' }
        : { task_id: r.taskId, card_id: r.cardId, note: 'The working card goes out with your message. Say one short line, like "On it." Do not list the steps.' };
    },
  };
  const messageTask: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof MessageArgs>> = {
    name: 'message_task',
    description: "Passes a person's words to a running job: the answer to its question, a one-time code for its hand-off, or more to do. Use the job's id from the jobs list.",
    input: MessageArgs,
    run: (a) => asAnswer(async () => {
      await d.tasks.message(a.task_id, a.text);
      return { ok: true, note: 'Passed on. Say so only if someone is waiting to hear it.' };
    }),
  };
  const stopTask: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof StopArgs>> = {
    name: 'stop_task',
    description: 'Stops a running job. Its card says Stopped and it posts no results.',
    input: StopArgs,
    run: (a) => asAnswer(async () => {
      await d.tasks.stop(a.task_id);
      return { ok: true };
    }),
  };
  const settleHandoff: QuickTool<z.infer<typeof HandoffArgs>> = {
    name: 'settle_handoff',
    description: 'Tells a job waiting on a person that they did it themselves ("done"), could not ("could_not"), or will later ("not_now").',
    input: HandoffArgs,
    run: (a) => asAnswer(async () => {
      await d.tasks.handoff(a.task_id, a.outcome);
      return { ok: true };
    }),
  };
  return [startTask, messageTask, stopTask, settleHandoff];
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/taps.ts
/**
 * The working card's buttons. Watch mints a fresh 15-minute session on the trip's screen for every tap and answers it
 * as "open" (a link is never posted in the chat); it asks for control only for the person a hand-off waits on, and
 * falls back to a view when /v1 has shared control off. Stop ends a live job.
 */
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import { LIVE_STATES, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import type { TasksService } from './tasks.js';
import { V1Error, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';

export interface TaskTapDeps {
  store: TaskStore;
  tasks: Pick<TasksService, 'stop'>;
  v1: V1Client;
  userIdFor: (tripId: string) => string;
  log: { warn(obj: object, msg: string): void };
}

async function openSession(v1: V1Client, userId: string, conversationId: string | null, screen: 'view' | 'control'): Promise<{ url: string; expires_at: string }> {
  try {
    return await v1.call<{ url: string; expires_at: string }>('POST', '/v1/sessions', {
      body: { user_id: userId, ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}), screen },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2_000),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (screen === 'control' && err instanceof V1Error && err.code === 'screen_control_unavailable') return openSession(v1, userId, conversationId, 'view');
    throw err;
  }
}

export function registerTaskTaps(taps: Brain['taps'], d: TaskTapDeps): void {
  taps.on('working', 'watch', async (tap, card) => {
    const row = card.kind === 'working' ? await d.store.get(card.task_id) : null;
    if (!row || row.tripId !== tap.trip_id) return { result: 'refused', toast: 'That job is not here any more' };
    const control = row.state === 'needs_you' && row.needsPersonId === tap.person_id;
    try {
      const s = await openSession(d.v1, d.userIdFor(row.tripId), row.conversationId, control ? 'control' : 'view');
      return { result: 'open', url: s.url, expires_at: s.expires_at };
    } catch (err) {
      d.log.warn({ err, taskId: row.id }, 'tasks: a Watch session did not open');
      return { result: 'refused', toast: "Can't open Go's screen right now" };
    }
  });

  taps.on('working', 'stop', async (tap, card) => {
    const row = card.kind === 'working' ? await d.store.get(card.task_id) : null;
    if (!row || row.tripId !== tap.trip_id) return { result: 'refused', toast: 'That job is not here any more' };
    if (!LIVE_STATES.includes(row.state)) return { result: 'refused', toast: 'That job has already finished' };
    await d.tasks.stop(row.id);
    return { result: 'done', toast: 'Stopping it' };
  });
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/context.ts
/**
 * How jobs read to the composer: the "tasks" block of right now (running jobs with their step, and the last few that
 * ended), and the "task" wake lines. Anything a job wrote (its steps, its summary, its questions) came from websites,
 * so it is fenced as data and cannot close its fence.
 */
import type { Brain } from '../app.js';
import type { NowProvider } from '../composer/context.js';
import type { DeskItem } from '../desk/desk.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../trips/pack.js';
import type { TaskRow, TaskStore } from './store.js';
import type { TaskWake } from './tasks.js';

const RECENT_MS = 6 * 3_600_000;

export function fence(text: string): string {
  return `<job_output>${text.replace(/</g, '‹').replace(/>/g, '›').slice(0, 1500)}</job_output>`;
}

function ago(at: Date | null, now: Date): string {
  if (!at) return 'just now';
  const minutes = Math.floor((now.getTime() - at.getTime()) / 60_000);
  return minutes < 1 ? 'just now' : minutes < 60 ? `${minutes} min ago` : `${Math.floor(minutes / 60)} h ago`;
}

function liveLine(r: TaskRow, now: Date, nameOf: (id: string) => string): string {
  switch (r.state) {
    case 'queued':
      return 'waiting to start (three jobs are running)';
    case 'starting':
      return "starting on the trip's computer";
    case 'needs_you':
      return `waiting on ${r.needsPersonId ? nameOf(r.needsPersonId) : 'someone'}`;
    default: {
      const at = r.steps.findIndex((s) => s.state === 'doing');
      const step = at >= 0 ? `, step ${at + 1} of ${r.steps.length}: ${fence(r.steps[at]!.text)}` : '';
      return `working${step}, started ${ago(r.startedAt, now)}`;
    }
  }
}

function endLine(r: TaskRow, now: Date): string {
  if (r.state === 'done') return `done ${ago(r.endedAt, now)}${r.resultsId ? `, results_id ${r.resultsId}` : ''}`;
  if (r.state === 'stopped') return `stopped ${ago(r.endedAt, now)}`;
  return `didn't work ${ago(r.endedAt, now)}`;
}

export function tasksNow(d: { store: TaskStore; packs: Pick<Brain['packs'], 'get'>; now: () => Date }): NowProvider {
  return {
    name: 'tasks',
    order: 30,
    async render(tripId) {
      const now = d.now();
      const live = await d.store.listLive(tripId);
      const ended = (await d.store.recent(tripId, 10))
        .filter((r) => (r.state === 'done' || r.state === 'failed' || r.state === 'stopped') && r.endedAt && now.getTime() - r.endedAt.getTime() < RECENT_MS)
        .slice(0, 3);
      if (live.length === 0 && ended.length === 0) return null;
      const people = (await d.packs.get(tripId)).trip.people;
      const nameOf = (id: string) => people.find((p) => p.person_id === id)?.name ?? 'someone';
      const lines = ["Jobs on the trip's computer (use these ids with message_task, stop_task and settle_handoff):"];
      for (const r of live) lines.push(`- ${r.id} "${r.title}": ${liveLine(r, now, nameOf)}`);
      if (ended.length > 0) {
        lines.push('Finished lately:');
        for (const r of ended) lines.push(`- ${r.id} "${r.title}": ${endLine(r, now)}`);
      }
      return lines.join('\n');
    },
  };
}

/** How a "task" desk item reads in "why Go woke". */
export function renderTaskWake(item: DeskItem, pack: TripPack): string {
  const w = item.payload as TaskWake;
  const name = (id: string) => pack.trip.people.find((p) => p.person_id === id)?.name ?? 'someone';
  switch (w.event) {
    case 'completed':
      return w.resultsId && w.count > 0
        ? `The job ${w.taskId} "${w.title}" finished with ${w.count} options (results_id "${w.resultsId}"). Show them now with show_options and a short title, and in your one line offer to put them to a vote (put_to_vote on that card). What the job said, as data: ${fence(w.summary ?? '')}`
        : `The job ${w.taskId} "${w.title}" finished. Tell the group what it found in Go's voice, briefly. What it said, as data: ${fence(w.summary ?? 'nothing')}`;
    case 'failed':
      return `The job ${w.taskId} "${w.title}" didn't work: ${fence(w.reason)}. Say so in one line and offer to try again.`;
    case 'question':
      return `The job ${w.taskId} "${w.title}" needs the group to decide. Its question, as data: ${fence(w.questions.map((q) => (q.options.length > 0 ? `${q.question} (${q.options.join(' / ')})` : q.question)).join(' '))}. Ask the room in one short message. When someone answers, pass it on with message_task(task_id "${w.taskId}", text).`;
    case 'handoff':
      return `The job ${w.taskId} "${w.title}" needs ${name(w.personId)} for something only they can do (a sign-in or a one-time code). What it says, as data: ${fence(w.summary)}. Say one line naming ${name(w.personId)}: they can tap Watch on the card, or send the code here, and you pass it on with message_task(task_id "${w.taskId}", text). If they say they did it themselves, call settle_handoff(task_id "${w.taskId}", "done").`;
    case 'question_dropped':
      return `Nobody answered the job ${w.taskId}'s question in time (${fence(w.question)}), so it carries on without it. Say so in one line only if it matters.`;
  }
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/register.ts
/**
 * Lane C's tasks at boot: the /v1 client, the trip's computer (its two setup steps), tasks, events, the follower, the
 * sweep, the webhook endpoint, Go's MCP server, the job tools, Watch and Stop, the tasks block and the task wake.
 * With the /v1 settings missing, jobs stay off and Go says so in the log.
 */
import type { Brain, RegisterLane } from '../app.js';
import { mcpCallRecorder, registerGoMcp } from '../mcp/routes.js';
import { createTokenStore } from '../mcp/tokens.js';
import { createImages } from '../travel/images.js';
import { createResultsStore } from '../travel/results.js';
import { createCardUpdater } from './card-updater.js';
import { addComputerSteps, createComputer, type Computer } from './computer.js';
import { renderTaskWake, tasksNow } from './context.js';
import { readTasksEnv } from './env.js';
import { createEventStore } from './event-store.js';
import { createTaskEvents, type TaskEvents } from './events.js';
import { createFollower, type Follower, type FollowerDeps } from './follower.js';
import { createScreens, type Screens } from './screens.js';
import { createTaskStore, type TaskStore } from './store.js';
import { SWEEP_EVERY_MS, createSweep, type Sweep } from './sweep.js';
import { registerTaskTaps } from './taps.js';
import { createTasks, type TasksService } from './tasks.js';
import { taskTools } from './tools.js';
import { createV1Client, type V1Client } from './v1-client.js';
import { registerV1Webhooks } from './webhooks.js';

export interface TasksLaneOptions {
  /** 0 turns the timed sweep off (tests run it by hand). */
  sweepEveryMs?: number;
  cardGapMs?: number;
  follower?: Partial<Pick<FollowerDeps, 'reconnectMs' | 'quietMs' | 'pollMs' | 'screenMs'>>;
}

export interface TasksLane {
  tasks: TasksService;
  events: TaskEvents;
  follower: Follower;
  sweep: Sweep;
  screens: Screens;
  computer: Computer;
  v1: V1Client;
  store: TaskStore;
}

export async function createTasksLane(brain: Brain, o: TasksLaneOptions = {}): Promise<TasksLane | null> {
  const env = readTasksEnv(brain.env);
  if (!env) {
    brain.log.warn('tasks: VESTRA_API_URL, VESTRA_API_KEYS, VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY, VESTRA_WEBHOOK_SECRET and VESTRA_DEPLOYMENT_ID are not all set, so jobs are off');
    return null;
  }
  const v1 = createV1Client({ baseUrl: env.VESTRA_API_URL, keys: env.VESTRA_API_KEYS, adminKey: env.VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY });
  const tokens = createTokenStore(brain.pool, brain.now);
  const computer = createComputer({ pool: brain.pool, v1, tokens, env, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });
  addComputerSteps(brain.setup, computer);
  const store = createTaskStore(brain.pool);
  const eventStore = createEventStore(brain.pool, brain.now);
  const results = createResultsStore(brain.pool);
  const images = createImages({ pool: brain.pool, publicUrl: env.GO_PUBLIC_URL });
  const cards = createCardUpdater({ link: brain.door.link, log: brain.log, ...(o.cardGapMs !== undefined ? { gapMs: o.cardGapMs } : {}) });
  let following: Follower | null = null;
  const follow = (conversationId: string, tripId: string): void => following?.ensure(conversationId, tripId);
  const tasks = createTasks({ store, v1, computer, cards, link: brain.door.link, desk: brain.desk, packs: brain.packs, now: brain.now, log: brain.log, follow });
  const events = createTaskEvents({ store, tasks, v1, desk: brain.desk, results, packs: brain.packs, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });
  const screens = createScreens({ v1, store, images, tasks, userIdFor: computer.userIdFor, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });
  const follower = createFollower({ v1, store, streams: eventStore, apply: events.apply, screens, log: brain.log, ...(o.follower ?? {}) });
  following = follower;
  const sweep = createSweep({ v1, store, eventStore, tasks, events, computer, follow, desk: brain.desk, packs: brain.packs, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });

  await registerV1Webhooks(brain.http, { secrets: env.VESTRA_WEBHOOK_SECRET, events: eventStore, apply: events.apply, now: brain.now, log: brain.log });
  await registerGoMcp(brain.http, {
    packs: brain.packs,
    memory: () => brain.memory,
    travel: () => brain.travel,
    results,
    record: mcpCallRecorder(brain.pool, (tripId) => store.activeIds(tripId), brain.now),
    tokens,
    log: brain.log,
  });
  for (const t of taskTools({ tasks })) brain.tools.register(t);
  registerTaskTaps(brain.taps, { store, tasks, v1, userIdFor: computer.userIdFor, log: brain.log });
  brain.context.addNow(tasksNow({ store, packs: brain.packs, now: brain.now }));
  brain.context.addWake('task', renderTaskWake);
  sweep.start(o.sweepEveryMs ?? SWEEP_EVERY_MS);
  // When Go stops (built.stop()): no new sweep, every stream and wait aborted, the last card updates sent.
  brain.onStop(async () => {
    await sweep.stop();
    await follower.close();
    await cards.flush();
  });
  brain.tasks = tasks;
  return { tasks, events, follower, sweep, screens, computer, v1, store };
}

export const registerTasks: RegisterLane = async (brain) => {
  await createTasksLane(brain);
};
```

In `apps/brain/src/main.ts`, add `import { registerTasks } from './tasks/register.js';` beside the other lane imports, and put `registerTasks` in `LANES` right after `registerTravel`, for example:

---

### Task 20: End to end: the villa job, and what goes wrong

The G3 run: P1's brain with the travel and tasks lanes on a real port, `@go/sim`'s `FakeServer` playing the Server, P1's scripted model playing the composer's model, and the fake `/v1` playing Vestra. "Go, find a villa..." becomes "On it" with a working card that ticks; Watch opens the screen; a one-time code typed in the chat reaches the job; the job searches Go's stays through Go's MCP server with its trip token (the test plays that call, as the real job makes it); the villas come back as an options card with "Put it to a vote", each option answering `travel.offer` with the offer the job picked at its whole-stay price. Then what goes wrong (master §10 item 4): a job stopped from its card never posts results, a finish that came by no stream and no webhook is found by the sweep, a cut stream is resumed from its last event, and a hand-off nobody answers ends as "Didn't work". Go's clock (shared by the fake `/v1` and the sim) runs with real time plus an offset, so the desk's waits work and the test can jump ten minutes.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/tasks/e2e.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `buildBrain`, `Built` (`app.ts`, with `brain.travel`), `withSchema`, `loadEnv`, `registerRoom` (`room.ts`), `fakeAnthropic`, `Reply` (`__tests__/fixtures/fake-anthropic.ts`), `SECRET`, `TEST_TIMINGS`, `rightNow`, `newSince`, `afterTools` (`__tests__/e2e/harness.ts`); `armedToken`, `searchAsTheJob` (Task 13); P0 `FakeServer` (`new FakeServer({ goUrl, secret, instance, now })`, `start(timeoutMs)`, `stop()`, `createTrip`, `say`, `tap(cardId, actionId, personId)`, `waitFor(check, timeoutMs)`, `messages(tripId)`) and `goaFreshTrip()` from `@go/sim`; `registerTravel` (Task 8); `createTasksLane` (Task 19); `FakeV1` with `cutStreams` (Task 10); `HARNESS_KEYS`, `HARNESS_ADMIN` (Task 16); `eventually` (Task 1).
- Produces: nothing new; this is the gate's test.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
// apps/brain/src/tasks/e2e.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import { FakeServer, goaFreshTrip } from '@go/sim';
import { afterTools, newSince, rightNow, SECRET, TEST_TIMINGS } from '../__tests__/e2e/harness.js';
import { fakeAnthropic, type Reply } from '../__tests__/fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import { buildBrain, type Built } from '../app.js';
import { withSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import { loadEnv } from '../env.js';
import { registerRoom } from '../room.js';
import { registerTravel } from '../travel/register.js';
import { createTasksLane, type TasksLane } from './register.js';
import { FakeV1 } from './testing/fake-v1.js';
import { HARNESS_ADMIN, HARNESS_KEYS } from './testing/harness.js';
import { armedToken, searchAsTheJob } from './testing/job-search.js';
import { eventually } from './testing/lane-brain.js';

const WEBHOOK_SECRET = 'whsec_e2e_0123456789abcdef';
const TRIP = 't_goa';
/** The job's search, as its instructions ask: Go's search_stays for the trip's dates and people, pools only. */
const STAY_SEARCH = { area: 'North Goa', check_in: '2026-11-06', check_out: '2026-11-09', guests: 5, must_have: ['pool'] };
/** What the job returns: three of the villas Go's search showed, each with its offer_id and whole-stay price. */
const VILLAS = {
  summary: 'Three villas with a pool near the beach.',
  options: [
    { title: 'Villa Sol', area: 'Anjuna', price_minor: 1280000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3840000, highlights: ['Private pool', '8 min to the beach'], rating: 4.7, offer_id: 'fake:st-villa-sol-anjuna-20261106-20261109-5', why: 'Pool and the beach close by.' },
    { title: 'The Palms Villa', area: 'Vagator', price_minor: 1220000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3660000, highlights: ['Private pool'], rating: 4.5, offer_id: 'fake:st-the-palms-vagator-20261106-20261109-5', why: 'Closest to Vagator beach.' },
    { title: 'Ocean Breeze Villa', area: 'Candolim', price_minor: 1310000, price_note: 'per night', total_paise: 3930000, highlights: ['Private pool'], rating: 4.3, offer_id: 'fake:st-ocean-breeze-candolim-20261106-20261109-5', why: 'Three minutes from the sand.' },
  ],
};

/** The composer's model, scripted on what each request asks. */
function goScript(body: any): Reply {
  const now = rightNow(body);
  const fresh = newSince(body);
  if (afterTools(body)) {
    if (now.includes('finished with')) return { text: 'Three villas that fit. Want a vote?' };
    if (fresh.includes('find a villa')) return { text: 'On it. Looking for villas with a pool in North Goa.' };
    return { tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'done' } }] };
  }
  if (now.includes('you just joined this trip')) return { text: 'Hi all, I am Go.' };
  const results = /results_id "([^"]+)"/.exec(now)?.[1];
  if (results && now.includes('finished with')) return { tools: [{ name: 'show_options', input: { results_id: results, title: 'Villas with a pool in North Goa' } }] };
  if (now.includes("didn't work")) return { text: "That didn't work. Want me to try again?" };
  if (now.includes('needs Meher for something only they can do')) return { text: 'Meher, the villa site wants a one-time code. Send it here, or tap Watch on the card.' };
  const code = /\b(\d{6})\b/.exec(fresh)?.[1];
  const job = /\b(gt_[0-9a-z]{6,40})\b/.exec(now)?.[1];
  if (code && job) return { tools: [{ name: 'message_task', input: { task_id: job, text: code } }] };
  if (fresh.includes('find a villa')) {
    return {
      tools: [{
        name: 'start_task',
        input: {
          title: 'Finding villas with a pool in North Goa',
          goal: "Find villas with a pool in North Goa for 6 to 9 Nov for 5 people, under 8,000 rupees a head, near the beach. Use Go's search_stays first.",
          returns: 'stays',
          steps: ['Search stays in North Goa', 'Keep villas with a pool', 'Read the reviews', 'Pick the best three'],
          context: { area: 'North Goa', budget_per_head_inr: 8000, must_have: ['pool', 'near the beach'] },
          asked_by: 'Meher',
        },
      }],
    };
  }
  return { tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'chatter' } }] };
}

let offset = 0;
const now = (): Date => new Date(Date.now() + offset);
let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let fake: FakeV1;
let built: Built;
let lane: TasksLane;
let server: FakeServer;
let goUrl = '';

const goMessages = (): C.Message[] => server.messages(TRIP).filter((m) => m.author.kind === 'go');
const working = (): C.Message[] => goMessages().filter((m) => m.card?.kind === 'working');
const options = (): C.Message[] => goMessages().filter((m) => m.card?.kind === 'options');
const cardOf = (cardId: string): any => goMessages().find((m) => m.card?.card_id === cardId)?.card;

/** Asks for a new villa job and waits for its working card and its /v1 task. */
async function newJob(personId: string, text: string): Promise<{ cardId: string; v1TaskId: string; message: C.Message }> {
  const cardsBefore = working().length;
  const tasksBefore = fake.tasks.size;
  await server.say(personId, text);
  const message = await server.waitFor(() => (working().length > cardsBefore ? working().at(-1)! : null), 20_000);
  await eventually(() => fake.tasks.size > tasksBefore, 10_000);
  const v1TaskId = fake.lastTask()!.id;
  return { cardId: message.card!.card_id, v1TaskId, message };
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  fake = new FakeV1({ keys: HARNESS_KEYS, adminKey: HARNESS_ADMIN, now });
  await fake.start();
  const env = loadEnv({
    PORT: '0', DATABASE_URL: process.env.DATABASE_URL, GO_SIGNING_SECRET: SECRET, GO_PUBLIC_URL: 'https://go.test', ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: 'test-key', LOG_LEVEL: 'silent',
    TRAVEL_MODE: 'fake', VESTRA_API_URL: fake.url, VESTRA_API_KEYS: HARNESS_KEYS.join(','), VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY: HARNESS_ADMIN,
    VESTRA_WEBHOOK_SECRET: WEBHOOK_SECRET, VESTRA_DEPLOYMENT_ID: 'dep_go', VESTRA_MCP_SERVER_ID: 'mcp_go',
  });
  const model = fakeAnthropic(goScript);
  built = await buildBrain(env, { pool: db.pool, anthropic: model.client, logger: false, timings: TEST_TIMINGS, now });
  await registerRoom(built.brain);
  await registerTravel(built.brain);
  lane = (await createTasksLane(built.brain, { sweepEveryMs: 0, cardGapMs: 0, follower: { reconnectMs: [20, 50], screenMs: 60_000 } }))!;
  const { port } = await built.start();
  goUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
  // The sim shares Go's clock, so signatures still check after the test jumps ten minutes.
  server = new FakeServer({ goUrl, secret: SECRET, instance: 'p4-e2e', now });
  await server.start(10_000);
  await server.createTrip(goaFreshTrip());
  await server.waitFor(() => (goMessages().length > 0 ? true : null), 20_000);
  await eventually(() => fake.users.size > 0, 10_000);
}, 60_000);

afterAll(async () => {
  server.stop();
  await built.stop();
  await fake.close();
  await db.drop();
});

describe('G3: Go does real work', () => {
  it('turns "find a villa" into a job: "On it" with a working card that ticks, Watch, a code from the chat, and the villas as an options card', async () => {
    const { cardId, v1TaskId, message } = await newJob('p_meher', 'Go, find a villa with a pool in North Goa under 8k a head, near the beach');
    expect(message.text).toBe('On it. Looking for villas with a pool in North Goa.');
    expect(message.card).toMatchObject({ kind: 'working', state: 'running', steps: [{ text: 'Search stays in North Goa', state: 'doing' }, { text: 'Keep villas with a pool', state: 'todo' }, { text: 'Read the reviews', state: 'todo' }, { text: 'Pick the best three', state: 'todo' }] });
    const handed = fake.callsTo('POST', /\/messages$/).at(-1)!;
    expect(handed.body).toMatchObject({ hand_off: 'always', output_schema: { required: ['summary', 'options'] }, context: { area: 'North Goa', trip: { people: 5 } } });

    fake.progress(v1TaskId, [['Search stays in North Goa', 'completed'], ['Keep villas with a pool', 'in_progress']]);
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.steps?.some((s: { text: string; state: string }) => s.text === 'Keep villas with a pool' && s.state === 'doing') ? true : null), 10_000);

    const watch = await server.tap(cardId, 'watch', 'p_priya');
    const again = await server.tap(cardId, 'watch', 'p_priya');
    expect(watch).toMatchObject({ result: 'open', url: expect.stringMatching(/^https:\/\//) });
    expect((again as { url?: string }).url).not.toBe((watch as { url?: string }).url);
    expect(goMessages().some((m) => (m.text ?? '').includes('web.vestra.test'))).toBe(false);

    const ask = fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'handoff', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'The villa site sent a one-time code to Meher', browser: { suggested_mode: 'control', url: null } });
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'needs_you' ? true : null), 10_000);
    expect(cardOf(cardId)?.for_person_ids).toEqual(['p_meher']);
    await server.waitFor(() => goMessages().find((m) => (m.text ?? '').startsWith('Meher, the villa site')) ?? null, 20_000);
    await server.say('p_meher', 'the code is 482913');
    await eventually(() => fake.interactions.get(ask.id)?.status === 'resolved', 20_000);
    expect(fake.interactions.get(ask.id)?.answer).toEqual({ answers: ['482913'] });
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'running' ? true : null), 10_000);

    // The job searches Go's stays with the trip token Go armed for it; Go keeps what it showed for the trip.
    const searched = await searchAsTheJob({ goUrl, token: armedToken(fake, TRIP), tool: 'search_stays', args: STAY_SEARCH });
    for (const v of VILLAS.options) expect(searched.results.find((r) => r.offer_id === v.offer_id)?.total_paise, v.title).toBe(v.total_paise);

    const optionsBefore = options().length;
    fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found three villas.');
    const shown = await server.waitFor(() => (options().length > optionsBefore ? options().at(-1)! : null), 20_000);
    expect(shown.text).toBe('Three villas that fit. Want a vote?');
    const card = shown.card as Extract<C.Card, { kind: 'options' }>;
    expect(card.options.map((o) => o.title)).toEqual(['Villa Sol', 'The Palms Villa', 'Ocean Breeze Villa']);
    expect(card.actions?.map((a) => a.action_id)).toEqual(['pick', 'vote']);
    // Bookable end to end: each option is the offer the job picked, at the whole-stay price a vote and a payment use.
    const offers = await Promise.all(card.options.map((o) => built.brain.travel!.offer(o.option_id)));
    expect(offers.map((o) => [o?.offerId, o?.priceMinor])).toEqual(VILLAS.options.map((v) => [v.offer_id, v.total_paise]));
    expect(cardOf(cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'done', actions: [] });
  }, 120_000);

  it('a job stopped from its card says Stopped, and never posts results when /v1 finishes it anyway', async () => {
    const { cardId, v1TaskId } = await newJob('p_kabir', 'Go, find a villa in Morjim with a pool for us');
    expect(await server.tap(cardId, 'stop', 'p_kabir')).toMatchObject({ result: 'done' });
    await server.waitFor(() => (String(cardOf(cardId)?.fallback_text ?? '').startsWith('Stopped') ? true : null), 10_000);
    expect(fake.tasks.get(v1TaskId)?.stopped).toBe(true);
    const optionsBefore = options().length;
    fake.tasks.get(v1TaskId)!.status = 'working';
    fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found them anyway.');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2_000));
    expect(options()).toHaveLength(optionsBefore);
    expect(cardOf(cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'failed', actions: [] });
  }, 60_000);

  it('a finish that came by no stream and no webhook is found by the sweep', async () => {
    const { cardId, v1TaskId } = await newJob('p_priya', 'Go, find a villa in Assagao with a pool');
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'running' ? true : null), 10_000);
    const optionsBefore = options().length;
    fake.finishSilently(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found three villas.');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1_000));
    expect(options()).toHaveLength(optionsBefore);
    await lane.sweep.runOnce();
    await server.waitFor(() => (options().length > optionsBefore ? true : null), 20_000);
    expect(cardOf(cardId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'done' });
  }, 60_000);

  it('a dropped stream is reopened from the last event it saw, and nothing is missed or applied twice', async () => {
    const { cardId, v1TaskId } = await newJob('p_arjun', 'Go, find a villa in Siolim with a pool');
    const resumes = (): number => fake.callsTo('GET', /^\/v1\/conversations\/[^/]+\/events$/).filter((c) => c.headers['last-event-id'] !== undefined).length;
    const before = resumes();
    await eventually(() => fake.cutStreams() > 0, 5_000);
    fake.progress(v1TaskId, [['Search stays in North Goa', 'completed'], ['Read the reviews', 'in_progress']]);
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.steps?.some((s: { text: string; state: string }) => s.text === 'Read the reviews' && s.state === 'doing') ? true : null), 15_000);
    expect(resumes()).toBeGreaterThan(before);
    const optionsBefore = options().length;
    fake.complete(v1TaskId, VILLAS, 'Found three villas.');
    await server.waitFor(() => (options().length > optionsBefore ? true : null), 20_000);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1_000));
    expect(options()).toHaveLength(optionsBefore + 1);
  }, 60_000);

  it('a hand-off nobody answers ends as "Didn\'t work" after 10 minutes, unpinned, and Go says so', async () => {
    const { cardId, v1TaskId } = await newJob('p_meher', 'Go, find a villa in Vagator with a pool');
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'running' ? true : null), 10_000);
    const toMeher = (): number => goMessages().filter((m) => (m.text ?? '').startsWith('Meher, the villa site')).length;
    const linesBefore = toMeher();
    const ask = fake.ask(v1TaskId, { type: 'handoff', kind: 'ask_question', summary: 'Sign in to the villa site', browser: { suggested_mode: 'control', url: null } });
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'needs_you' ? true : null), 10_000);
    // Go has told Meher (its turn is over) before the clock jumps, so no turn is cut in half by the jump.
    await server.waitFor(() => (toMeher() > linesBefore ? true : null), 20_000);
    offset += 10.5 * 60_000;
    await lane.sweep.runOnce();
    await server.waitFor(() => (cardOf(cardId)?.state === 'failed' ? true : null), 10_000);
    expect(cardOf(cardId)).toMatchObject({ fallback_text: "Didn't work: Finding villas with a pool in North Goa", actions: [] });
    expect(cardOf(cardId)?.for_person_ids ?? undefined).toBeUndefined();
    expect(fake.interactions.get(ask.id)?.status).toBe('cancelled');
    await server.waitFor(() => goMessages().find((m) => m.text === "That didn't work. Want me to try again?") ?? null, 20_000);
  }, 60_000);
});
```

### Task 21: Bash PR 1: no Composio when a deployment allows no apps

In `vestra-ai/bash`. Today `apps.allow` is read only when a connect link is made (`POST /v1/connections`), never while a task runs, so a Go job could still ask to connect Gmail. When a deployment's `apps.allow` is empty, `buildDispatchPlatform` now appends `{ rule: 'mcp__composio__*', behavior: 'deny' }` to the task's permission rules (the rule engine already matches tool-name globs). About 4 lines and a 25-line test (master §8 item 1, `research/v1.md` §13a). PR 2 (Task 22) touches other files, so the two PRs can be built side by side.

**Files (in `vestra-ai/bash`, worktree `bash-worktrees/go-composio-deny`):**
- Modify: `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/dispatch-platform.ts` (the deny rule)
- Test: `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/dispatch-platform.test.ts` (one more `describe`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `buildDispatchPlatform`, `DeploymentManifest` (Bash).
- Produces: a task of a deployment with `apps.allow: []` carries the permission rule `mcp__composio__*` deny, after the company rules and the approval rules, once.

### Task 22: Bash PR 2: `deny_tools` on deployments

In `vestra-ai/bash`. Bash's house tools cannot be switched off for a company's work today. This adds `deny_tools: string[]` (optional) to `DeploymentManifest`, carries it in the task's platform context (with every Composio tool added when the deployment allows no apps), and reads that context at the TOP of `buildNativeToolList`, so denied tools are never built: not in the top-level list, not in the subagents' set, not in the Claude Code and Codex engines (which take their tools from the same function), and a deployment that denies Composio never opens the Composio session. PR 1's rule stays as the backstop (master §8 item 2, `research/v1.md` §13b).

`/v1` refuses a manifest key it does not know (`freezeManifest` is strict), so Go's setup script sends `deny_tools` only with `--deny-tools`, after this PR is live on staging.

**Files (in `vestra-ai/bash`, worktree `bash-worktrees/go-deny-tools`):**
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/platform/deployment.ts` (`deny_tools` on `DeploymentManifest`)
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/platform/task-context.ts` (`denyTools?: string[]` on `TaskPlatformContext`)
- Modify: `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/task-platform.ts` (`deniedToolsFor`, carried into the context)
- Create: `apps/sandbox/src/harness/deny-tools.ts`
- Modify: `apps/sandbox/src/harness/native-tools.ts` (the context read hoisted, the filters)
- Test: `packages/shared/src/platform/__tests__/deployment.test.ts` (one more `describe`)
- Test: `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/task-platform.test.ts` (one more `describe`, one import name)
- Test: `apps/sandbox/src/harness/__tests__/deny-tools.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/sandbox/src/harness/__tests__/native-tools.deny-tools.test.ts`
- Regenerate: `openapi/v1.json`, `apps/web/public/developers/v1.json`, `openapi/vestra.schema.json`, `apps/web/public/developers/vestra.schema.json`, `sdks/typescript/src/schema.d.ts`, `sdks/python/vestra/_generated/**`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DeploymentManifest`, `TaskPlatformContext`, `buildTaskPlatformContext`, `buildNativeToolList`, `getTaskPlatformContext`, `noteTaskPlatformContext`, `__resetTaskPlatformContextForTest` (Bash).
- Produces: `DeploymentManifest.deny_tools?: string[]` (names, or a name ending in `*`); `TaskPlatformContext.denyTools?: string[]`; `deniedToolsFor(manifest): string[]`; `makeToolDeny(patterns): ToolDeny { denies; keep; coversPrefix }`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeToolDeny } from '../deny-tools.js';

describe('makeToolDeny', () => {
  it('denies exact names and every name under a prefix ending in *', () => {
    const d = makeToolDeny(['Agent', 'mcp__composio__*']);
    expect(d.denies('Agent')).toBe(true);
    expect(d.denies('AgentTeams')).toBe(false);
    expect(d.denies('mcp__composio__GMAIL_SEND_EMAIL')).toBe(true);
    expect(d.denies('mcp_invoke')).toBe(false);
    expect([{ name: 'Bash' }, { name: 'Agent' }].filter(d.keep).map((t) => t.name)).toEqual(['Bash']);
  });

  it('says when a pattern covers a whole prefix, and denies nothing without patterns', () => {
    expect(makeToolDeny(['mcp__composio__*']).coversPrefix('mcp__composio__')).toBe(true);
    expect(makeToolDeny(['mcp__*']).coversPrefix('mcp__composio__')).toBe(true);
    expect(makeToolDeny(['mcp__composio__GMAIL_SEND_EMAIL']).coversPrefix('mcp__composio__')).toBe(false);
    expect(makeToolDeny(undefined).denies('Agent')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * A company task's deployment denies tools (Go, 5 Oct): they are never built, at the top level or for subagents, and
 * a deployment that denies Composio never opens the Composio session. Same set-up as native-tools.toolorder.test.ts.
 */
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { buildNativeToolList } from '../native-tools.js';
import { FakeTransport } from '../transport/transport.js';
import { buildOpenAIMcpServers } from '../openai-mcp.js';
import { __resetTaskPlatformContextForTest, noteTaskPlatformContext } from '../../platform/context.js';

const h = vi.hoisted(() => ({ subagentTools: [] as string[][] }));

vi.mock('../openai-mcp.js', () => ({
  buildOpenAIMcpServers: vi.fn(() => [
    {
      connect: async () => {},
      listTools: async () => [{ name: 'GMAIL_FETCH_EMAILS', description: 'fetch', inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: {} } }],
      callTool: async () => ({}),
    },
  ]),
}));

// The Agent tool is built as usual; the test only notes the subagent toolset it was handed.
vi.mock('../tools/index.js', async (importOriginal) => {
  const mod = await importOriginal<typeof import('../tools/index.js')>();
  return {
    ...mod,
    makeAgentTool: (opts: Parameters<typeof mod.makeAgentTool>[0]) => {
      h.subagentTools.push((opts.subagentTools ?? []).map((t) => t.name));
      return mod.makeAgentTool(opts);
    },
  };
});

const TASK = 'task-deny';
const CTX = { orgId: 'org-go', mode: 'test', memoryMode: 'vestra' } as const;

async function names(o: { mcp?: boolean } = {}): Promise<string[]> {
  const prev = process.env.MCP_SERVER_CONFIG;
  if (o.mcp) process.env.MCP_SERVER_CONFIG = '{"composio":{"url":"https://x"}}';
  else delete process.env.MCP_SERVER_CONFIG;
  try {
    const { tools } = await buildNativeToolList(
      { abilities: [], signal: undefined } as never,
      () => null,
      { gateTool: async () => ({ decision: 'allow' as const }) },
      () => 'uuid',
      async () => 'PAUSE',
      TASK,
      () => {},
      new FakeTransport([]),
      'test-model',
    );
    return tools.map((t) => t.name);
  } finally {
    if (prev === undefined) delete process.env.MCP_SERVER_CONFIG;
    else process.env.MCP_SERVER_CONFIG = prev;
  }
}

afterEach(() => {
  __resetTaskPlatformContextForTest();
  h.subagentTools.length = 0;
  vi.mocked(buildOpenAIMcpServers).mockClear();
});

describe('a deployment that denies tools (Go, 5 Oct)', () => {
  it('builds none of them, and keeps every other tool in its order', async () => {
    const all = await names();
    const denied = ['Agent', 'Weave', 'session_search', 'session_create', 'mcp__browser-control__computer_eval'];
    noteTaskPlatformContext(TASK, { ...CTX, denyTools: denied });
    const kept = await names();
    expect(kept).toEqual(all.filter((n) => !denied.includes(n)));
    expect(kept).toContain('Bash');
    expect(kept).toContain('WebFetch');
  });

  it("keeps them out of the subagents' set too", async () => {
    noteTaskPlatformContext(TASK, { ...CTX, denyTools: ['WebSearch'] });
    expect(await names()).not.toContain('WebSearch');
    expect(h.subagentTools.at(-1)).not.toContain('WebSearch');
    expect(h.subagentTools.at(-1)).toContain('WebFetch');
  });

  it('never opens Composio when every Composio tool is denied', async () => {
    noteTaskPlatformContext(TASK, { ...CTX, denyTools: ['mcp__composio__*'] });
    expect((await names({ mcp: true })).some((n) => n.startsWith('mcp__composio__'))).toBe(false);
    expect(buildOpenAIMcpServers).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('changes nothing for a task with no platform context', async () => {
    const plain = await names({ mcp: true });
    expect(plain).toContain('mcp__composio__GMAIL_FETCH_EMAILS');
    expect(plain).toContain('Agent');
  });
});
```

In `apps/server/src/platform/deployments/task-platform.ts`, add above `buildTaskPlatformContext`:

```ts
/** Go, 5 Oct: what a task's tool list leaves out: the deployment's deny_tools, and every Composio tool when it allows no apps. */
export function deniedToolsFor(manifest: DeploymentManifest): string[] {
  const allow = manifest.apps.allow;
  const noApps = Array.isArray(allow) && allow.length === 0;
  return [...new Set([...(manifest.deny_tools ?? []), ...(noApps ? ['mcp__composio__*'] : [])])];
}
```

and in `buildTaskPlatformContext`, after `const companyLayer = ...`:

```ts
  const denyTools = manifest ? deniedToolsFor(manifest) : [];
```

and as the last entry of the returned object, after the `pins` line:

```ts
    ...(denyTools.length > 0 ? { denyTools } : {}),
```

Create `apps/sandbox/src/harness/deny-tools.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The tools a company task's deployment denies (TaskPlatformContext.denyTools): exact names, or a name ending in * for
 * every tool that starts with it. Denied tools are never built into the task's catalog, so the model never sees them
 * (Go, 5 Oct).
 */
export interface ToolDeny {
  denies(name: string): boolean;
  /** For Array.filter: keeps a tool the deployment does not deny. */
  keep: (tool: { name: string }) => boolean;
  /** True when one pattern denies every tool whose name starts with this prefix. */
  coversPrefix(prefix: string): boolean;
}

export function makeToolDeny(patterns: readonly string[] | undefined): ToolDeny {
  const exact = new Set<string>();
  const prefixes: string[] = [];
  for (const p of patterns ?? []) {
    if (p.endsWith('*')) prefixes.push(p.slice(0, -1));
    else exact.add(p);
  }
  const denies = (name: string): boolean => exact.has(name) || prefixes.some((pre) => name.startsWith(pre));
  return {
    denies,
    keep: (tool) => !denies(tool.name),
    coversPrefix: (prefix) => prefixes.some((pre) => prefix.startsWith(pre)),
  };
}
```

### Task 23: Mapping ixigo's real tools (the day we get access)

ixigo's tool names, argument shapes and answers are unknown until ixigo shares them. Everything that knows them is `IXIGO_MAP` in `travel/ixigo-map.ts`; this task adds a read-only probe that lists ixigo's tools and tries every search the map has (flights and stays; trains and buses too once Task 8b maps them), and the checklist for that day.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/travel/ixigo-probe.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/scripts/ixigo-probe.ts`

- Produces: `probeIxigo(caller, map): Promise<ProbeReport>`, `PROBE_QUERIES`, `ProbeReport`.
