- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```ts
// apps/brain/src/travel/ixigo-probe.ts
/**
 * The day we get ixigo's MCP access: list its tools with their schemas and hints, and try each search Go maps, so the
 * mapping table (IXIGO_MAP in ixigo-map.ts) can be filled in from facts. Read-only by construction: it calls only a
 * tool the server marks readOnlyHint, and never a hold, a confirm or a cancel.
 */
import type { IxigoMap } from './ixigo-map.js';
import type { McpCaller, McpToolInfo } from './mcp-caller.js';
import type { TravelKind, TravelQuery } from './travel.js';

export const PROBE_QUERIES: Record<TravelKind, TravelQuery> = {
  flights: { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' },
  trains: { from: 'Delhi', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' },
  buses: { from: 'Mumbai', to: 'Goa', date: '2026-11-06' },
  stays: { area: 'North Goa', checkIn: '2026-11-06', checkOut: '2026-11-09', guests: 5 },
};

export interface ProbeReport {
  tools: McpToolInfo[];
  readOnly: string[];
  writes: string[];
  searches: Array<{ kind: TravelKind; tool: string; found: boolean; readOnly: boolean; ok: boolean; count: number | null; error: string | null; sample: unknown }>;
}

/** The first item of the answer's first list, so a person can see the raw shape. */
function sampleOf(data: unknown): unknown {
  if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
    for (const v of Object.values(data as Record<string, unknown>)) if (Array.isArray(v)) return v[0] ?? null;
  }
  return data;
}

export async function probeIxigo(caller: McpCaller, map: IxigoMap): Promise<ProbeReport> {
  const tools = await caller.listTools();
  const byName = new Map(tools.map((t) => [t.name, t]));
  const readOnly = tools.filter((t) => t.annotations?.readOnlyHint === true).map((t) => t.name);
  const writes = tools.filter((t) => t.annotations?.readOnlyHint !== true).map((t) => t.name);
  const searches: ProbeReport['searches'] = [];
  for (const kind of ['flights', 'trains', 'buses', 'stays'] as const) {
    const m = map.search[kind];
    // Trains and buses are mapped only once the stretch Task 8b lands; a kind the map leaves out is not tried.
    if (!m) continue;
    const tool = byName.get(m.tool);
    const isReadOnly = tool?.annotations?.readOnlyHint === true;
    if (!tool || !isReadOnly) {
      searches.push({ kind, tool: m.tool, found: Boolean(tool), readOnly: isReadOnly, ok: false, count: null, error: tool ? 'not marked read-only, so the probe does not call it' : 'no such tool', sample: null });
      continue;
    }
    const r = await caller.call(m.tool, m.args(PROBE_QUERIES[kind]) ?? {});
    let count: number | null = null;
    let error: string | null = r.isError ? r.text.slice(0, 300) : null;
    if (!r.isError) {
      try {
        count = m.results(r.data).length;
      } catch (err) {
        error = `It answered, but the mapping could not read it: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message.slice(0, 300) : 'unknown'}`;
      }
    }
    searches.push({ kind, tool: m.tool, found: true, readOnly: true, ok: error === null, count, error, sample: sampleOf(r.data) });
  }
  return { tools, readOnly, writes, searches };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/scripts/ixigo-probe.ts
/**
 * A read-only look at ixigo's real MCP server, run by hand:
 *   IXIGO_MCP_URL=https://... IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN=... pnpm --filter ./apps/brain ixigo-probe > ixigo-probe.json
 * Prints every tool (name, read-only or not, input schema) and tries every search IXIGO_MAP has.
 */
import { IXIGO_MAP } from '../src/travel/ixigo-map.js';
import { probeIxigo } from '../src/travel/ixigo-probe.js';
import { createHttpMcpCaller } from '../src/travel/mcp-caller.js';

const url = process.env.IXIGO_MCP_URL;
if (!url) {
  process.stderr.write('IXIGO_MCP_URL is not set\n');
  process.exit(1);
}
const token = process.env.IXIGO_MCP_TOKEN;
const caller = createHttpMcpCaller({ url, name: 'go-ixigo-probe', ...(token ? { token } : {}) });
const report = await probeIxigo(caller, IXIGO_MAP);
process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`);
await caller.close();
```

In `apps/brain/package.json` `"scripts"`, add:

```json
"ixigo-probe": "tsx scripts/ixigo-probe.ts"
```

## Interface notes for the conductor

1. **Settled in master §6.3: `Travel.offer(optionId)`.** P4 implements master's exact signature (`Promise<{ offerId: string | null; title; priceMinor: number | null; currency: string | null; kind: 'flight' | 'train' | 'bus' | 'stay'; link: string | null } | null>`, Task 6; `link` is null until ixigo's tools are mapped); the richer `StoredOffer` stays inside P4 (`ResultsStore.offer`). P5 resolves a vote's winner as `decision.options[i].option_ref` (always Go's `option_id`, never a travel offer id) → `brain.travel.offer(option_ref)` → `offerId`, null when the option came from a job's web browsing (P5 refuses those). `priceMinor` is the whole stay for a stay, as the vote shows it; the card shows the nightly price. Still open: `Travel.simulate` (optional, P4's addition). The two backends implement `TravelBackend`; `brain.travel` is the layer. Master's binding notes say the same: a vote made from an options card sets each option's `option_ref` to the card option's `option_id`.
1a. **Settled in master §6.3: `Hold.refundableUntil` and `confirm` safe to repeat** (F15, F18). The fake ixigo's refundable fares (villas with free cancellation, Air India flights) carry `refundable_until` on the hold; `IXIGO_MAP.hold` maps it when ixigo's answer has it (Task 4). Each backend keeps a hold's booking by hold id; `IxigoMap.bookingForHold` (optional, filled in Task 23 if ixigo has such a tool) looks the booking up before confirming again after a restart.
2. **Settled in master §6.3: `Tasks.handoff(taskId, outcome)`.** Still P4's own additions: `start`'s brief gains optional `title`, `steps`, `successCriteria`, `returnsKind`, `askedBy`, `key`, `cardId`, `cards`; `running()` answers `{ id, brief: <the card title>, state }` for Jev's question 4.
3. **Env (master §6.7).** P4 takes over P1's `travel/env.ts` and `tasks/env.ts` stubs (Tasks 8 and 11; no second file, no edit of `env.ts`) and adds every variable to `apps/brain/.env.example` in the same task, so P1's `env-example.test.ts` keeps passing. `TasksEnv` adds `VESTRA_ADMIN_KEY` (admin scope, same mode as the pool) and `VESTRA_MCP_SERVER_ID`. Two variables are deliberately not in `apps/brain/.env.example`, because Go's service never reads them: `VESTRA_SETUP_KEY` (read from the shell by `scripts/setup-v1.ts` once; an all-scoped key must never sit on the running service) and `FAKE_IXIGO_TOKEN` (the standalone fake ixigo's own bearer, Task 3). The pool keys need the runtime and setup scopes (`PUT /v1/secrets` and the MCP check are setup scope). `VESTRA_WEBHOOK_SECRET` may hold two comma-separated secrets during a roll. Keshav's list (master §4, Tue noon) becomes: two runtime+setup keys, one admin key, and one all-scoped key for the one-time setup script (the admin key will do if it is created all-scoped).
4. **Settled in master §6.3: `addWake` keeps a list per kind, renderers return `string | null`, the first non-null wins, P1's generic line is the fallback.** P4's `tap` renderer answers only `payload.source === 'options'` and returns null otherwise (no generic line of its own); the lane test kit's `FakeContext` keeps the same list rule.
5. **Go's image cache** (`GET /img/:file`, `createImages`) lives in `travel/images.ts`. P5 (ticket and pay images) and P6 can import it; it could move to a shared folder later.
6. **`apps/brain/package.json`** gains `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` pinned to `1.29.0`, dev `tsx` (P1 Task 17 adds it too: whichever lands first), and the scripts `fake-ixigo`, `setup-v1`, `ixigo-probe`.
7. **Working cards ride with the turn's message**, so background updates can reach `replace_card` before the post: the card updater tries a failed update once more after 3 s. If P1's `speak` could post `ctx.cards` right after a tool returns, P4 would not need the retry; nothing to change otherwise.
8. **Settled (P1's shared harness): P4's end-to-end test** (Task 20) uses P1's `buildBrain(env, { pool, anthropic, logger, timings, now })`, `registerRoom`, `fakeAnthropic`, and `SECRET`, `TEST_TIMINGS`, `rightNow`, `newSince`, `afterTools` from `src/__tests__/e2e/harness.ts`, plus P0's `FakeServer` as you described (`{ goUrl, secret, instance, now }`, `start`, `stop`, `tap(cardId, actionId, personId)`, `waitFor(check, timeoutMs)`, `messages(tripId)`). It assumes `messages()` shows Go's cards as `replace_card` left them, that `tap()` resolves with the `CardActionResult`, and that the sim signs and checks with the `now` it is given (the test jumps Go's clock ten minutes and passes the same clock to the sim). **Settled:** P1 now exports `apps/brain/src/testing/start-room.ts` (`startRoom`, built into `dist`, on P0's real `FakeServer`, with `LANES` registered; master §6.2's `testing/` row), and `src/__tests__/e2e/harness.ts` re-exports it, so Task 20's imports work unchanged. Task 20 keeps its own `buildBrain` wiring because it adds the fake `/v1` and test-only lane options (`createTasksLane(brain, { sweepEveryMs: 0, ... })`). The lane test kit (`tasks/testing/lane-brain.ts`) stays for P4's unit tests, which run without the socket, the composer or a model.
9. **The lane test kit** (`tasks/testing/lane-brain.ts`) builds a whole `Brain`; if P1 adds a required field to `Brain`, add it there. Done for master's `anthropic` (a stub that throws: lane C's unit tests never call the model), for `onStop` (collects into `stoppers`; `lb.stop()` runs them newest first, once, as `built.stop()` does) and for `Context`'s `setStory` and `addTurnRules` (no-ops in `FakeContext`).
9a. **Settled in master §6.3: `Brain.onStop(fn)`.** Every timer P4 keeps is `unref()`ed, and `registerTasks` registers one stopper (the sweep, then the follower's streams and waits, then the card updater's timers) (never a Fastify `onClose` hook); `registerTravel` registers one that closes the ixigo MCP connection when `IXIGO_MCP_URL` is set. The setup steps `computer.user` and `computer.warm` keep no timer: P1's setup pass retries them, and P1 stops that pass.
10. **Desk ids P4 uses:** `task:<task_id>:done`, `task:<task_id>:failed`, `task:<task_id>:ask:<interaction_id>`, `task:<task_id>:handoff:<interaction_id>`, `task:<task_id>:dropped:<interaction_id>`, `travel:pick:<tap_id>`, `travel:vote_failed:<tap_id>`. Out-of-turn writes use ids made from their trigger: `go_task_<task_id>_card`, and hashed `go_tap_...` ids for the vote a tap opens.
11. **One trip token at a time per trip user.** `TRIP_TOKEN` is one per-user secret, so each job start mints a fresh token and stores it (the earlier one stays valid in Go while its job runs); all of a trip's tokens are revoked when its last running job ends. MCP calls are logged against the trip and the jobs running at the time, since the token names the trip, not one job.
12. **One event stream per trip conversation**, not per job: 20 streams per key carry 20 busy trips per key.
13. **Not built here, decisions for Keshav (spec §19):** per-trip day and month caps on the trip users (`PATCH /v1/users/{id}`), org caps (`PATCH /v1/limits`), and giving a quiet trip's computer back (`status: disabled` after N idle days). The deployment's `spend_per_task_usd: 2` is the cap in place for the week.
14. **Settled in master §6.3 notes ("Big jobs keep booking possible").** A stay or travel job takes its inventory from Go's MCP search tools; every search Go's MCP server answers is saved as the trip's result set (Task 13), and each result carries `offer_id` (and, for a stay, `total_paise`, the whole stay). The job's `returns` schema requires `offer_id` (null only for a pick found on the web, or a place, restaurant or activity) and `total_paise` on every pick (Task 14), and the instructions say so (Task 12). On completion `resolvePicks` (Tasks 14, 16) turns each stay or journey pick's offer id into the offer Go's search showed this trip (its checked prices; for a stay `totalMinor` is the whole stay, so `Travel.offer(option_id)` answers that `offerId` with `priceMinor` = the whole stay, and the vote option carries it), and marks every other stay or journey pick "Found on the web" with no offer (`offerId: null`: P5 refuses to book it with words that offer a call). An offer id the job returns that Go never showed in this trip is treated as found on the web, never trusted. The card's `checked_at` is the oldest check behind its offers. `tasks/testing/job-search.ts` (`armedToken`, `searchAsTheJob`) plays the job's search for Task 20 and for P7's demo kit.
15. **Settled in master §6.3: `TapRouter.dispatch(tap)`.** The lane kit's `FakeTaps` implements it so it still satisfies `TapRouter`; lane C never dispatches a tap by id, so it throws if called.
16. **Flights and stays first; trains and buses are the stretch Task 8b (master §9).** `Travel.search` keeps master §6.3's four kinds; until Task 8b lands, `IxigoMap.search` has only `flights` and `stays` (`trains` and `buses` are optional fields), a search for the other two answers `TravelError('refused', 'Go cannot search trains yet.')`, and the composer, Go's MCP server and the fake ixigo offer flights and stays only. `GO_READ_ONLY_TOOLS` keeps all four search names (a name of a tool that is not there yet is never called), and the deployment's instructions say "search_trains and search_buses when mcp_search lists them", so landing Task 8b needs no new deployment version. P2's prefetch (Task 17, cut) and P6's train-ticket watches (`pnr_status`, a stretch) do not depend on it.
17. **Booking flights for the whole group is cut this week (master §9).** A flights card shows the per-person fare and can be put to a vote; the demo books the stay. P4 promises no group flight booking; whether a flights vote may be paid for is P5's rule.

## Assumptions (what the research left open)

- ixigo's tools, arguments and answers: unknown. `IXIGO_MAP` is the fake's until Task 23's checklist runs.
- `/v1` has no route to message a running task. Go answers an open question or hand-off exactly (`POST /v1/interactions/{id}/answer`); otherwise it sends a new message with `hand_off: 'auto'` that names the job, relying on Vestra's composer to follow the job up. Check on staging before the demo.
- The data of an `interaction.created` for an approval carries `server` and `name` (or the interaction's `details.server` and `details.tool`); an approval should never appear, since Go's tools are read-only or listed to run without asking.
- `task.progress` carries `todos: [{ content, status }]` with Bash's statuses `pending`, `in_progress`, `completed`.
- A stream opened with no `after` and no `Last-Event-ID` may start from now; Go always sends `after=0` or `Last-Event-ID`.
- `POST /v1/tasks/{id}/stop` ignores an `Idempotency-Key` header; `POST /v1/sessions` answers `url` and `expires_at`.
- `GO_PUBLIC_URL` is a public https host that Vestra can reach (for `/mcp` and `/v1-webhooks`); local development needs a tunnel.
- The fake villas' pictures are `https://picsum.photos/seed/...` placeholders, re-hosted by Go on first view.

**Review Focus → tests:** (1) a job outlives its moment: `events.test.ts` "never posts results for a job that was stopped", `sweep.test.ts` "ends a job whose hand-off nobody answered", `e2e.test.ts`; (2) a lost webhook or a dropped stream: `webhooks.test.ts` duplicate, `follower.test.ts` drop and 410 and polling, `sweep.test.ts` first test, `e2e.test.ts`; (3) constraints in code: `filters.test.ts` first test, `tools.test.ts` "search_flights applies Arjun's 6 pm"; a job's prices only from Go's own check: `returns.test.ts` "turns an offer id Go's search showed..." and "marks a pick with no offer id, or one Go never showed, as found on the web", `results.test.ts` "finds an offer Go's search showed this trip ... never another trip's or a job's", `events.test.ts` "builds the options card from the job's picks"; (4) one trip per token: `tokens.test.ts`, `server.test.ts` (401s, "reads only the trip its token names", no budget or money); (5) double taps and retries: `register.test.ts` (travel) two vote taps, `tools.test.ts` reruns, `tasks.test.ts` "starts one job for one key", `webhooks.test.ts`, `mcp-travel.test.ts` "confirms a hold once" and "looks up the booking for a hold before confirming it again".

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD", "TODO", "similar to Task", "handle edge cases" or bare "..." in code (searched again after the offer-id and `dispatch` changes). **Names:** every shared name is master §6.3's; P1's seam files (`travel/travel.ts`, `tasks/tasks.ts`) and env objects (`TravelEnv`, `TasksEnv`) keep their names and only gain fields.
